## Iteration 12 — 2026-10-31 — choice: a settings row that steps through a few named options

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on Ladvien/bevy_gooey: no open issues (no bug, no `consumer`, no enhancement). Parked work, looked up after triage: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is iteration 6's draft (stale, ignored, as logged in iterations 9 to 11); no parked branch newer than iteration 11's `loop/iter-11` (adopted last pass). Home-still, recorded live: `hs status` healthy at 04:59 (18670 documents, 18073 embedded, distill idle on CUDA at 192.168.1.110, scribes on 192.168.1.110 and bmb converting, Qdrant 1.20.0 up); `catalog_recent` (limit 200) held only embeds of couples-therapy and chronic-pain papers, forest ecology, SIGGRAPH 2024 and *Advances in Real-Time Rendering* course notes and two MCP papers, so `corpus.md` is unchanged; two more `hs distill search` queries on the choice ("option cycling widget gamepad settings menu", "stepper left right arrows select one of few values interface") found no game-UI document. Scribe was not exercised.

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates: (1) **`choice`**, researched last pass and the log's top backlog item (section 4's rank 3, a missing widget every game needs); (2) dialog elevation and tooltip polish (rank 4, looks flat); (3) scroll list and tabs; (4) slider and choice feedback and narration; (5) `HudText`, `Shortcut`, `HudDetail`. The pick beats the runner-up because a game cannot build a graphics or difficulty row without it (it hand-rolls three buttons, or a slider with no names), while elevation only makes a working dialog prettier; a settings-screen kit needs it too.

**What changed** (research `7371874`; implementation `0f6617d`, `25974c3`; docs `d374b26`, `fd8ff29`):
- `choice(label, options, selected)`: a row with the label on the left and the selected option on the right between two chevrons. The root is Bevy's headless `Button` carrying `ChoiceValue(usize)` (the selected index: read it, or write one to move the choice from code) and `ChoiceCount`, so it takes `observe`, `Tooltip` and `InteractionDisabled`. Left and Right step to the previous and next option and **stop at the ends** (keys through the choice's own observer; the D-pad through `gamepad_adjust`, a held direction repeating); the chevron that cannot step dims, and a press there changes and says nothing. Home and End jump; **Enter, Space, South and a click on the row cycle to the next option and wrap** (Bevy's `Activate` becomes "next"); a click on a chevron steps once on release. Every change is one `ValueChange<usize>` and one `UiFeedback::Activate`; focus writes `Focus`. Up and Down still move focus on, and `navigate_by_input` leaves Left and Right to a focused choice as to a slider (`FocusScopes::keeps_sideways`). Every option is laid out in one grid cell, so the row, the chevrons and the value never move while stepping.
- The chevron is a square with two borders of the skin's line weight, turned an eighth of a turn by a constant `UiTransform` and moved by `1 / (2 √2)` of its width to put its ink in the middle of its arrow: no font glyph, one line weight with every other border, growing with `text_scale`.
- `update_choices` (public, `GooeySystems::Style`, before `apply_skin`), `model::{choice_step, choice_next}` (engine-free, with tests and doctests), six `Role` variants (`Choice`, `ChoiceArrow`, `ChoiceBack`, `ChoiceForward`, `ChoiceCell`, `ChoiceOption`) styled in `apply_skin`; `ChoiceValue` and `ChoiceCount` registered for reflection.
- Gallery: a Quality choice (Low, Medium, High, Ultra; it logs and toasts the option) and `GOOEY_KEYS` tokens `space`, `home`, `end`, `back`, `forward`. `docs/media/gallery.png` regenerated (it shows the choice; caption updated).
- Assumed decisions (owner may override), in the note's Deviations: chevron 0.6 text sizes (the note said 0.5: a 7 px stroke pair read light beside 14 px text) and half a `gap` of air round the value (the note said a gap: the chevrons stood 30 px from the word); a `Button` root, so a game that observes `Activate` hears cycles and not steps (docs say to observe `ValueChange<usize>`); public `ChoiceCount` (so `update_choices` can be public); a held arrow key repeats like a slider's.

**Sources.** Craft: Zhou, *Design Guidelines for Input Steppers* (NN/g, 2018: "Use large buttons for both desktop and mobile. Target areas should be big enough to support the input modality."; "if the input value has a maximum or minimum limit, be sure to indicate it", Expedia's grayed-out segment), Kaley, *Listboxes vs. Dropdown Lists* (NN/g, 2020) and Nielsen, *Checkboxes vs. Radio Buttons* (NN/g, 2004: the "limited space might sometimes force you to violate this guideline" it is deviated under). Shipping game: Microsoft, *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 107* (Minecraft: "only single key presses are required to adjust settings—even slider inputs"; Gears 5's "Button Tap Challenges" row as a label and one value; activation on the up event). Technical: W3C WAI-ARIA APG, *Spinbutton Pattern* (one tab stop, Home and End), and Bevy 0.20.0 `bevy_ui_widgets` `button.rs`, `checkbox.rs`, `slider.rs` and `bevy_input_focus` `pointer_focus.rs`. All the quotations in `docs/research/choice.md` were fetched again this pass and match (NN/g steppers, listbox and radio articles, XAG 107, the APG page).

**Checklist, before → after** (`/tmp/g12/findings.md`, `critique.md`, removed; 1280x720 dark unless noted; pixels from the stills):
- Before: no stepper; a game used three buttons or a slider. After: the gallery panel is 327 px wide instead of 259 ("Medium" sets the cell) and 526 px high instead of 464 (the row and one gap).
- Text 14.22:1 dark (237,240,245 on 30,32,40), 10.20:1 parchment, unchanged. Chevron live 14.22:1 / 10.20:1; spent 6.64:1 / 4.84:1 (plainly dimmer, still above 3:1); amber under the pointer.
- Focus ring on the row: 3 px on all four sides (rows 463-465 and 513-515, columns 493-495 and 784-786), 53 px high; the row is a toggle's height (tests allow 1 px: Bevy rounds 52.8 to 52 or 53 by position). At 2560x1440 the row is 2 x 52.8 within 1.5 px.
- Hit targets: arrows 44 x 46.8 px (test: at least 44 at 720p, 88 at 1440p).
- Spacing: labels of Subtitles, Quality and Volume start at x 517; the forward chevron's ink ends at x 762 and the switch's track at x 762 (0 px); chevron ink 10 x 16 px, centred in its arrow within 1 px (test); stroke 3 px = the row's frame at 1x, and equal to it, whole, at 1.5x, 1.11x and 2x (test).
- Nothing moves while stepping: row, both arrows and the cell, 0 px, with a 17-character option beside two short ones (test). `GooeySkin::problems()` empty for both presets (no new skin field). Dialog scrim dims the choice like every widget; it is outside the dialog's focus scope. Meter fill unchanged.
- Real gallery, `GOOEY_KEYS`: `down x4` focuses Quality (blue ring); `right x3` logs `quality 2`, `quality 3` and nothing for the third (two events, two `Activate` feedbacks); `forward` (a click on the forward arrow) logs `quality 2`, hides the ring, shows the amber chevron and the grey row outline; `home` dims the back chevron; `enter, enter` logs `quality 2`, `quality 3`.

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): `choice` **GREAT**, its navigation **GREAT**. Flaws left, none against the validation targets: at rest the row has no edge, so it is the lightest control in the column (the chevrons are the only affordance until the pointer is over it; the toggle and the slider have no edge at rest either); in parchment the spent chevron is 4.84:1 against a live 10.20:1, quieter than on dark; an arrow has no pressed look (the step on release is the feedback; XAG 107's cancel-by-sliding-off is kept); the chevron's outer corner is a one-pixel stair at 3x zoom (Bevy's shader, like the meter's fill); a long option widens the row and the panel (no ellipsis, by design); the accessibility node is Bevy's `Button`, not a spin button with a value text (backlog, with the slider's and the toasts').

**Tests.** `tests/choice.rs` (19, headless window, real keyboard, pointer and gamepad input): state kept and one change and one `Activate` feedback each, an end changes and says nothing, Home and End, a value the game sets (no event back) and one past the end, no options, Enter, Space and South cycling and wrapping, the D-pad and its repeat stopping at the end (clock set by `TimeUpdateStrategy::ManualDuration`), a click on a chevron (once; a spent one does nothing and is no click on the row; the row is not left pressed; focus on the row, ring hidden), a click on the label or the value cycling, chevron colours live, under the pointer and spent, disabled, Left and Right never leaving the row beside a button, Up and Down moving on, focus feedback and the ring, row height and edges against a toggle, 2x scale, 44 px arrows, nothing moving while stepping, one line weight and the ink centred in the arrow, the weight whole at 1.5x, 1.11x and 2x. `src/model.rs`: two unit tests and two doctests. Whole suite: 19 unit, 19 choice, 17 controls, 7 dialog, 2 leaf, 3 lines, 3 meter, 1 nav, 5 pointer, 4 scale, 10 skin, 16 toast, 10 tooltip, 37 doctests, and with `--features serde` 4 serde tests more. Mutations checked: `if true` for the "no change" guard fails the five end-of-list tests; `Or<(With<Slider>, With<Slider>)>` in `TakesSideways` fails the "never leave the row" test. Not caught by any test, deliberately: `click.propagate(false)` in `arrow_on_click` is belt and braces (the root `Button` stops a click unconditionally, and `arrow_on_press` is what keeps it unpressed).

**mean_ms** (sib, Xvfb, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 12 alternating pairs of the gallery from `main` and this tree, which has one more widget; host load 12 to 38 from other loops): minimum 23.85 against 23.84 ms (−0.01), median 25.40 against 25.31 (−0.09), paired median difference +0.11 ms. No regression: a choice that did not change costs nothing (`update_choices` walks nothing; `apply_skin` restyles it only on a change). Single-run stills before the change, 120 frames: 30.1 / 32.7 / 30.0 ms (dark / parchment / 1.5x text) at 1280x720 and 130 / 125 / 106 ms at 2560x1440; after, 26.3 / 27.2 / 27.6 and 118 / 153 / 156 ms, the latter taken while a gate compiled beside them.

**Gate.** All six steps green (`CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1`): `cargo build`, `--release`, `cargo test` (all binaries), `--features serde`, the README test, `cargo build --examples`, clippy `-D warnings` (the first run flagged `too_many_arguments` on `gamepad_adjust`, 8 of 7: its two control queries became one, `Query<Has<Slider>, TakesSideways>`), `cargo doc` with `-D warnings`, `cargo fmt --check`, the wasm32 library build. One `cargo test --features serde` link was killed by the OOM killer (`ld terminated with signal 9`) while another loop's CI container linked beside it; only the steps from there on were run again. Trap: a `setsid nohup ./script > log & disown` start left an empty log twice in this pass while the same script ran fine in the foreground; run long scripts through the tool's own `async` instead.

**Process notes.** Two things went against the rules and are recorded so they are not repeated: the two model unit tests were appended with a short `python3` script instead of the `edit` tool (the result was checked and compiled, and every later edit used `edit`), and the first `edit` of `skin.rs` anchored on a line I had not been shown, which the tool refused; the retry showed the real line.

## Iteration 13 — 2026-10-31 — list: rows that scroll, and a focus that never leaves the screen

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on Ladvien/bevy_gooey: no open issues (the three ever filed, #2 to #4, are closed): no bug, no `consumer`, no enhancement. Parked work, looked up after triage: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is iteration 6's draft (stale, ignored, as logged in iterations 9 to 12); no parked branch newer than `loop/iter-12`. Home-still, recorded live: `system_status` healthy at 06:13 (18670 documents, 18073 embedded; distill idle on CUDA at 192.168.1.110; scribes on 192.168.1.110 (3 converting) and bmb (5 of 6 slots busy); Qdrant 1.20.0 up); `catalog_recent` (limit 200) held only embeds of couples-therapy, chronic-pain and forest-ecology papers, SIGGRAPH 2024 and *Advances in Real-Time Rendering* course notes and two MCP papers, so `corpus.md` is unchanged; three `hs distill search` queries (scrolling menus with a pad, scrollbar affordance, paging through long lists) found no game-UI document. Scribe was not exercised.

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates: (1) **a scrolling list**, section 4's rank 3 (a missing widget), hand-rolled by the owner's own projects (bevy_emerge's `chrome.rs` `scroll_list` and four lists that each follow their selection by hand in `chooser.rs`, `anim_tab.rs` and `compose.rs`, and Sanctuary's `board/mod.rs`), and the thing that stops a settings screen of ten rows fitting 720p; (2) dialog elevation and tooltip polish (rank 4, looks flat); (3) a settings-screen kit (needs a scrolling page first); (4) tabs; (5) slider, choice and list feedback and narration. The list beats the runner-up because a game cannot show more than about eight rows without it and elevation only makes a working dialog prettier; it also unblocks (3).

**What changed** (research `269f74d`; implementation `a433c5a`, `bbf12b0`; docs `963d799`, `69e4d91`):
- `list(rows, children![..])`: a wrapper (`Role::List`) holding the viewport (`ListView { rows }`, Bevy's `ScrollArea`, so the wheel and a trackpad work; `Display::Block` around one `Role::ListRows` column that does not shrink) and a rail with a thumb (Bevy's `Scrollbar`, so a thumb drag and a rail click work; the click pages a viewport). The viewport stands `rows × (row + gap)` tall, `row` a button's height from the skin's sizes, so `4.5` cuts the fifth row at every scale and text size; the rail (`2 × border` wide, thumb `text_muted` on a `surface_raised` track, `text` hovered, `accent` dragged) is `Display::None` while the rows fit (`update_lists`), its lane always reserved. Five `Role` variants styled in `apply_skin`.
- **The list follows focus** (`scroll_to_focus`, `GooeySystems::Navigate`): when a key, the D-pad or Tab moves focus onto a row, the least scroll that leaves 1.5 rows beyond it (`model::scroll_to_reveal`), moved on to a row's top (`model::snap_to_row`), so the next row is whole and the one after is cut at every step and the list stands as at rest; flush with the last row at the end; a row taller than the list scrolls to its top, a jump of more than a viewport centres. Never for a focus a pointer press set (a press that scrolled content under the pointer loses its click). `page_lists`: PageUp, PageDown and the pad's triggers move a viewport (`model::page_target`).
- **Navigation in and around a list** (`navigate_by_input`): a row the list has clipped away is not a neighbour from outside it, and Up and Down stay among a list's rows until they run out. Both found by tests: without the first, Left from a button beside the list landed on a clipped row 6; without the second, Down from the last visible row skipped the clipped rows and landed on the button under the list.
- A `Tooltip` on a widget inside a clipped container was clipped by it (its box stands beside the widget): the box carries `OverrideClip`. A fix with its own test (`a_tooltip_on_a_row_is_not_clipped_by_its_list`, which failed before).
- Gallery: a Load game list of twelve save slots beside the panel; `GOOEY_KEYS` tokens `slots`, `pagedown`, `pageup`, `wheel`. `docs/media/gallery.png` regenerated (the list scrolled, Orchard focused, two toasts; the Options tooltip would stand over the new panel, so the hero no longer shows it, and its caption describes the file as it is).
- Assumed decisions (owner may override), in the note's Deviations: the `list(rows, children![..])` shape; `rows` counted in row pitches, each row with its gap; the lane always reserved; a follow margin of 1.5 rows and a snap to row tops (the least-scroll rule left a 17 px sliver of the cut row and a blank band at the top after the first step); the triggers for paging, no Home and End; the rail click pages (Bevy's own).

**Sources.** Craft: Fessenden, *Scrolling and Attention* (NN/g, 2018: "The illusion of completeness can interfere with scrolling. Include signifiers (such as cut-off text) to tell people that there is content below the fold."). Shipping: Microsoft, *Gamepad and remote control interactions* (Xbox: "One focus visual should always be visible on the screen so that the user can pick up where they left off without searching for the focus."; Page up/down on the triggers; "you must scroll through each item to navigate outside of the ScrollViewer"), *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 107* (single presses), and the owner's editor, `chrome.rs` (the measured defects its comments record: a reveal that glues the selection to the edge, a bar that disagrees with the wheel by its gutter, an out-of-range scroll the component keeps). Technical: W3C, *Understanding SC 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum)* ("When a user interface component receives keyboard focus, the component is not entirely hidden due to author-created content"), the Bevy 0.20.0 source (`bevy_ui_widgets` `scrollarea.rs`, `scrollbar.rs`, Feathers' `listview.rs`, `bevy_ui` layout and clipping). Pending index: none of the three is in home-still.

**Checklist, before → after** (`/tmp/g13/findings.md`, `critique.md`, removed; 1280x720 dark unless noted; pixels from the stills):
- Before: no scrolling widget; the gallery's one panel is 526 px high. After: a second panel, 300 px wide, with the list standing 255.6 px (4.5 × 56.8) high.
- Text on a row 10.26:1 dark, 8.19:1 parchment (a row is a button). Thumb on its track 4.78:1 / 3.89:1 (the knob-on-track pair `problems()` already holds), thumb on the panel 6.64:1 / 4.84:1; the track itself is 1.39:1 on the panel (it is not the component; the thumb is).
- Focus ring on a row: 3 px on all four sides (rows 364-366 and 408-410, columns 682-684 and 931-933), whole inside the viewport at every step of a 12-row walk down and back up, by arrows, Tab and D-pad (tests). Rows 46.8 px high (at least `min_target` 44); pitch 56.8; rail 6 px (12 device px at 2x), 16 px lane, 10 px of air between a row and the rail; flush rows at the top (fill 253 = viewport top 250 + the 3 px ring slot).
- The cut: at every step away from the end the fifth row shows 25 of 41 fill px (28 of 47 px of node, 60 %); the first version showed 17 px (36 %) after the first step and a 13 px blank band above the first row, which is what the snap fixed. Thumb 97 of 256 px for 255.6 of 671.6 px of content (expected 97).
- 2560x1440: everything twice, 4.5 rows still; text scale 1.5: rows 52.8 px, 4.5 rows of them. `GooeySkin::problems()` empty for both presets (no new skin field).

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): `list` **GREAT**, its navigation (follow, paging, neighbours) **GREAT**. The first look was NOT YET on one flaw, the sliver (above); fixed and re-judged at four zooms and both skins. Flaws left, none against the validation targets: the track is a hint (1.4:1) and the thumb a 6 px mouse target; no fade or shadow at the clipped edge (Bevy UI has no gradient without an image); a list that fits keeps its 16 px lane, a wider right margin than a menu; the wheel is Bevy's 100 px a notch and nothing animates (a nine-row jump centres at once); not announced to screen readers (Bevy's `Scrollbar` carries no accessibility node); the thumb has no pointer cursor; a wheel-scrolled list can leave the focused row out of view, so Enter acts on an unseen row (as in any list).

**Tests.** `tests/list.rs`, 22 tests, headless window, real keyboard, pointer and gamepad input: rows tall and the fifth cut at 1280x720, 2560x1440 and 1.5x text; the lane with and without the rail; the rail only while the rows do not fit and gone again when they are removed; the thumb's size and place; its colours at rest, hovered and dragged and a drag by 40 px; the wheel and both ends; a walk down (each focus whole, the next row whole, a row flush at the top and the cut row more than a third showing at steps 3 to 8, a pitch a step, the list ending on the last row, the ring 3 px) and back up; Tab; the D-pad; a click on a half-visible row focusing it with no scroll and the next Down bringing the row below in; a far row centred; an unseen focus not followed; a clipped row not a neighbour from outside (this one failed with the filter off); PageDown and PageUp to both ends, by keys and by triggers; paging outside a list doing nothing; thirty idle frames writing no scroll position and no rail node; the rail on whole device pixels at four scales; `ListView::rows` changed live; a tooltip on a row not clipped; a rail click paging a viewport and back. `src/model.rs`: six new unit tests and four doctests (`list_height`, `scroll_to_reveal`, `snap_to_row`, `page_target`), and doctests on `list` and `ListView`.

**mean_ms** (sib, Xvfb, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 12 alternating pairs of the gallery from `main` and this tree, host load 12 to 23 from other loops): minimum 24.31 against 23.77 ms, medians 24.65 against 24.50, paired mean difference −0.14 ms (the new gallery has one more panel and twelve more buttons). No regression: a list at rest costs a comparison (`update_lists`) and a changed-focus check (`scroll_to_focus`), and a test holds that thirty idle frames write nothing. Single stills, 120 frames: before 26.7 / 25.9 / 22.9 ms (dark / parchment / 1.5x text) at 1280x720 and 142 / 127 / 110 ms at 2560x1440; after 23.1 / 23.0 / 22.8 and 92.5 / 92.4 / 92.1 (and 27.4 / 25.2 / 24.1 and 115 / 103 / 112 for the final binary under a heavier load): host noise exceeds the difference.

**Gate.** All six steps green after the clippy fix (the first run flagged `type_complexity` and `too_many_arguments` (8 of 7) on `page_lists`: its two node queries became one with a type alias): `cargo build`, `--release`, `cargo test` (all binaries), `--features serde`, the README test, `cargo build --examples`, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo doc` with `-D warnings` (a doc link to the private `CONTEXT_ROWS` was removed first), `cargo fmt --check`, the wasm32 library build.

The next release runs `cargo semver-checks check-release` first and will say major (eleven new variants on the exhaustive `Role` enum), so pre-1.0 it is 0.4.0.

## Iteration 14 — 2026-10-31 — tabs: pages of a settings screen, turned by the bumpers (adopting the killed pass's build)

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey` (the repository moved from `Ladvien/` this morning; both paths answer the same): no open issues, so no bug, no `consumer`, no enhancement. Parked work, looked up after triage: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is iteration 6's draft (stale, ignored, as logged in iterations 9 to 13); **`origin/loop/iter-14` is the killed iteration-14 pass's tabs build** (`b7b8ff5` the widget and 26 tests, `4c6f027` its docs, and a driver commit `f7f9f9b`, "WIP(pass 12, parked)", of two files left unfinished: a clippy type alias in `src/nav.rs` and an edge test in `tests/tabs.rs`). It is the item this pass would pick, so it was adopted onto `loop/iter-14-tabs` (cherry-picked, never merged, `loop/iter-14` untouched): the one conflict was `README.md`, which the org move (below) had rewritten, so main's README was kept and the tabs text became `docs/guide/tabs.md`. Its earlier gate had died at a `tests/tabs.rs` compile error; the parked edit is the fix. Home-still, recorded live: `hs status` healthy at 14:40 (18671 documents, 18073 embedded, distill idle on CUDA at 192.168.1.110, scribes on 192.168.1.110 (4 converting) and bmb (5), Qdrant 1.20.0 up); `catalog_recent` (limit 200) held only embeds of couples-therapy, chronic-pain and forest-ecology papers, SIGGRAPH 2024 and course notes, two MCP papers and a download of the IReST reading-speed texts (13:56), so `corpus.md` is unchanged and no corpus commit was made.

**Main moved under the pass.** `origin/main` had four commits that the clone did not (the repository's move to `bevy-coven`, a gallery frame sequence for a GIF with `GOOEY_SHOT_DIR`, `GOOEY_SHOT_EVERY`, `GOOEY_KEY_EVERY`, and a README cut to the org's `DOCUMENTATION.md` with the detail in `docs/guide/` and a media list in `docs/media/README.md`). Merged `origin/main` into local `main` (`32b83a7`, a merge, no rewrite: the clone's research commit `7b080d3` is also on `origin/loop/iter-14`), then built the branch on it. From now on a visible feature is done when its README bullet, its `docs/guide/` page and its GIF exist; this pass did all three.

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates: (1) **tabs**, section 4's rank 3 (a missing widget every settings screen needs; the owner's editor draws "the tab strip" by hand), researched (`docs/research/tabs.md`, `7b080d3`) and built; (2) dialog elevation and tooltip polish (rank 4, looks flat); (3) a settings-screen kit (text size, interface size, skin as rows; needs the pages to be short enough); (4) narration and feedback for the controls; (5) `HudText`, `Shortcut`, `HudDetail`, the meter's damage trail. Tabs beats the runner-up because a settings screen of more than a handful of rows still does not fit 720p even with a `list` (a list scrolls one column; a game's Audio, Display and Controls are pages), and the work was finished and needed only a gate and a visual judgement, where elevation only makes a working dialog prettier.

**What changed** (research `7b080d3`; implementation `0841b45` and `538bf84`; docs `5e1f6c9` and `9833799`):
- `tabs(labels, selected, children![tab_page(..), ..])` and `tab_page(children![..])`: a strip of one- or two-word labels over pages of widgets, one shown at a time. The strip is Bevy's headless `TabList` and `Tab` (click, Left and Right with wrap, Home and End, Enter and Space, a roving Tab stop, `TabActivation::Automatic`: focus selects) in equal grid columns; the selected tab is `accent` with `accent_text`, the others a button's colours; every page stands in one grid cell, so the widget is as large as the largest page and **turning a page moves nothing** (0 px); the pages not shown are `Visibility::Hidden`, which keeps their room and takes their widgets out of the pointer, Tab and the arrows.
- State on the root: `TabsValue(usize)` (read it, or set it to turn the page from code, no event back) and `TabsCount`; each change a player makes is one `ValueChange<usize>` (Bevy's `Option<Entity>` answer is turned into the place by `tabs_on_select`) and one `UiFeedback::Activate`; focus onto a tab is `Focus`.
- `step_tabs` (`GooeySystems::Navigate`): **LB and RB** and **Ctrl+PageUp and Ctrl+PageDown** turn the page from anywhere in the widget (or from beside it when it is the only tabs widget in the focus's scope; an open dialog is its own scope), wrapping; the D-pad's Left and Right step a focused tab. `land_tab_focus`: focus never rests on a page that is not shown (it lands on the new page's first widget, or on the tab). Up from a page lands on the **selected** tab, not the nearest. `page_lists` ignores PageUp and PageDown while Ctrl is held. `update_tabs` (`GooeySystems::Style`), `model::tab_step`, six `Role` variants.
- Gallery: the settings are three tabs (Audio: Volume and Subtitles; Display: Quality and Window; Controls: two buttons) and `GOOEY_KEYS` tokens `nexttab`, `prevtab`, `tab1`, `tab2`, `tab3`. `docs/media/gallery.png` and `gallery.gif` regenerated from the tabbed gallery (captions now say what the files show; the old hero caption said the ring reached Harbour, the file's last step before Page Down is Mill, 03:02), `docs/media/tabs.gif` new (4.7 s, 520x540, 83 KB), `docs/media/README.md` lists all three with their commands.
- Assumed decisions (owner may override), in the note's Deviations: the `tabs(labels, selected, children![tab_page(..)])` shape; automatic activation; fill as the selection indicator, not an underline; pages of one size; bumpers from anywhere in the widget's scope; Ctrl+PageUp and Ctrl+PageDown; wrapping; no vertical, closable or scrolling strip.

**Sources.** Craft: Sunwall, *Tabs, Used Right* (NN/g: "Use at least two selection indicators to enhance the visual salience of the selected tab"; "Tab labels should usually be 1-2 words"). Shipping: Microsoft, *Xbox Accessibility Guidelines 112, 114 and 106* ("“RB” and “LB” for moving through tabs", Forza Horizon 4's LB and RB between menu tabs, Minecraft's four filtered tabs). Technical: W3C WAI-ARIA Authoring Practices, *Tabs Pattern* ("It is recommended that tabs activate automatically when they receive focus as long as their associated tab panels are displayed without noticeable latency") and Bevy 0.20.0's `bevy_ui_widgets/src/tabs.rs`; the owner's editor `chrome.rs` for what is hand-rolled. home-still held none of them (four queries, 2026-10-31); all four are under the note's `## Pending index`.

**Checklist, before → after** (`/tmp/g14/findings.md`, `critique.md`; 1280x720 dark unless noted; pixels from the stills):
- Before: no tabs; the gallery panel held Subtitles, Quality and Volume in a column (panel 96 to 623, 528 px). After: the same controls on three pages under one strip, panel 99 to 620 (522 px) on **every** page (Audio, Display, Controls all 99 to 620).
- Text on the lit tab 9.39:1 dark, 7.37:1 parchment; on an unlit tab 10.26:1 and 8.19:1; lit fill against unlit fill 6.03:1 and 4.69:1 (the target is 3:1); an unlit tab on the panel 1.39:1 dark, a button's own.
- Focus ring on a tab: 3 px on all four sides at 1280x720 (x 251-253 and 392-394, y 403-405 and 447-449), 6 px at 2560x1440. A tab's ring box is 47 px high at 1x and 93 px at 2x (nodes of 46.8 px, at least `min_target`).
- Spacing: tab fills 254-392, 408-546 and 562-700, 16 px apart (`gap` 10 plus two 3 px borders); the strip's edges are the menu buttons' edges, 254 and 700 (0 px); the page starts 13 px under the strip. At 1.5x text the strip stays inside the panel (131 to 721 in 111 to 741).
- A bumper from the Volume slider: page 1 shows, focus is on Quality (its ring 3 px, `bumper-from-page-1280x720.png`); RB with focus on the tab keeps focus on a tab; `prevtab` from the first page wraps to Controls and logs `tab 2`. `GooeySkin::problems()`: no new skin field, both presets empty (`tests/skin.rs`). Dialog scrim, meter fill: unchanged.

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): `tabs` **GREAT**, its navigation (bumpers, landing, hidden-page focus) **GREAT**. Flaws left, none against the validation targets: the strip floats with no frame joining it to its page, so it reads as three buttons with one lit (the note's choice: two cues, a button's colours, 13 px of proximity); a short page leaves a blank band under it (the lowest content on Display is 36 px and on Controls 33 px above Audio's; declared: pages take the largest room); in parchment the focus ring on the lit red tab is a darker blue on red and thinner in contrast than dark's blue round amber (still 3 px and visible at 3x); no bumper glyph prompts (the crate has no glyph vocabulary; the guide tells the game to show them, XAG 106); the hero still no longer shows the choice at rest (the Display page is one key away, `tabs.gif` shows it); not announced as "Tab 1 of 3, selected".

## Iteration 15 — 2026-10-31 — cancel: Escape and the pad's B back out of a dialog or a screen

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey`: no open issues (empty list, no `403`/`429`): no bug, no `consumer`, no enhancement. Parked work, looked up after triage: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is iteration 6's draft (stale, ignored, as logged in iterations 9 to 14); `origin/loop/iter-14` is the killed iteration-14 pass's branch, already adopted and merged as `loop/iter-14-tabs` in iteration 14; no parked branch newer than that. `origin/main` had not moved. Home-still, recorded live (2026-10-31 15:59 local): `system_status` healthy (18673 documents, 18073 embedded; distill idle on CUDA at 192.168.1.110; scribes on 192.168.1.110 (1 converting, 11 of 12 slots free) and bmb (5 converting, none free); Qdrant 1.20.0); `catalog_recent` (limit 200) held only embeds of psychology and biomedical papers, SIGGRAPH 2024 and 2026 abstracts and a few software-update papers, so `corpus.md` got no new game-UI source (one reading-speed paper, *IReST*, `10.1167_iovs.11-8284`, is typography but not for games or screens; not added). One `distill_search` ("back button cancel gamepad menu navigation undo exit user control and freedom") returned a controller-usability chapter, a Rust game book, a game-mechanics book and unrelated papers, so the three kinds of source are web copies, Bevy's source and the owner's editor (`## Pending index` of the note).

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates: (1) **Cancel / Back**, section 4's rank 1 (a navigation gap: no way out of a dialog or a screen with Escape or B; a pad player must Down-and-Enter "Stay"), deferred since iteration 1 ("the backlog keeps a cancel hook") and hand-rolled by the owner's editor three times (`bevy_emerge_mapper/src/confirm.rs:250` `KeyCode::KeyN || KeyCode::Escape`, `chooser.rs:6399`, `snip.rs:131`; none reads a pad, none asks which layer has focus); (2) dialog elevation and tooltip polish (rank 4, looks flat; first in the backlog for three iterations); (3) a settings-screen kit (text size, interface size, skin as rows); (4) a key-rebinding row and an inventory grid (rank 3, heavy); (5) narration and feedback for the controls. Cancel beats dialog elevation because a dialog that a pad cannot back out of is unusable there, where elevation only makes a working dialog prettier; it is also the smallest self-contained slice (one event, one system, one traversal). The dialog's look is still the weakest thing the new GIF shows (critique 1) and is the top of the backlog.

**What changed** (research `566e2d3`; docs `53308de`; implementation `f90512d`, test `a tab is not changed by Back either`):
- `Cancel { entity }`, a public `EntityEvent` with `propagate = CancelScope, auto_propagate`: Escape, or any gamepad's `East` (B on Xbox, Circle on PlayStation), `just_pressed`, so one event a press however long it is held. It starts at the focused widget and bubbles up its parents (`observe(|cancel: On<Cancel>, ..| ..)` on the screen or dialog being left; `cancel.entity` is the entity the observer is on, `On::original_event_target()` the widget that had focus; `cancel.propagate(false)` keeps a layer above from also acting). **It never leaves a `dialog()`** (`CancelScope`, a `Traversal` that returns no parent on an entity with `Dialog`): a Back inside a modal dialog does not reach the screen the dialog was spawned under. With nothing focused in the active scope, or focus left behind the dialog, **the open dialog on top is the target** (`FocusScopes::cancel_target`), so a dialog can always be dismissed; with no dialog and no focused widget (or only the window Bevy focuses at start-up) **nothing is sent**, so Escape stays the game's pause key. A press with a target shows the focus ring (`show_focus`), as every key does. The crate closes nothing: the gallery's Quit dialog has one observer that despawns it, and focus goes back to Quit through the existing `return_focus`.
- `cancel_by_input` in `GooeySystems::Navigate` after `gamepad_select` (returns at once without a press), registered with the `Cancel` type by `GooeyNavPlugin`; `FocusScopes::cancel_target`. No change to `GooeySkin`, `Role` or any existing system.
- Gallery: the Quit dialog closes on `Cancel` (logs `cancel: the Quit dialog is closed`), `GOOEY_KEYS` token `escape`. `docs/media/cancel.gif` new (4.1 s, 848x540, 150 KB), README bullet extended (not a new one: the list holds seven) and the GIF under the tabs one with a caption read off the file, `docs/guide/navigation.md` "Back", `docs/guide/host-api.md`, `docs/media/README.md` row with the command, `docs/research/dialog.md` (the iteration-1 deviation "Escape not adopted" marked superseded), AGENTS.md layout and the one-place rule for Escape and East, CHANGELOG `[Unreleased]`.
- Assumed decisions (owner may override), in the note's Deviations: the name `Cancel` and an event of its own (not Bevy's `RequestClose`, which belongs to Bevy's `ModalDialog`, not this crate's `dialog()`); bubbling from the focused widget with the dialog as the end of the road; the dialog as target with nothing focused; East positional; showing focus; closing nothing; no click on the scrim as Cancel; no prompt glyph.

**Sources.** Craft: Nielsen, *10 Usability Heuristics* (NN/g), heuristic 3 ("Users often perform actions by mistake. They need a clearly marked "emergency exit" to leave the unwanted action without having to go through an extended process."; "Make sure the exit is clearly labeled and discoverable."). Shipping: Microsoft, *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 112* (Sea of Thieves: "they're always provided the ability ... to press “B” to go back to the previous menu screen.") and *106* ("On Xbox, the B button is almost always equivalent to Escape"), and Microsoft's *Gamepad and remote control interactions* (the built-in mapping "Escape | B/Back button"; `BackRequested` with `if (!e.Handled)`: the innermost handler wins). Technical: W3C APG *Dialog (Modal) Pattern* ("Escape: Closes the dialog."; "Windows under a modal dialog are inert."), Godot's `input/ui_cancel` ("Default InputEventAction to discard a modal or pending input."), and Bevy 0.20.0's `bevy_ui_widgets` (`RequestClose`, the popup menu's Escape with `propagate(false)`, `text_input` leaving Escape to "the surrounding context") and `bevy_input_focus` (`FocusedInput` and `WindowTraversal`), whose pattern the event follows. home-still held none of them; all fetched 2026-10-31 and quoted in the note.

**Checklist, before → after** (`/tmp/g15/findings.md`, `critique.md`, removed; 1280x720 dark unless noted):
- **Eight stills are pixel-identical before and after** (dark, parchment and 1.5x text at 1280x720 and 2560x1440, the Quit dialog open at both sizes; `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `magick compare -metric AE` 0 on each): the change adds no pixel to any existing state.
- New state, Escape closes the dialog: the ring is on Quit, 3 px on all four sides (x 251-253 and 700-702, y 279-281 and 323-325), 6 px at 2560x1440; on Stay in the open dialog x 550-552 and 727-729, y 382-384 and 426-428. Ring contrast unchanged: dark 6.33:1 on the button fill and 8.78:1 on the panel; parchment 3.66:1 and 4.56:1.
- The dialog (unchanged): the scrim takes the menu panel from (30,32,40) to (16,17,23) and the window from (18,20,26) to (8,9,13); the heading is 14.26:1 on its solid panel; the dialog is 212 px wide round a 180 px heading, 16 px of air each side. Hit targets, spacing, text in its box: unchanged. `GooeySkin::problems()` empty for both presets (no new skin field).

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): `Cancel` **GREAT**: every validation target has a test that fails without the behaviour (the dialog stop was removed and two tests failed, the screen hearing a Back from inside the dialog), the real gallery closes the Quit dialog on Escape with the ring back on Quit in both skins and at both sizes, and nothing else moved. The Quit dialog as `cancel.gif` shows it: **NOT YET**, and not touched (a separate improvement, the top of the backlog). Flaws left: the dialog reads as a large tooltip (212 px wide, 16 px of air, the menu's fill), which is on screen for four seconds of the README; nothing on screen says Escape or B exits (NN/g: "clearly labeled and discoverable"; the crate has no glyph vocabulary, the guide tells the game to draw the prompt); the close is instant (no animation anywhere in the crate); a mouse player pressing Escape sees the ring appear on the button that opened the dialog; the pad's East is covered by raw gamepad events in tests and not seen on screen (the GIF is the keyboard path, the same system); [INFERENCE, not tested] an Escape in the very frame a dialog is spawned goes to the widget behind it, since `FocusScopes::active` needs the dialog's `InheritedVisibility`.

**Tests.** `tests/cancel.rs`, 10, headless window, real keyboard and gamepad input: Escape reaches the screen's observer from the focused button (its `entity` the screen, `original_event_target()` the button) and shows focus; a pad's East is the same event and West and North are not; one press is one event however long it is held (repeats and ten frames) and a second press a second event; a dialog spawned under a screen hears it and the screen does not; an inner `propagate(false)` keeps it from the dialog above; an open dialog hears it with nothing focused and with focus on a button behind it (its `original_event_target()` the dialog); no dialog with nothing focused, or only the window, sends nothing and shows nothing; a dialog closed by its observer gives focus back to Quit with the ring visible; a focused slider, choice, toggle and tab are unchanged (no `ValueChange`, no `Activate`); thirty idle frames send nothing. The first run of the focus-back test failed because the helper spawned its `AutoFocus` button before the dialog (so the dialog remembered Stay as its opener); the helper now spawns the dialog in one call, as a game does.

**mean_ms** (sib, Xvfb, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 12 alternating pairs of the gallery from `44ec30c` and this tree, host load 1 to 21 from other loops): minimum 21.38 against 21.75 ms (+0.37), median 22.17 against 22.11, **paired median difference 0.00 ms** (mean +0.23). Single stills, 120 frames, 1280x720 (base then new): dark 25.5 / 22.3, parchment 22.5 / 22.6, 1.5x text 25.9 / 22.7; 2560x1440: 100.8 / 92.6, 109.0 / 98.4, 99.5 / 94.1, Quit open 97.8 / 103.6 (load noise both ways). No regression: `cancel_by_input` is one `just_pressed` check and a gamepad iteration a frame and a test holds that thirty idle frames send nothing.

**Gate.** All six steps green on `f90512d` plus the docs commit (`CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=2`, 25 minutes at load 22): `cargo build`, `--release`, `cargo test` (every binary, the new `tests/cancel.rs` and the `Cancel` doctest among them), `--features serde`, the README test, `cargo build --examples`, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo doc` with `-D warnings`, `cargo fmt --check`, the wasm32 library build. The tab assertion was added to `tests/cancel.rs` after that run; the gate runs again on the merged tree.

## Iteration 16 — 2026-10-31 — dialog presence: a panel over the scrim reads as a layer

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey`: no open issues (empty list, no `403`/`429`): no bug, no `consumer`, no enhancement. Parked work, looked up after triage: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is iteration 6's draft (stale, ignored, as logged in iterations 9 to 15); no parked branch is newer. `origin/main` had not moved (`git fetch` at 17:16 CDT: `main` = `origin/main` = `6daf050`). Home-still, recorded live (2026-10-31 17:16 CDT): `system_status` healthy (18673 documents, 18073 embedded; distill idle on CUDA at 192.168.1.110; scribes on 192.168.1.110 (2 converting, 10 of 12 slots free) and bmb (4 converting, none free); Qdrant 1.20.0), so no restart was needed; `catalog_recent` (limit 200) held only embeds of psychology and biomedical papers, SIGGRAPH 2024 and 2026 papers and one reading-speed download (the IReST text set, an ophthalmology instrument, not a game-UI document), so `docs/research/corpus.md` gained nothing. Two `distill_search` queries on dialog layering and confirmation prompts returned a spoken-dialogue paper, an agent-memory paper and rendering slides: the gap is in the note's `## Pending index`.

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates: (1) **dialog presence**, first in the backlog for four iterations and the one flaw iteration 15 named on its own README GIF (a "Leave the game?" box 212 px wide, the menu's fill, the frame of every button); (2) prompts for Back and Select (a "B Back" row by the last input device); (3) a settings-screen kit (text size, interface size and skin as rows); (4) a key-rebinding row and an inventory grid; (5) narration and feedback for the controls. Section 4 ranks a missing widget above "looks flat", and the pick follows the log instead: the dialog needs no new API (a game's `dialog()` + `panel()` code is better the day it updates), costs nothing at rest, and is the confirm box every game ships; the runner-up needs a glyph vocabulary (Xbox, PlayStation and keyboard art the crate cannot ship) and a last-input-device resource, a design of its own, and the other three each need a widget the crate does not have yet.

**What changed** (research `d4b8805`; implementation `b48b552`, `cac1fb5`; docs `0fefc58`):
- A `panel()` that is a direct child of a `dialog()` is the dialog (`apply_skin`, the selector that already gave it an opaque fill, now read once as `in_dialog`; no new `GooeySkin` field, no new widget, no signature change): `min_width` 21 and `max_width` 36 body text sizes (420 to 720 px at the default size, scaling with `text_scale`; Microsoft's `ContentDialog` 548 epx maximum at this crate's 4/3), padding and row gap one and a half times a screen panel's, and its frame `outline` (3:1 against the panel by `problems()`) where a screen's is `surface_raised`. A `heading` and a `label` in it are the title and the body.
- The scrim pads itself `Val::Percent(5.0)` on every side (Microsoft's TV-safe 5%): a long body wraps at the maximum, or at 2x text the panel is squeezed to the window less its edges (1152 px of 1280), and nothing reaches the bezel.
- **Parchment's scrim 0.55 → 0.7** (an assumed decision, in the note's Deviations and the changelog): the first parchment capture was NOT YET, the cream card 1.94:1 off the dimmed cream menu behind it (the renderer blends in linear space, so 0.55 of a near-black dims cream only to (160,149,126)); at 0.7 it is (133,124,104) and the card stands 2.71:1 off it.
- Gallery: the Quit dialog has a `heading`, a line of body and the two choices. `docs/media/cancel.gif` regenerated (the old file showed the old box), `docs/media/dialog.png` new (both presets, 1176×268), `docs/guide/widgets.md` "A dialog", the README's navigation bullet and two captions read off the files, `dialog()`'s rustdoc and doctest, the media list, AGENTS layout and one rule, `docs/research/gooey.md`'s preset table.
- Assumed decisions (owner may override), in the note's Deviations: the measure's numbers and their conversion, the padding and gap factors, the `outline` frame, no lifted fill, no shadow (invisible on a 60% black scrim), the 5% edge, the parchment scrim.

**Sources.** Craft: Microsoft, *Dialog controls* ("Clearly identify the issue or the user's objective in the first line of the dialog's text."; "If a dialog title is used, use the content area to provide more detail … Don't repeat the title"; "Make sure that the title relates directly to the button choices.") and NN/g, Fessenden, *Modal & Nonmodal Dialogs* ("placing the dialog box in the middle of the screen as the focal point of the interface, made it very effective"). Shipping: Koncewicz, *Watch Your Game Prompts!* (Resident Evil 5's "Ready" confirmation: "it always makes me stop and think") and Microsoft, *Designing for Xbox and TV* ("Make the focus clear and unmistakable."; "avoid the screen edges … draw only non-essential visuals within 5% of the screen edges"; 15 epx text, which is this crate's 20 px). Technical: Microsoft Q&A ("The default maximum width of `ContentDialog` is 548."), W3C WCAG 2.2 1.4.11 and Bevy 0.20's taffy layout. home-still held none (two queries); every quotation was fetched on 2026-10-31. The WinUI theme file for the minimum width was not reachable (404) and the 21-text-size minimum rests on a search result.

**Checklist, before → after** (`/tmp/g16/findings.md`, `critique.md`, removed; 1280x720 dark unless noted; pixels from the stills):
- Dialog: about 214 × 173 px (x 533-747, y 274-447), a 180 px label, 16 px of air, fill (29,32,40), frame (51,56,69): frame vs fill **1.39:1**, vs the dimmed menu panel 1.61:1, vs the dimmed window 1.70:1. After: **540 × 244 px** (x 370-910, y 238-482), a 3 px frame (112,120,138), padding 21 px, 32 px heading, a 20 px body line, two 46.8 px choices: frame vs fill **3.68:1**, vs the dimmed panel 4.25:1, vs the dimmed window 4.49:1. At 2560x1440 the same dialog is 1080 px wide with a 6 px frame.
- Parchment: frame vs fill 3.81:1 (was 1.20:1); fill vs the dimmed menu panel 1.94:1 at the old scrim and **2.71:1** at 0.7; frame vs the dimmed window 3.10:1. The frame against the dimmed panel is 1.40:1: the card is read by its fill there (named in the critique).
- Text contrast unchanged (the fill is unchanged): heading and body 14.26:1 dark; choices 10.26:1 dark and 8.19:1 parchment; `GooeySkin::problems()` empty for both presets. Focus ring on Stay 3 px on all four sides (6 px at 1440p), blue on cream in parchment. Hit targets 46.8 px (≥ 44). Title to body and body to choices 15 px, padding + frame 24 px on every side (tests, ±1.5 px).
- Text in its box at the longest label: a 134-character body wraps in three lines at 720 px with 24 px each side; at 2.0x text the panel is 1152 px (64 to 1216, 5% of 1280) and the body still wraps in three lines inside it; at 1.5x text the dialog is 784 px wide, still centred. **Four stills with no dialog open are pixel-identical before and after** (dark at 1280x720 and 2560x1440, parchment and 1.5x text at 1280x720; `magick compare -metric AE` 0), so the change adds no pixel to any other state.

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): the dialog panel **GREAT**, the safe edge **GREAT**, parchment **GREAT after the scrim fix** (NOT YET before it). Flaws left, none against the validation targets: in dark the fill is the dimmed menu's own colour (1.16:1) and only the frame separates the layers (4.25:1), no lift and no shadow; in parchment the frame is 1.40:1 off the dimmed panel and the fill carries the edge; the width follows the body line (420 to 720 px), so two dialogs of one game can differ; nothing on screen says Escape or B closes it (the next backlog item); a panel wrapped in another node inside `dialog()` is a screen's panel again (documented, an AGENTS rule).

**Tests.** `tests/dialog_look.rs`, 6, headless window: a short dialog 420 px wide, centred, its choices 372 px (the panel's inside), 24 px of padding and frame on every side and title, body and choices 15 px apart; a 134-character body wraps at 720 px and stays inside the panel's padding, the choices inside the panel; 1.5x text makes the least measure 630 px and 2.0x squeezes the dialog to 64 and 1216 px; the `outline` frame, opaque fill, padding and gap of a dialog's panel and the `surface_raised` frame, `surface` fill, one padding and gap and no measure of a screen's, in both presets; the frame at 3:1 against its panel and the dimmed window in both presets; thirty idle frames write nothing. **Four of them fail without the change** (the measure 322 px against 420, the maximum 1280 against 720, 466 against 630, the frame colour), run with `src/skin.rs` from `HEAD` and the new one restored from a copy; the two that pass either way guard the presets' colours and the idle writes.

**mean_ms** (sib, Xvfb, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 12 alternating pairs of the gallery from `6daf050` and this tree): minimum 21.78 against 21.42 ms (−0.36), median 21.98 against 21.98, **paired median difference +0.03 ms** (mean 0.00). A first set of 12 pairs on a loaded host had three outliers (a heavier load in one binary's slot each, +14 to +22 ms) and, without them, a mean of −0.05 ms; six pairs with the Quit dialog open: median +0.07 ms, minimum 21.52 against 21.59. No regression: the change is in `apply_skin`, which restyles on a change only (a test holds thirty idle frames writing nothing), and a dialog adds a percentage padding and two limits to nodes that were laid out anyway.

### Ranked backlog

1. **Prompts for Back and Select**: a small `prompts(..)` row that shows "Esc Back" or "B Back" by the last input device (XAG 112's bottom-left prompts, "A Accept, B Back"), so Back is discoverable (NN/g: "clearly labeled and discoverable") without every game hand-drawing glyphs; the dialog now has the room for it, and the crate still has no glyph vocabulary.
2. **A settings-screen kit** from the controls in hand (`choice`, `slider`, `toggle`, `list`, `tabs`): text size (`GooeySkin::text_scale`), interface size (`UiScaleSettings::scale`) and skin as rows on a Display page; source: Xbox Accessibility Guideline 101 ("scale larger or smaller").
3. **A confirm builder** if games write the same four lines each time (`heading`, `label`, a `menu` of two choices, `AutoFocus` on the safe one): the note's deviation left `dialog()`'s signature alone; decide from what the owner's projects hand-roll (`confirm.rs`).
4. **A key-rebinding row** (with Escape to cancel the capture: Xbox's "Press \[ESC\] to exit remapping mode") and an **inventory grid** (a list in two dimensions): the widgets the owner's projects build by hand.
5. **Narration and feedback for the controls**: accesskit roles and values ("Volume, slider, 70%"; "Audio, tab, 1 of 3, selected"; a toast as a live region, WCAG 4.1.3), a `UiFeedbackKind::Adjust` tick per step crossed, smooth scrolling, and a `UiFeedbackKind::Cancel` for the sound of backing out; and balanced tooltip wrapping.

## Iteration 17 — 2026-10-31 — binding: a row that rebinds a key, mouse button or pad button (adopting the killed passes' build)

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey`: no open issues (empty list, no `403`/`429`). The previous audit row (`loop-stats`, 00:31Z) said `missing: release`, but it predates the release it was waiting for: v0.4.1 is on crates.io (2026-11-01T02:23Z, 21:23 CDT, checked at crates.io's API), so no leftover job. Parked work, looked up after triage: iteration 17's build was written by pass 33 (`loop/iter-17`, parked as `WIP(pass 33)`), parked again by pass 34 (`loop/iter-17-binding`), and re-adopted by passes 35, 37 and 38 (`loop/iter-17-adopt`, `-ship`, `-gate`); every one was killed before its gate finished. The three adopt branches hold the same tree (`git diff` between them is empty), `loop/iter-17-binding` differs only in `CHANGELOG.md` and `Cargo.toml` (an older base). This pass cherry-picked `-gate`'s two commits onto `loop/iter-17-pass39` from `main` and left all five branches, as the rules say. Home-still, recorded live (21:50 CDT): `hs status` healthy (18673 documents, 18073 embedded; two scribes converting; distill idle on CUDA; Qdrant 1.20.0); `catalog_recent` (limit 200) held psychology, biomedical and SIGGRAPH embeds and two software downloads, nothing on game UI (a grep of the names for interface, HUD, gamepad, menu, accessibility, usability, rebind, controller found none), so no `Research: corpus` commit.

**Why four passes died, and what this pass did differently.** `wiggum@gooey.service` has `MemoryHigh=3G` (`wiggum@gooey.service.d/memory.conf`) and `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=3`; `journalctl --user -u wiggum@gooey.service` shows `systemd-oomd killed 218 process(es) in this unit` at 21:41:54 and `144 process(es)` at 21:47:04 (`Consumed 2h 1min CPU over 14 min`, then `18 min CPU over 5 min`, each "3G memory peak"), and the unit came back two minutes later (`Restart=always`, `RestartSec=120`): the driver log's `driver START` every 5 to 14 minutes from 21:15 is that, not the owner. The kills came when a pass started its gate. This pass ran every cargo command with `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1` (one rustc or link at a time) from a script that logs the cgroup's `memory.current` per step: the cgroup's peak was 3.23 GB, almost all page cache (`anon` 384 MB, `file` 1.9 GB while linking tests), `memory.events` showed `high` throttling and `oom_kill 0`, and the unit was not killed. The unit's setting is the owner's, so it is untouched; the lesson is that a pass on this host gates with one job.

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates: (1) **binding**, the first half of the backlog's item 4 and the one the committed note `0d33b66` researched; the build exists and was never gated, so finishing it is the shortest road to a widget every game with a controls screen ships (Game Accessibility Guidelines, XAG 107); (2) prompts for Back and Select (needs a glyph vocabulary the crate does not have); (3) a settings-screen kit; (4) an inventory grid; (5) narration. The pick is (1): research done, tests written (1070 lines), docs and GIF made; only the gate stood between it and a release.

**What changed** (research `0d33b66`; build `1112b15`; docs `35925b7`; this pass's fixes `f36c608`):
- `binding(label, Binding::Key(KeyCode::Space))` is a row of the label and a keycap, Bevy's headless `Button` at the root, state in `BindingValue`, each change said once as `ValueChange<Binding>`. Enter, Space, South and a click start a capture: the keycap lights in the accent and says "Press a key, Esc exits" (a pad row "Press a button, Menu exits"), and the player's next press is the binding. A key row takes any key and any mouse button (the primary one too, behind a transparent `Catcher` node so it presses nothing), a pad row any pad button; Escape and Start exit and cannot be bound (`BindingSettings::cancel_key`, `cancel_button`); a key on a pad row or a pad button on a key row exits. While a row waits the crate's own input systems stand still (`not_capturing`), the row stops every key (Tab is bindable) and no `Cancel` is sent for the Escape that exits. `Binding` is `Eq + Hash`, `label(PadStyle)` names it ("Space", "Right Click", "A" or "Cross"), the rows' keycaps never change size.
- Fixes this pass found by running the gate: clippy `type_complexity` on `update_bindings`' change filter (now the alias `Written`), and two rustdoc links (`[ESC]`, and `ValueChange<Binding>` that is not in `widgets.rs`'s scope); the crate doc's widget list rewrapped.
- Gallery: the Controls tab holds `Jump` (key) and `Use` (pad), logs and toasts the key. `docs/media/binding.gif` is new and `tabs.gif` regenerated (it showed the two old buttons), `docs/guide/bindings.md`, the README bullet, `docs/research/binding.md`, `AGENTS.md`'s layout and one rule, the changelog.

**Sources** (from the note; the corpus has nothing on remapping, recorded under its Pending index): craft, Game Accessibility Guidelines, *Allow controls to be remapped / reconfigured* ("remappable controls are one of the best value accessibility features") and Microsoft's *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 107* ("Players should be given the option to remap all of the controls within the game itself"); shipping, XAG 112's remapping screen ("Press any key or mouse button to remap", "Press [ESC] to exit remapping mode"; Grounded, Gears 5) and the GAG's Overwatch remapping page; technical, W3C *UI Events KeyboardEvent code Values* and Bevy 0.20.0's `bevy_input`, `bevy_ui_widgets` and `bevy_input_focus` source (which handlers also see the press).

**Checklist** (`/tmp/g39/findings.md`, `critique.md`; dark 1280x720 unless noted; pixels from the captures): rest and waiting differ in one 334x32 box (the keycap's inside; 668x64 at 1440p), so the row and the panel move 0 px. Name on the keycap 10.26:1, prompt on the lit keycap 9.39:1, lit keycap against the panel 8.36:1 (parchment 8.19, 7.37 and 5.85); the resting keycap's fill is 1.39:1 off the panel and its `outline` frame carries the 3:1 edge. Ring on the focused row 3 px (6 px at 2560x1440), row 53 px high (52.8, hit target above 44), keycaps of the two rows in one column (x 340-678), the longest prompt 312 px in a 338 px keycap. Dark and parchment at both sizes with the Audio page showing are pixel-identical to `main` (`magick compare -metric AE` 0, four stills); at 1.5x text the gallery's panel is 674 px wide against 630 (the Controls page's keycap, 17 text sizes, is the widest page now; the panel does not move on a tab turn).

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): the row in dark, in parchment, at 1.5x text, at 2560x1440 and with the pad prompt **GREAT**; `binding.gif` **GREAT** (eight frames sampled: Audio, Controls, ring on Jump, "Press a key, Esc exits", Q, ring on Use, "Press a button, Menu exits", back to A); `tabs.gif` shows the Controls page with the two rows. Flaws left, none against the validation targets: the gallery panel's 44 px at 1.5x; a short name sits in a 338 px keycap (the note's trade for a fixed column and a panel that never jumps); the longest prompt leaves 13 px of air, so a longer translation widens the keycap (never clipped); the resting keycap reads by its frame.

**Tests.** `tests/binding.rs` (adopted, 1070 lines, headless window): names by kind, a value the game sets, the four starts and the press that started it never the binding, one binding once with focus and ring kept, Escape exiting with no `Cancel`, Tab, arrows, Enter, PageDown, Home and a modifier bound like any key, the mouse catcher, a pad row, the wrong device exiting, focus the game takes ending a capture, size unchanged at 1x, 2x and 1.5x text, contrast and idle frames writing nothing; plus rows in `tests/serde.rs` and the doctests.

No regression above 0.5 ms in the minimum or the quiet pairs; at rest nothing runs but six one-query run conditions, `bind_by_input`, `catch_pointer` and `update_bindings` over empty or unchanged queries.

## Iteration 18 — 2026-10-31 — prompts: the Select and Back hints, in the words of the player's last device

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey`: no open issues (empty list, no `403`/`429`). The last audit row on `loop-stats` (pass 39, 03:31Z) had `missing: -` and v0.5.0 released, so no leftover job. Parked work: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (old, a Focus checkpoint) and the `loop/iter-17-*` branches are all adopted into `main` through `loop/iter-17-pass39`; nothing parked for this item. home-still (checked 2026-11-01 03:32Z): `system_status` up, distill healthy (Cuda, bge-m3, 488,845 chunks), two scribe instances healthy; `hs distill search` answered, the MCP bridge did not fail. Corpus re-scan, `catalog_recent` limit 200: psychology, autism, parenting and social-science embeds, nothing on game UI, so no `Research: corpus` commit.

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates, from iteration 17's backlog: (1) **prompts** (Back and Select hints by the last device), (2) a settings-screen kit, (3) conflicts for `binding`, (4) an inventory grid and a confirm builder, (5) narration and feedback. The pick is (1): Back is already sent to the game (`Cancel`) but no screen says so (NN/g's "show a clear way to exit"), Xbox's guideline 112 asks for the prompts on every screen, and the crate has the one thing missing for them, the names (`Binding::label`); what it lacked was the fact every pad-and-keyboard game hand-rolls, which device the player is using. The runner-up, the settings kit, composes widgets that exist; prompts add a capability (`InputDevice`).

**What changed** (research `14c1b92`; build `636d14c`; gallery `1d2310b`; docs `66b7077`; this pass's last doc touch in the commit after):
- `prompt(label, Prompt::SELECT)` is one hint: a keycap and the game's words. `prompts()` is the legend: a bar on the skin's `surface` in its parent's bottom-left corner, 5% from the left and bottom (absolute, so it moves nothing), no wider than 90% of its parent; one inside a `dialog()` stands at the same corner over the scrim. The keycap names the input of the device the player used last: `Binding::label`, so "Enter", "Esc", "Right Click", "A" or "Cross" (by `BindingSettings::pad_style`), and is a rounded rectangle for a key and fully round for a pad button (a circle for "A", a pill for "LB"), the same colours as a binding keycap at rest.
- `Prompt { keyboard, pad }` (component, the prompt's state: set it after a rebind and the keycap follows in the frame), `Prompt::SELECT` (Enter, South), `Prompt::BACK` (Escape, East), `Prompt::new`. `InputDevice` (`Keyboard` or `Pad`, a resource both plugins initialise) kept by `track_input_device`, first in `GooeySystems::Navigate`: a key or mouse button press is `Keyboard`, a pad button press or a stick pushed past `model::STICK_PUSH` (0.5) is `Pad`, and a mouse that only moves changes nothing. A game reads `InputDevice` to swap glyphs of its own. `Binding::device()` replaces the private `BindingDevice` component (a binding row's device is its value's).
- `update_prompts` (Style, before `apply_skin`), `Role::{Prompts, Prompt, PromptKey, PromptButton}`; every part is `Pickable::IGNORE` and none is a tab stop. Gallery: the legend in the corner and in the Quit dialog, `GOOEY_KEYS` tokens `plug` and `pad`; `docs/guide/prompts.md`, a README bullet (the navigation one extended) and `prompts.gif`, `gallery.png` and `gallery.gif` regenerated (the legend is in them now), host-api, media list, AGENTS layout and rules, changelog.
- Found by running it: a `flex_wrap: Wrap` bar with auto width broke a line a float's width early when the pad's names (narrower than the keyboard's) shrank the bar, so "B Back" wrapped under "A Select" in the first capture; the legend is a plain row with `max_width` 90% and squeezes its words instead (AGENTS.md says why).

**Sources** (`docs/research/prompts.md`; the corpus has nothing on prompts, recorded under its Pending index): craft, Cockburn et al., *Supporting Novice to Expert Transitions in User Interfaces* (`10.1145_2659796`: "a tendency for users to persistently fail to adopt faster methods") and Malacria et al., *Promoting Hotkey use through rehearsal with ExposeHK* (`10.1145_2470654.2470735`: "when participants are aware of EHK they use it to their advantage"); shipping, Microsoft's *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 112* (Sea of Thieves' "select" and "back" in "the bottom-left corner of the screen", "in the same location and relative order throughout the navigation experience") and Epic's *Common UI Quickstart Guide* (one action, "Keyboard Input Type Info" and "Default Gamepad Input Type Info", the icon swapped by the input last used); technical, Bevy 0.20.1's `bevy_input` messages (`GamepadButtonStateChangedEvent`, `GamepadAxisChangedEvent`, `KeyboardInput`, `MouseButtonInput`) and this crate's own `gamepad_select`, `cancel_by_input` and `Binding::label`.

**Checklist** (`/tmp/g18/findings.md`, `critique.md`; dark 1280x720 unless noted; pixels from the captures): outside the legend, dark and parchment at 1280x720 and 2560x1440 are identical to `main`'s (`magick compare -metric AE` 0 above the legend's rows); the legend is 338x53 at (64, 631) at 720p (left 5% of 1280, bottom 5% of 720) and exactly twice that at 1440p. Legend words on the bar 14.22:1 (parchment 10.20), a keycap's name on its fill 10.26:1 (8.19), its frame on the bar 3.67:1 (3.96); cap 38.4 px high (a binding keycap's), cap to words 10 px (`gap`), prompt to prompt 28 px (two paddings), 17 px from the bar's edge to the first cap's (14 padding and a 3 px frame). `problems()` is empty for both presets (test). A throwaway capture with five prompts in PlayStation names held "Page Down", "Cross", "Circle", "R2", "L3" in their caps.

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): the legend in dark and parchment at both sizes, the keyboard and pad states, the dialog's legend and `prompts.gif` (115 frames, 7.7 s) **GREAT**. One flaw, **named and not fixed**: at 1.5x text on a 720p window the gallery's panel (654 px tall, already past the 5% top margin) runs into the legend's corner and the bar covers "Subtitles" (`dark-large-1280x720.png`); an absolute legend makes no room by contract, and the guide says so with the remedy (padding under the content, or a `list`). The README shows no 1.5x capture. Others, left: 4 px of air between the bar's frame and a keycap's (a binding row's), frames as heavy as a panel's (one language), pad buttons as words in round caps (no glyph art, by design).

**Tests.** `tests/prompts.rs` (14, headless window, real keyboard, mouse and gamepad input): the keyboard's words at start, a pad button turning every prompt to the pad's and a key or a mouse button back, a mouse that only moves changing nothing, a stick pushed hard switching and a nudge not, `PadStyle::PlayStation` ("Cross", "Circle"), `InputDevice` and `Prompt` set by a game (same keycap, no new entity, a mouse binding is a key's shape), the legend 5% from the left and bottom at 720p, 1080p and 1440p, adding it moving no other widget, a dialog's legend at the same corner, five long prompts squeezing inside 90% of the window, a character a circle or square at a key's height and a pad button fully round, contrast in both presets, the pointer going through and Tab visiting the button only, thirty idle frames and a repeated press of the same device writing nothing. Mutation checks (stick threshold 0.5 to 0.1; the legend made pickable): each fails exactly its tests (`a_stick_pushed_hard_is_the_pad_and_a_nudge_is_not`, `the_pointer_goes_through_the_legend_and_no_prompt_is_a_stop`); both files restored and compared.

**mean_ms** (sib, Xvfb, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs of the gallery from `d368f06` and this tree, host load 7 to 13): minimum 22.30 against 22.18 ms (-0.12), medians 23.07 and 23.03, paired median -0.24; the paired differences run -2.08 to +1.05. No regression above 0.5 ms. At rest the cost is four empty message queues, one resource flag check and an empty query.
