### Ranked backlog

1. **A settings-screen kit** from the controls in hand (`choice`, `slider`, `toggle`, `list`, `tabs`, `binding`, and now `prompts`): text size, interface size and skin as rows on a Display page (XAG 101), a controls page that lists a game's actions as `binding` rows in a `list`, with the legend under it.
2. **Context prompts**: a legend that follows focus (a slider shows "Left Right Adjust", a toggle "Select Toggle", a tab "LB RB Page"), the way Sea of Thieves' prompts change "depending on the context of the specific UI screen"; needs one input shown as two (a chord or a pair), the open gap in `prompts.md`.
3. **A safe corner**: a way for a screen to reserve the legend's corner (padding or a `SafeArea` setting), so a tall panel at the player's largest text size never runs under it (this pass's one named flaw).
4. **Conflicts for `binding`** (a `BindingConflict` helper or an unbound state) and **an inventory grid** (a `list` in two dimensions); a confirm builder if games write the same four lines each time.
5. **Narration and feedback for the controls**: accesskit roles and values, a toast as a live region, a `UiFeedbackKind::Adjust` tick per step, smooth scrolling, a `UiFeedbackKind::Cancel`; balanced tooltip wrapping; a layout-aware key name (the web's `KeyboardLayoutMap`) for AZERTY players.

## Iteration 19 — 2026-11-01 — text input: a row with a field the player types in (adopting the parked pass-41 build)

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey`: no open issues (empty list, no `403`/`429`). The last audit row on `loop-stats` (pass 40, 04:18Z) had `missing: -` with v0.6.0 released, so no leftover job. Parked work, looked up before picking: `loop/iter-19` (`origin/loop/iter-19`) holds pass 41's research commit (`ba82255`, text input) and the driver's `WIP(pass 41, parked)` commit `ef8e710`, 17 files never built, tested or gated (`src/text.rs` 290 lines, `tests/text.rs` 984, a guide page, gallery knobs, docs). It is the item this pass would pick, so it was adopted: `loop/iter-19-text` from `main`, `ba82255` cherry-picked, the rest checked out of `ef8e710`; `loop/iter-19` itself untouched. `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` and the `loop/iter-17-*` branches are old and already in `main`. home-still (2026-11-01 05:00Z): `hs status` up (distill idle on Cuda, two scribes, Qdrant 1.20.0, 488,845 chunks); `catalog_recent` limit 200 holds psychology, couples and SIGGRAPH embeds and nothing on game UI, so no `Research: corpus` commit.

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates: (1) **text input** (a missing widget every game needs: a name, a seed, a server address; the owner's editor hand-rolls `text_field` and `NameBox` and says its caret idiom "was written five times before it had one home"); researched and drafted by pass 41, so finishing it is the shortest road to a widget no other can stand in for; (2) a settings-screen kit; (3) context prompts; (4) a safe corner for the legend; (5) conflicts for `binding`, an inventory grid, narration. The pick is (1): it adds a capability (typing, `TextEditing` for a game's hotkeys and a pad's platform keyboard) where the runner-up (2) composes widgets that exist.

**What changed** (research `7371f12`; build `6d6602c`; docs `f99de65`; media `6e152e0`; clippy fix `9128875`; merge `bb676aa`; release `7b2b1fa`):
- `text_input(label, value)` is a row like a toggle's: the label on the left, outside the field and always visible, and a framed field on the right: Bevy 0.20's headless `TextInput` + `EditableText` (caret, selection, copy, paste, IME). State is `TextInputValue(String)` on the row (set it from code and the field shows it with no event back); each edit that changes the text is one `ValueChange<String>` with `is_final: false`, and the end of the edit (Enter, a submit even with nothing changed, or focus leaving after a change) one with `is_final: true`. `TextHint` (muted words while empty; the label is never the hint, NN/g) and `TextLimit` go on the row. A focused field takes the keys at once; Left, Right, Home, End are the caret's, **Up, Down, Tab, the D-pad and Escape leave it** (WCAG 2.1.2), a click on the label, the field or the row's padding focuses it. `TextEditing` (on the row exactly while its field has focus) and `is_text_editing` are the host's hook: a game's hotkeys wait on it, and a host opens the platform's keyboard for a pad player when it is added (Valve). The frame is the `outline`, lighter under the pointer, the `focus_ring` while the field holds focus **even after a pointer press hid every other ring**; `problems()` checks two more pairs (`text_muted` on `surface` 4.5:1, `focus_ring` on `surface` 3:1). `Role::{TextInput, TextCell, TextField, TextHint}`, `track_text_editing` (Navigate) and `update_text_inputs` (Style).
- **This pass's fixes to the draft.** (a) Found by capturing it: a full 16-character name in a 16-character-wide field scrolled its first letter away by 12 px (the gallery's Save as field at its `TextLimit(16)` showed "ria-of-the-Long"), because Bevy keeps one character of room after the caret (`bevy_text-0.20.0/src/scroll.rs`); the field is 17 characters wide now, and `a_line_that_fills_a_limit_of_sixteen_shows_whole_with_its_caret` holds it at 1x, 2x and 1.5x text (it fails at 16: "scrolled away by 12 px", mutation-checked). (b) clippy `type_complexity` on `text_row_on_acquire`'s query: the alias `EnabledField`. (c) Layout: the Save as row is label + gap + the field wide, which widens the gallery's Load game panel from 302 to 402 px and moves the settings panel 50 px left; `tabs.gif` and `binding.gif` regenerate **byte-identical** with their crop x moved (`cmp`), `cancel.gif` (now 948 px wide), `prompts.gif` and `dialog.png` were regenerated, and `docs/media/README.md`'s commands carry the new crops.
- Docs: `docs/guide/text-input.md`, README bullet, caption and `text-input.gif` (75 frames, 5.0 s, 600×560), regenerated `gallery.png` and `gallery.gif`, host-api, navigation, the widget table, AGENTS layout and rules, the research note's Deviations (the 17-character width is one), the changelog.

**Sources** (`docs/research/text-input.md`; home-still has nothing on game text entry, recorded under its Pending index): craft, Wobbrock, Myers and Aung, *Writing With A Joystick* ("prevalent joystick text entry methods are slow because they are selection-based") and Nielsen Norman Group, *Placeholders in Form Fields Are Harmful* ("Fields need labels and (sometimes) instructions, but placing this text inside the field lowers usability and accessibility"); shipping, Valve, *Getting your game ready for Steam Deck* ("We strongly recommend (and require for Deck/Machine Verified) that games automatically display an on-screen keyboard when requiring the user to input text") and the Steamworks SDK 1.52 `ShowFloatingGamepadTextInput` note; technical, W3C *Understanding Success Criterion 2.1.2: No Keyboard Trap* and Bevy 0.20.0's `bevy_text` (`EditableText`, `TextEditChange`, the scroll rules), `bevy_ui_widgets` `text_input.rs` and `bevy_input_focus`.

**Checklist** (`/tmp/g19m/findings.md`, `critique.md`, removed; dark 1280x720 unless noted; pixels from the stills): typed text 14.26:1 on the field (parchment 9.81:1), hint 6.65:1 (4.66:1); resting frame against the panel 3.67:1 (3.96:1); focus ring 8.78:1 (4.56:1); ring 3 px (6 px at 2560x1440); field 44 px high at 720p and 88 px at 1440p, the row a toggle's height (test, 1 px); the row's label stands where the toggle's does, 21 px + frame in from each panel's inner edge (test, 1 px); 60 characters typed move no row, panel, field or neighbour at 1x, 2x and 1.5x (test); `problems()` empty for both presets. **Layout moved**: Load game panel 302 → 402 px, settings panel x 234 → 184 (see above); the settings panel's frames are byte-identical (`tabs.gif`, `binding.gif`).

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): the field at rest in dark, parchment and 2560x1440, focused and typed, and `text-input.gif` (quiet outline over the muted hint; blue ring and caret while "Aria" is typed; Enter shows "Saved as Aria"; Up moves the ring onto `Cellar, 01:48`, the field quiet again with "Aria" kept) **GREAT**; the long line at the limit **GREAT after the width fix** (NOT YET before it); the regenerated hero, `cancel.gif`, `prompts.gif`, `dialog.png` **GREAT**. Flaws left, none against the validation targets: the field's fill is the panel's own colour (1.04:1 in parchment) and it reads by its frame alone, as a binding keycap does; the label-to-field gap is the skin's `gap` (about 11 px); the caret's blink puts dark-focus.png and dark-typed.png in the off phase (the GIF shows it on); **not captured, held by tests only**: the hover frame, the selection and a disabled field (the gallery's `hover` token targets the slider thumb); at 1.5x text the settings panel still runs into the legend (iteration 18's named flaw); a pad cannot type (by design, `TextEditing` is the hook).

**Tests.** `tests/text.rs`, 22 in the same headless window, driven by real keyboard, pointer and gamepad input: the field right of its label, a target high, the hint over it while empty; each edit one non-final change and none for a caret move; Enter a final change every time and the field kept; leaving after an edit one final change and after none nothing; a value the game sets shown with no event; a limit that stops the player and never cuts the game's text; Left and Right the caret's and Up, Down, Tab and the D-pad leaving (a button beside it proves it); a click on the label, the field or the row's edge focusing it; the frame lit after a pointer press; `TextEditing` exactly while focused; a dialog that opens on its field and Escape one `Cancel`; a disabled row; a tooltip on the row opening while its field has focus; contrast in both presets; 60 characters moving nothing at three scales; **the new one for the limit**; thirty idle frames and the caret's blink writing nothing.

**mean_ms** (sib, Xvfb, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs of the gallery from `578aac2` and this tree, host load average 21 from other loops): minimum 22.93 against 22.32 ms (−0.61), medians 24.93 and 26.71, paired median **+0.02 ms** (mean −1.73; the pairs run from −12.7 to +6.9 ms, outliers either way from other builds). No regression above 0.5 ms in the minimum or the paired median; the gallery now holds one more widget and Bevy's text-input layout. At rest the new code is `track_text_editing` (returns when focus did not change), `update_text_inputs` (change filters over an empty set) and observers that run only on an edit, a key or a press.

**Gate.** Run once with `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1` from a script (memory.current at most 1.9 GB, no kill): `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo build`, `--release`, `cargo test` (23 `test result: ok`, 312 tests, no failure), `--features serde` (23 ok), the README test, `cargo build --examples` green; `cargo clippy --all-targets --all-features -D warnings` failed once (`type_complexity`, fixed in `9128875`); then fmt, clippy, `cargo doc -D warnings`, the wasm32 library build and `cargo test --test text` green. The alias is the only source change after the full test run; the merged tree is the gated tree (`git diff --stat loop/iter-19-text main` empty).

**Process notes.** Things that went against the rules, recorded so they are not repeated: **`cp` of my baseline gallery to `~/g19_bin/gallery_before` overwrote a file pass 41 had left there (the directory's mtime did not change, so the name existed), and my "before" stills went into `/tmp/g19/before/` beside pass 41's, over stills of the same names that were there (that directory grew by one name, not five); nothing of value was lost (both are regenerated by the build and capture commands), but I did not check either directory first.** After that I kept my own scratch in `/tmp/g19m` and `~/g19`. Reads issued in the same turn as the bash that makes their file ran before it (a missing-file error, then fine); an `edit` of a long line needs its `:raw` read first. No baseline target directory: the baseline worktree `~/g19_base` (detached at `578aac2`) built into the shared `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` in 2 min 10 s.

**Merge.** `loop/iter-19-text` pushed and merged into `main` with `--no-ff` (`bb676aa`); `origin/main` had not moved. `main` pushed.

## Iteration 20 — 2026-11-01 — player options: text size, interface size and controller as one-line rows

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey`: no open issues (empty list, no `403`/`429`). The last audit row on `loop-stats` (pass 42, 05:37Z) had `missing: -` with v0.7.0 released, so no leftover job. Parked work, looked up before picking: `git branch --list 'wiggum/parked-*'` holds only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, not this item) and `loop/iter-19` is last pass's adopted draft; no `loop/iter-20`, no parked commit newer than pass 41's, so nothing to adopt. The corpus re-scan (`catalog_recent`, limit 200) found embed events for psychology and clinical papers, SIGGRAPH 2024 and 2026 papers and software books and no game-UI, HUD, menu, navigation or accessibility document `corpus.md` lacks, so it is unchanged. home-still: `system_status` healthy (both scribes, distill idle on CUDA), searches answered in 2 to 5 s.

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates: (1) **the options rows for what the crate itself reads** (the first slice of the log's settings-screen kit): `text_size`, `interface_size` and `pad_style`, each a `choice` that edits one field of `GooeySkin`, `UiScaleSettings` or `BindingSettings` and follows it, so a game wires nothing and a saved game shows on the rows; the player-facing form of XAG 101 and WCAG 1.4.4, the one accessibility setting every game ships and the one only this crate can keep true (it owns `text_scale`); (2) a name prompt (`dialog()` + `text_input`); (3) context prompts; (4) a safe corner for the legend; (5) text-input depth, an inventory grid, narration. The pick is (1): it removes the same eight lines from every game's options screen and, by promising 200%, put the whole widget set under a test it had never had (`tests/large_text.rs`), where (2) composes widgets already there. Runner-up (2).

**What changed** (research `a335c2a`; build `a040c37`; docs `e2b5ee2`; merge `b3da583`; release `83368ae`):
- `text_size(label)` (100, 125, 150, 200%), `interface_size(label)` (75, 100, 125, 150%) and `pad_style(label)` (Xbox, PlayStation, Switch) are a `choice` plus `OptionRow` and the row's own observer. The setting lives once, in its resource: a player's step writes that one field and no other (a custom skin, the scale's `reference_short_side`, the binding prompts survive; one step, one write, one `ValueChange<usize>`); `update_option_rows` (public, `GooeySystems::Style` before `update_choices`) sets the row to `model::nearest_step` of the resource on its change flag and for a row just spawned, with `set_if_neq` and no event, so a loaded save shows on the row in its first frame, a second row follows the first and a value between steps (1.1) shows the nearest and is left alone. `model` gains `TEXT_SIZE_STEPS`, `INTERFACE_SIZE_STEPS`, `PAD_STYLES`, `nearest_step` and `PadStyle::name`. A row whose resource is absent (`interface_size` without `GooeyScalePlugin`) steps and breaks nothing.
- **Found by capturing, both fixed and both in the changelog.** (a) The first still showed the three rows' arrows and values out of line (each choice's value cell was as wide as its own widest option: "PlayStation" against "100%"; the gallery's Quality and Window rows had always had it): a choice's value cell is never narrower than 8 body text sizes now (`CHOICE_MIN_TEXT_SIZES`), `tests/choice.rs` holds one column for back arrow, value and forward arrow across four choices (mutation-checked), and `tabs.gif` is regenerated for the Display page. (b) A GIF frame at 125% text had one row twice as tall with its label at the top; it looked like a transient and was 36 identical frames. A sweep of every 5% from 100% to 200% found it only at 1.25: the row that decides its panel's width came out a hair under its label's 210 px and the label wrapped (height measured at two lines, glyphs drawn on one). The label of a toggle, slider, choice, binding and text input, a choice's options, a slider's readout and a binding's keycap parts are `NoWrap` now (`ONE_LINE`).
- `examples/options` (the three rows and the legend on a screen of their own, the panel at the top so at 200% it stands clear of the legend; the gallery's capture knobs and D-pad tokens `padup`, `paddown`, `padleft`, `padright`), `docs/guide/options.md`, the README bullet and `options.gif` (82 frames, 5.5 s, 960×540, 416 KB), host-api, the widget table, the media list, AGENTS (layout lines and one rule), the changelog, the research note's Deviations.

**Sources** (`docs/research/options.md`; home-still has the craft paper and nothing on text size in shipped games, recorded under its Pending index; `paper_search` found nothing either): craft, Cairns, Barlet, Haynes and Power, *Future design of accessibility in games: A design vocabulary* (stem `10.1016_j.ijhcs.2019.06.010`: "all the guidelines could be framed as options, things that could be selected or not according to the particular needs and abilities of players", and "disability does not define the audience", with Beeston et al.'s 27 players with hearing loss against 83 who use subtitles); shipping, Microsoft's *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 101* ("Players should be able to resize text up to 200 percent … without the loss of content, functionality, or meaning"; "Any adjustable text settings … should carry over to all important text—this includes additional windows that overlay a UI") and the Game Accessibility Guidelines' *Allow interfaces to be resized* and *EVE Online UI scaling* ("UI scaleable up to 150%"); technical, W3C *Understanding SC 1.4.4: Resize Text* ("If the resizing mechanism … offers incremental resizing steps between 100% and 200%, authors must ensure that there is no loss of content or functionality at these incremental steps"; failure F69) and Buitinck et al. (stem `W1728842521`, "one way to do a thing").

**Checklist** (`/tmp/g20/findings.md`, `critique.md`, removed; dark 1280x720 unless noted; pixels from the stills): row label 14.22:1 on the panel (parchment 10.20:1); the focus ring 3 px on the focused row (6 px at 1440p); a row is a choice's height (53 px at 720p, a toggle's within 1 px by test), arrows 44 px; the three rows' back arrow, value and forward arrow in one column each (before: Controller's left arrow 84 px left of the others); `tests/large_text.rs`: a panel of every widget, a dialog, a toast and the legend have no child more than 1 px outside its parent at 100, 125, 150 and 200% (a squeezed-panel control, throwaway, reports 15 escapes, so the check can fail), and every control's text is one line at every 5% from 100% to 200% with each widget alone in its panel; `problems()` empty for both presets. The gallery's four stills (dark, parchment, 1.5x text, 2560×1440) are byte-identical before and after (`compare -metric AE` 0, with `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`: the first comparison without it differed by 138 to 5298 px, the draining meter).

**Critique and verdict** (`critique.md`): `final-dark.png`, `final-parchment.png`, `final-parchment-150.png` (Text size 150% loaded from `GOOEY_TEXT_SCALE` and shown on the row, Interface size stepped to 125% under the ring), `final-200.png` and `final-1440.png` **GREAT**; `options.gif` **GREAT after fix (b)** (NOT YET before: frame 19); `tabs.gif` regenerated, the Display page aligned, **GREAT**. Flaws left, none against the validation targets: a real options screen of eight rows is taller than 720 px at 150% text and the interface does not scroll for a game (the guide says so and sends long parts to a `list`); the heading is 30 px left of the row labels (the crate-wide look since the first panel); 200% text on 150% interface is 3x, past a 720p window, the player's choice. The gallery at 1.5x text still has the legend over its Subtitles row (`dark-large.png`, iteration 18's safe-corner flaw, unchanged).

**Tests.** `tests/options.rs`, 11 in the same headless window and driven by real keyboard input: a row spawned over a loaded setting shows it in its first frame (over a settled app, so only the row's own `Added` can; mutation-checked), the steps in order, a value the game sets moves the row with no event and a whole new skin leaves it where its `text_scale` says, a value between two steps shows the nearest and is left alone, a second row follows the first with no event of its own, a step writes one field and no other and one write a step and none at the end, `UiScale` 1.25 the frame after a step and 0.75 after two back, Controller turns a binding's keycap "A", "Cross", "B", a disabled row, a row whose plugin is left out, nothing written while idle (mutation-checked). `tests/large_text.rs`, 3 (the two above; the one-line sweep fails at 1.25 without `ONE_LINE`, mutation-checked after the first version of it did not: its panel was wider than the row, so the tightness never occurred, and each widget alone in its own panel reproduces it). `tests/choice.rs` +1 (one column). `model` +2 unit tests and a doctest.

**mean_ms** (the gallery from `c3d4fcb` against this tree, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs, Xvfb on sib at load 0.2 to 1): minimum 21.37 against 21.46 ms (+0.09), medians 22.14 and 21.91, paired median **0.00 ms** (mean −0.11; the pairs run from −0.82 to +0.39). No regression above 0.5 ms. The gallery does not spawn the rows; the new code is a change-gated system over an empty set and three observers that run on a step.

**Gate.** Run once from `~/g20/gate.sh` with `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1`, in an `async` call with the script in the foreground (the detached `setsid nohup … & disown` start left an empty log and no process again): `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy --all-targets --all-features -D warnings` (green the first time), `cargo doc --no-deps -D warnings`, the wasm32 library build, `cargo build`, `--release`, `--examples`, `cargo test` (51 `test result: ok` lines over the three test runs, 673 tests, no failure), `--features serde` and the README test, 13.4 minutes, `memory.current` at most 2.0 GB, no kill. The merged tree is the gated tree (`git diff --stat loop/iter-20 main` empty; `origin/main` had not moved).

**Process notes.** Things that went against the rules, recorded so they are not repeated: **I edited `docs/research/options.md` once with a `python3` heredoc (a string replace) instead of the `edit` tool**, and used `sed -i` three times, but only to apply mutations to a file I restored from a `/tmp` copy and `cmp`'d afterwards; every other edit went through `edit`. The first pixel-identity run of the gallery stills differed because the health meter drains with time (hold it with `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`), and the second hung inside a `for` loop for the whole 400 s call (one gallery run never exited), so the retry put `timeout 100` on every run. `xdpyinfo` is not installed, so "display DOWN" from it means nothing; `Xvfb :93` was up. A `git diff main -- src/ | grep '^-'` after the multi-range `lib.rs` edit caught that `PUT 120.=121:` had replaced `mod nav; mod prompt;` with `mod nav; mod options;` and dropped `mod prompt;` (restored before the first build). The `todo` tool prints the whole list on every call: one batched `done` per phase next time.

**Merge.** `loop/iter-20` pushed and merged into `main` with `--no-ff` (`b3da583`); `main` pushed.

**Release: v0.7.1**, https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.7.1 (published 2026-11-01T06:50Z, 01:50 CDT; tag `v0.7.1`, release commit `83368ae`; the crates.io API returns the version, not yanked). `cargo semver-checks check-release` (0.51.0) against 0.7.0: 202 checks, 202 pass, 58 skip, no breaking change (a new function, constants, a `#[non_exhaustive]` enum and a method: no `Role` variant, no changed signature), so the version is a patch bump before 1.0. `cargo publish --dry-run` packaged 26 files (495.9 KiB, 132.6 KiB compressed), then `cargo publish`; `cargo info bevy_gooey@0.7.1` resolves. No sibling-repo commits.

### Ranked backlog

1. **The rest of the settings-screen kit**: a controls page that lists a game's actions as `binding` rows in a `list` (with a "Reset to defaults" row, and conflicts: two actions on one key), a profile name row, the skin as a choice (the presets, or a game's own, and a high-contrast preset: Game Accessibility Guidelines asks for one and the options note names it as not adopted).
2. **A name prompt**: a `dialog()` holding a `text_input` and its two choices (the owner's editor builds it by hand as `NameBox`), opening on the field with Enter as submit and Escape as `Cancel`; and a **confirm builder** if games write the same four lines each time.
3. **A safe corner**: a way for a screen to reserve the legend's corner, so a tall panel at the player's largest text size never runs under it. Now with a picture: the gallery at 1.5x text puts the legend over the Subtitles row (`dark-large.png`), and the new text-size row puts every player one step from it.
4. **Context prompts**: a legend that follows focus (a slider "Left Right Adjust", a toggle "Select Toggle", a field "Enter Done"), the way Sea of Thieves' prompts change by screen; needs one input shown as two.
5. **Text input depth and the rest of the list**: a password mask, a character filter and undo, a multi-line area; an inventory grid; narration and feedback for the controls (accesskit roles and values, a toast as a live region, `UiFeedbackKind::Adjust`); XAG 101's other text aspects (spacing, case) if a game asks.

## Iteration 21 — 2026-11-01 — a high-contrast skin, held to 7:1, and the Theme row that picks it

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey`: no open issues (empty list, no `403`/`429`). The last audit row on `loop-stats` (pass 43, 06:52Z) had `missing: -` with v0.7.1 released, so no leftover job. Parked work, looked up before picking: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` is old and not this item; `origin/loop/iter-20` is the merged iteration 20 (`git log main..origin/loop/iter-20` empty); no newer parked commit, so nothing to adopt. The corpus re-scan (`catalog_recent`, limit 200) found the same embed events as last pass (psychology and clinical papers, SIGGRAPH 2024 and 2026, software books) and no game-UI, HUD, menu, navigation or accessibility document `corpus.md` lacks, so it is unchanged. home-still: `system_status` healthy (both scribes and distill up, distill idle on CUDA); `hs distill search` answered in under 4 s. The owner's projects (Sanctuary's `src/ui/`, bevy_grimoire's `chrome.rs`) were **not** read: both shallow `gh repo clone`s ran past 300 s and were killed, and neither is checked out on this host (recorded in the note's Pending index).

**Pick and ranking.** Candidates, from the log's backlog: (1) **a high-contrast skin and the theme row**, the first slice of the backlog's "the skin as a choice ... and a high-contrast preset" and the options note's named non-adoption: Xbox's XAG 102 says "A high contrast mode ... should be provided. When enabled, the contrast ratios should equal or exceed 7:1 for all UI elements", WCAG 1.4.6 names the mechanism (G174, a control that switches to a presentation with sufficient contrast) and no game can write the third palette without finding seven more contrast pairs; (2) a controls page (binding rows in a list, reset, conflicts); (3) a name prompt and a confirm builder (a dialog, a panel, a text input and two buttons is about twelve lines today); (4) a safe corner for the legend (the gallery at 1.5x text puts it over Subtitles); (5) context prompts. The pick is (1): it is the only candidate with a published numeric bar to test against, it completes the options screen's Display page (text size, interface size, theme), and both existing presets were untouched by it. Runner-up (2): the policy for a conflict (swap, unbind, warn) is the game's, and the crate's share needs its own research pass.

**What changed** (research `c0204f0`; build `70e57f0`; test `6b7dc0a`; docs `154ba23`):
- `GooeySkin::high_contrast()`: opaque near-black panels (`#050505`), `surface_raised` `#222222`, white text and outlines, amber accent `#FFA800`, cyan ring `#33E5FF`, light red danger `#FF8080` (WCAG's own advisory: avoid red on black, for protanopia), black scrim at 0.8, on `sizes()` so a layout never moves. `GooeySkin::high_contrast_problems()` is `problems()` with every pair at `model::ENHANCED_CONTRAST` (7.0): empty for the new preset, non-empty for dark and parchment, which meet the minimums and no more (the check can fail; mutation-checked). `problems()` is unchanged.
- `skin_preset(label)`: the fourth options row, a `choice` over `SkinPreset::ALL` (Dark, Parchment, High contrast). `SkinPreset` (`#[non_exhaustive]`, `ALL`, `name`, `skin`, `of`, serde under the feature) and `GooeySkin::with_colours_of(&other)`: a player's step writes the preset's colours **and no size**, so 200% text plus high contrast is both; the row follows the skin (`SkinPreset::of`: the nearest preset by colour, looked for only when the skin changed or the row is new, so a tuned skin shows the nearest and is not rewritten) with no event. `OptionRow::SkinPreset`. Gallery: the Display page's second row is the Theme row (in place of the demo-only Window); `examples/options` has it as its fourth; `GOOEY_SKIN=high_contrast` in both.
- **A new field, found by capturing:** the first capture of the palette alone showed the tooltip over the Load game panel as a black card on a black card with a `#222` edge at 1.3:1, which is the opposite of XAG's "Add borders around text or elements". `surface_edge: Option<Color>` (the border of a panel, a tooltip and the legend; `None` is `surface_raised`, so dark and parchment are pixel-identical: `compare -metric AE` = 0 on all six regression stills) is white in high contrast. It makes `GooeySkin` one field longer: `cargo semver-checks` reports `constructible_struct_adds_field`, so the release is **0.8.0**. A button at rest still has no frame in any preset: a white one would leave the cyan ring 1.6:1 from it, hue alone (WCAG 2.4.13 asks 3:1 between a focused and an unfocused control), and the hover mix (`surface_raised` a third of the way to the accent, which white text must still read at 7:1) caps the button fill at `#222`, 1.28:1 from the panel (the dark preset's is 1.4:1). Recorded as design choices in the note's Deviations.
- Docs: `docs/research/high-contrast.md`, `docs/guide/skins.md` (the three looks and the check) and `options.md` (the fourth row), the README (two bullets and `theme.gif`), `host-api`, the widget table, the media list, AGENTS (layout, the setting rule, a rule that a new skin colour joins the 7:1 bar), the changelog, `docs/media/theme.gif` (4.3 s, 960×552, 432 KB), `high-contrast.png`, and `options.gif` (a fourth row, ends in high contrast; 6.8 s, 580 KB) and `tabs.gif` (the Display page's Window became Theme: 3 of 70 frames differ by 682 px, the rest identical under a 6% fuzz) regenerated.

**Sources** (`docs/research/high-contrast.md`): craft, Lewandowska, Dziśko, Jankowski, *Investigation the role of contrast on habituation and sensitisation effects in peripheral areas of graphical user interfaces* (stem `10.1038_s41598-022-16284-2`: "the least visible elements were located in the corners of the display, especially in the low contrast setup. Only about 3 out of 10 users saw them" and "The increase in the contrast level significantly improved the visibility of these elements"; the legend, the toasts and the tooltips are corner things); shipping, Microsoft's *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 102* (the 7:1 rule, "the true intent of high contrast modes", The Outer Worlds' "white outline around the bright red and purple meters, as well as the bright yellow text against the opaque backgrounds of HUD elements", Minecraft's and Gears Tactics' sliders, and "read the platform-provided contrast settings ... at game launch"); technical, WCAG 2.2 *Understanding* SC 1.4.6 (why 7:1: 20/80 vision; G174; "avoiding red on black") and 1.4.11 (boundaries, focus indicators), MDN *forced-colors* (an operating system's high-contrast mode is one small palette of roles, which is what a skin is), and Buitinck et al. (stem `W1728842521`) for the API (one way to do a thing, the same row shape as `text_size`). The XAG, WCAG and MDN pages are not in home-still (the note's Pending index lists them); `paper_search` was not needed: the craft paper was in the corpus.

**Checklist** (`/tmp/g21m/findings.md`, removed; high contrast 1280x720, pixels from the stills with Pillow): text 20.37:1 on the panel, 15.91:1 on a button, muted 10.2:1 and 8.0:1; accent 10.53:1 on the panel and 8.22:1 on a button, black on accent 10.88:1; focus ring `#33E6FF` 13.49:1 on the panel and 10.53:1 on a button, 3 px (6 px at 1440p) on Options and on the dialog's Stay; panel and tooltip edge white, 21:1 on the black window; the dialog's edge is 4.17:1 from the dimmed panel edge under the 0.8 scrim; hit targets, spacing and the text-in-its-box tests unchanged (44 px, 88 px at 1440p; `tests/large_text.rs` now lays the Theme row out at every 5% from 100% to 200%, and the new row alone in its panel keeps its text on one line: `High contrast` fills the choice's 8-text-size cell). Dark, parchment and dark at 1.5x text, 1280x720 and 2560x1440: **pixel-identical** to the loop's own build of `e122cb0`.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g21m/critique.md`, removed): `hc/base-1280.png`, `hc/dialog-1280.png`, `hc/dialog-2560.png`, `hc/binding-1280.png`, `hc/field-1280.png`, `hc/toast-1280.png` and `t/hc-focus2.png`: **GREAT** (white frames, amber and cyan, 7:1 from the pixels; the tooltip is a card of its own, the lit keycap, the ring and the field's caret frame read at once); `theme.gif`, `options.gif` and `tabs.gif`: **GREAT**. Flaws left: (1) buttons are dark slabs at 1.28:1 from the panel, a recorded trade, not a defect to fix without changing `hovered()` for every skin; (2) **at 1.5x text the legend, now white-framed, runs over the Subtitles row** (`hc/large-1280.png`), the same geometry as the dark preset's `dark-large.png`: **NOT YET, pre-existing**, and the player's text-size row puts every player one step from it, so it is the next pass's item; (3) `toast_alert`'s danger mark was not looked at in a capture (the gallery has no alert token): 8.36:1 by constant.

**Tests.** `tests/skin.rs` (high contrast empty under both checks and the other presets not, every reported pair naming 7:1; **every text a high-contrast screen draws, with a hovered, a pressed and a disabled button, a toggle, a slider, a choice, a binding and the legend, measured against the opaque fill under it at 7:1**; the edge of a panel, a tooltip and the legend in each preset), `tests/options.rs` (the theme row: the three looks in order, a step writing the colours, `surface_edge` included, and no size and one write, the skin a game sets moving the row with no event and a tuned skin showing the nearest and left alone, a first frame over a loaded high-contrast skin, nothing written idle), `tests/serde.rs` (the edge through RON, a file without it reads `None`, `SkinPreset` as data), `tests/large_text.rs` (the row), and the preset loops of `tabs`, `dialog_look`, `binding`, `prompts`, `text` and `controls` now run over all three presets. **Mutation-checked:** a step that copies `self.surface_edge` instead of the preset's fails the theme test; `check` ignoring its floor fails the 7:1 test; a step that replaces the whole skin (`preset.skin()`) fails the theme test on the text scale; each restored and `cmp`'d.

**mean_ms** (the gallery from `e122cb0` against this tree, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs, twice, on a host whose load average ran from 12 to 154 because other loops were building): first run minimum 23.16 against 22.90 ms (−0.25), second run minimum 22.51 against 22.62 (+0.11), paired median +0.06 ms (the pairs run from −6.1 to +12.3: noise, as the log has said since iteration 4). The six stills' own lines at load about 15: 21.36 and 21.37 (dark), 21.35 and 21.43 (parchment), 21.39 and 21.55 (1.5x text) at 1280x720; 90.4 and 90.9, 89.6 and 85.6, 89.9 and 89.4 at 2560x1440. No regression above 0.5 ms. The gallery's own Theme row only costs a flag check and, on a change, three `GooeySkin` builds.

**Gate.** One script from `~/g21/gate.sh` with `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=2`, in an `async` call: `cargo fmt --check`, clippy `--all-targets --all-features -D warnings`, `cargo doc -D warnings`, the wasm32 library build, `cargo build`, `--release`, `--examples`, `cargo test` (all green); the call hit its 3000 s limit inside `cargo test --features serde` (the host's load was 25 to 150, and every test binary relinks), so the last two steps ran again from `~/g21/gate2.sh`: clippy again (the large-text test had changed), `cargo test --features serde` (24 binaries, no failure) and `cargo test --features readme-example --test readme`, `memory.current` at most 2.9 GB, no kill. The merged tree is the gated tree.

**Process notes.** An edit that replaced a range whose last line was already outside it was auto-repaired and dropped no line (checked with `git diff main -- src/ | grep '^-'` on every file I edited), and my own `sed -n 8,14p; sed -n 16,28p` skipped a line, which looked like a lost one in `src/lib.rs`. Two `gh repo clone`s hung for 300 s each; a shallow clone of a large repository on this host is not a quick call, so the owner's projects were not read. `uv run --no-project --with pillow` is how to read pixels (Pillow is not installed system-wide).

**Merge.** `loop/iter-21` pushed and merged into `main` with `--no-ff` (`cc3ac3a`; `git diff --stat loop/iter-21 main` empty, so the merged tree is the gated tree; `origin/main` had not moved); `main` pushed.

**Release: v0.8.0**, https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.8.0 (published 2026-11-01T08:40Z, 03:40 CDT; tag `v0.8.0`, release commit `bb639dc`; the crates.io API returns the version, not yanked; `cargo info bevy_gooey@0.8.0` resolves). `cargo semver-checks check-release` (0.51.0) against 0.7.1: 202 checks, 201 pass, 1 fail: `constructible_struct_adds_field` (`GooeySkin.surface_edge`, a public field on a struct a literal can build), so the version is a minor bump before 1.0 and the changelog says what a literal that names every field needs (`surface_edge: None`). `cargo publish --dry-run` packaged 26 files (507.2 KiB, 135.6 KiB compressed), then `cargo publish`. No sibling-repo commits.

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's: `~/g21` (the gate scripts and logs, 604 KB), `~/g21_bin` (the baseline and after galleries and options examples, 726 MB) and `/tmp/g21m` (captures, GIF frames, benchmark rows, findings, critique, mutation copies: 41 MB); no worktree was made (the baseline was this clone's own release build, copied out before the next build overwrote it). `df`: `/home` 103 GB free before, 92 GB after (other loops write there too and none of it is this pass's), `/tmp` 7.7 GB free before and after; the clone's own `target/` (`~/target/gooey-loop`) is left. **Left for a person, untouched** (earlier passes'): the worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked` and `~/g17s_base` (1.8 MB each, clean, detached at `4497376` and `4859669`, both commits on this repository: the likeliest to be stale), `~/g14_bin`, `~/g17_bin`, `~/g17b_bin`, `~/g17s_bin` and `~/g19_bin` (360 to 363 MB each, binaries only), and in `/tmp` (a tmpfs, so RAM) `/tmp/g11` (1.6 GB), `/tmp/g17` (1.3 GB) and `/tmp/g19` (1.5 GB), `/tmp/gooey-gif` and `/tmp/gooey-big-build.log`; none was touched this pass and none was checked for live users.

### Ranked backlog

1. **A safe corner**: a way for a screen to reserve the legend's corner, so a tall panel at the player's largest text size never runs under it. Now louder: in high contrast the legend is a white frame over the Subtitles row at 1.5x text (`hc/large-1280.png`), the player's own text-size row puts them one step from it, and the gallery's panel is the only one that has ever been laid out against the legend by eye.
2. **The rest of the settings-screen kit**: a controls page that lists a game's actions as `binding` rows in a `list` (with a "Reset to defaults" row, and conflicts: two actions on one key), a profile name row; and a **light high-contrast preset** (XAG: "light, dark, or both") if a game asks.
3. **A name prompt**: a `dialog()` holding a `text_input` and its two choices (the owner's editor builds it by hand as `NameBox`), opening on the field with Enter as submit and Escape as `Cancel`; and a **confirm builder** if games write the same four lines each time. Read Sanctuary's `src/ui/` and bevy_grimoire's `chrome.rs` first (a `git clone --depth 1 --filter=blob:none` of each, in the background).
4. **Context prompts**: a legend that follows focus (a slider "Left Right Adjust", a toggle "Select Toggle", a field "Enter Done"), the way Sea of Thieves' prompts change by screen; needs one input shown as two.
5. **Text input depth and the rest of the list**: a password mask, a character filter and undo, a multi-line area; an inventory grid; narration and feedback for the controls (accesskit roles and values, a toast as a live region, `UiFeedbackKind::Adjust`); XAG 101's other text aspects (spacing, case) if a game asks; a capture of `toast_alert` in high contrast.

## Iteration 22 — 2026-11-01 — SkinScope: a subtree carries its own skin (G-01, #5)

**Triage.** `gh issue list` on `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey`: 28 open, all `grimoire-ui` (G-01 to G-28), none a `bug`. The owner's rule of 2026-11-01 makes the lowest-numbered one whose `Depends on` is closed the pass's item: **#5 G-01, scoped skins** (`Depends on: none`). Skipped, by that rule and not blocked: #6 to #32 (G-02 to G-28), each the next pass's item in order. The last audit row on `loop-stats` (pass 44, 08:41Z, v0.8.0) had `missing: -`. Parked work looked up first: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` is old and not this item, `origin/loop/iter-21` is merged; nothing to adopt. Corpus re-scan (`catalog_recent`, limit 200): psychology, couples, clinical and SIGGRAPH 2026 embeds and no game-UI, HUD, navigation or accessibility document `corpus.md` lacks, so it is unchanged. home-still answered `distill_search` (nothing on theming or design tokens).

**What changed** (research `87f64e2`; build `09629f2`; docs and demo `aa2f5d5`):
- `SkinScope(GooeySkin)`, a public component on any node. That node and everything below it is styled from it and not from the `GooeySkin` resource; the nearest scope at or above a widget wins (its own included), a nested scope shadows the one above, none is the resource. It is the whole skin, `text_scale` and sizes included; `GooeyFonts` stays global. The editor's name is nowhere in the API.
- `apply_skin` (one system, no new one): `look_of` resolves the skin per entity (the walk up `ChildOf` is skipped while no scope exists, `any_scope`); a `Changed<SkinScope>`, a `RemovedComponents<SkinScope>`, a `Changed<ChildOf>` (a spawn or a move) and a `RemovedComponents<ChildOf>` (a widget taken out of its parent) dirty the node's whole subtree (`iter_descendants`), the last two only while a scope exists; a changed resource restyles every widget, each from its own skin, so a scoped widget does not change. A scope that fails `problems()` warns (`scoped skin: …`) and is applied. The six `RemovedComponents` readers became one `Removals` tuple parameter because the system hit Bevy's 16-parameter limit with two more.
- `examples/scope.rs`: the options panel (Text size, Theme, Subtitles, Volume, Play) beside a Preview panel under `SkinScope(GooeySkin::parchment())`; Enter on the Preview's button removes the scope. `docs/media/scope.gif` (4.3 s, 960×540, 295 KB, 65 frames) and the README caption, `docs/guide/skins.md` ("A skin for one subtree"), `host-api.md`, `docs/media/README.md`, AGENTS, the changelog.

**Ranking.** Pick: #5, by the owner's rule (it is the lowest open `grimoire-ui` request with nothing it depends on, and a prerequisite of G-03's tokens and of every preview the editor draws). Runner-up: #6 G-02 compact density, next pass.

**Sources** (`docs/research/skin-scope.md`): craft, Wagemans et al. 2012 (stem `10.1037_a0029333`: "all else being equal, the most similar elements ... tend to be grouped together"; a region in one skin reads as one thing); shipping, Microsoft's *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 101* ("Any adjustable text settings ... should carry over to all important text—this includes additional windows that overlay a UI": the reason a scope is explicit, and a deviation noted); technical, MDN *Using CSS custom properties* (a custom property "inherit[s] its value from [its] parent" and the declaring selector "defines the scope"), and scikit-learn's API design (`W1728842521`: one way to do a thing).

**Tests.** `tests/scope.rs`, 8: two scopes give two buttons their own accent and removing one restores the resource in the same frame; the nearest scope wins, a removed inner scope lets the outer show through and one added to a settled tree restyles; a widget may carry its own scope; a changed scope restyles its subtree and a sibling outside it keeps a sentinel fill; a changed resource restyles the unscoped and leaves a scoped button and label alone; a widget moved into and out of a scope (`add_child`, then `remove::<ChildOf>`; the second failed first, which found that `Changed<ChildOf>` does not fire on a removal); a scope's `text_scale` and `min_target` apply; thirty idle frames rewrite nothing. Mutation: dropping the `RemovedComponents<SkinScope>` read fails two of them.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g22/after/scope-dark.png`, `scope-hc.png`, the frames of `scope.gif`): the Preview stays cream and brown at 100% text while Options goes high contrast at 150%; its ring is blue (its own skin's) and the left one cyan; after Enter the Preview takes the global high-contrast look and 150% text with a cyan ring, in one frame. Flaws: the parchment Play button at rest is a pale slab on cream (parchment's own button, 1.28:1 as in `dark`, the recorded trade of iteration 21, not this change's); the two panels top-align but differ in height (layout, correct). **GREAT** for the feature. The three dark, parchment and 1.5x-text gallery stills at 1280x720: `compare -metric AE` 0 against the baseline (`3ae9eae`), so nothing outside a scope moved.

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `3ae9eae`, 12 alternating pairs, Xvfb): minimum 21.86 before and 21.75 after, medians 26.33 and 26.25, paired median **−0.03 ms**. The three stills' own lines: 27.31, 26.51, 26.03 before and 26.51, 27.00, 27.45 after (noise ±1 ms). No regression above 0.5 ms; with no scope the added cost is one empty-query check and `Changed<ChildOf>` and `RemovedComponents<ChildOf>` reads that are skipped.

**Assumed decisions.** (1) A scope carries the whole `GooeySkin` (options: colours only, whole skin; whole skin picked: the issue's own proposal and the only way a preview at another size works); a scope does not inherit the player's `text_scale`, which is XAG 101's caveat, recorded under the note's Deviations. (2) The toast stack and the legend are roots and follow the resource unless a scope is put on them (documented).

**Remaining items for the next pass:** #6 G-02 compact density for desktop tools, #7 G-03 design tokens, #8 G-04 anchored popover, then #9 G-05 menu bar. Also the earlier backlog: a safe corner for the legend, the controls page, a name prompt.

**Gate.** One script, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=2`, on the merged tree: `cargo fmt --check` (failed the first time on `tests/scope.rs`; `cargo fmt` and a commit, then the whole gate again), clippy `--all-targets --all-features -D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features` (25 `test result: ok` lines, no failure, doctests included: the `SkinScope` example compiles), `cargo check --lib`, the wasm32 library check and `cargo doc --no-deps -D warnings`, 2.6 minutes with a warm cache.

**Merge and release.** `loop/iter-22` pushed and merged `--no-ff` into `main`; `main` pushed. **Release: v0.9.0**, https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.9.0 (tag `v0.9.0`; `cargo info bevy_gooey@0.9.0` resolves). `cargo semver-checks check-release` reported one break, `apply_skin` now takes 13 parameters instead of 14 (the removal readers are one tuple, so the system's own signature changed), which is a minor bump before 1.0; the changelog says a host that adds it with `add_systems` is unchanged. Issue #5 closed with the tests as evidence; bevy_grimoire#211, the only issue naming G-01, got the "move the pin" comment.

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's `~/g22_base` (the baseline worktree, plain `git worktree remove`), `~/g22_bin` (both galleries, the perf and gate scripts and logs) and `/tmp/g22` (captures, GIF frames, montage): no build directory of its own, the baseline built into the loop's shared target. `df` `/home` 356 GB free before, 352 GB after. **Left for a person, untouched** (earlier passes'): the worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked` and `~/g17s_base` (1.8 MB each, clean, detached at `4497376` and `4859669`, both commits on this repository: the likeliest to be stale).

## Iteration 23 — 2026-11-01 — Compact density and the Tool look (G-02, #6)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 27 open, all `grimoire-ui` (G-02 to G-28), none a `bug`. The owner's rule of 2026-11-01 makes the lowest-numbered one whose `Depends on` is closed the pass's item: **#6 G-02, compact density** (`Depends on: none`). Skipped, by that rule and not blocked: #7 to #32 (G-03 to G-28), each the next pass's item in order. The last audit row on `loop-stats` (pass 50, 13:40Z, v0.9.0) had `missing: -`. Parked work looked up first: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is a day old and not this item; nothing to adopt. Corpus re-scan: not repeated (iteration 22 found no new game-UI document; `catalog_recent` unchanged in kind).

**What changed** (research `00d0bf9`; build `62c9925`; docs and demo `7a741ef`):
- `Density { Comfortable, Compact }` (`#[non_exhaustive]`, serde, Reflect) and `GooeySkin::density`. `GooeySkin::pad()` (half of `padding` when compact) and `GooeySkin::target()` (`min_target` capped at `MIN_TARGET_PX` when compact) are what `apply_skin`, the tooltip's placement and the text field's inset read: every `skin.padding` and `skin.min_target` there became `skin.pad()` and `skin.target()`, and `problems()` checks `target()`, so a compact skin with `min_target: 20` still reports `TargetTooSmall { px: 20 }` while 44 is capped to 24 and passes.
- `GooeySkin::tool()` and `SkinPreset::Tool` (fourth in `SkinPreset::ALL`, so the theme row has four options): a cool neutral grey with a blue accent, compact, 14 px text, gap 6, padding 10, radius 4, border 2, meter 10. `problems()` empty. A theme step onto it writes colours only.
- **A bug found by the new test, fixed with it:** at 200% text in a 24 px-target skin a choice's turned chevron stood 1.7 px outside its arrow (`ChoiceArrow -> ChoiceBack/Forward`). The arrow is now `max(target(), chevron × CHEVRON_ARROW_FIT)` wide (`1 + 1/√2` chevrons: the half plus the ink shift). Default skin unchanged (its 44 px arrow already holds it): the dark, parchment and 1.5x-text stills are `compare -metric AE` 0 against the baseline.
- `examples/density.rs` (an Inspector panel with a Compact switch and the Theme row), `GOOEY_SKIN=tool` in gallery and options, `docs/media/density.gif` (3.9 s, 560×560, 58 frames, 171 KB) and `tool.png` (600×304), the README bullet and caption, "Compact density for a tool" in `docs/guide/skins.md`, host-api, options, widgets, media list, AGENTS, changelog.

**Ranking.** Pick: #6, by the owner's rule (lowest open `grimoire-ui` request with nothing it depends on; G-03's tokens and every dense editor panel build on it). Runner-up: #7 G-03 design tokens, next pass.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; also in the commit body):
1. `SkinPreset::Tool` has its **own palette** (grey and blue) and compact sizes, not Dark's colours. Options: Dark colours plus compact (then `SkinPreset::of`, which picks the nearest preset by colour, could never show it and a theme step could not tell Dark from Tool), or its own palette (picked: the reachable one, and an editor's blue is not a game's amber).
2. A theme step writes colours only, so Tool does not set density or sizes from the Theme row; density is the player's or the host's (`GooeySkin::tool()` inserted whole is the full look). Consistent with the existing "no size" rule.
3. Compact **caps** `min_target` at 24 (not replaces it): a `min_target` already under 24 is still refused.
4. **Assumed owner exception, additive:** `tests/options.rs`'s theme-row test (`…offers_the_three_looks…`) became `…offers_the_four_looks…`: Tool is stepped to after High contrast and the end-of-row check (nothing written, no event) now follows Tool, with the change count raised from 2 to 3. Nothing was loosened.

**Sources** (`docs/research/density.md`): craft, Cockburn et al. 2014 *Supporting Novice to Expert Transitions in User Interfaces* (stem `10.1145_2659796`: "interfaces explicitly designed for experts (e.g., keyboard shortcuts or command-line interaction) allow high levels of performance but only after extensive training"); shipping, Microsoft *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 101* ("**PC/VR:** Font size should equal or exceed: 18 px at 1080p": Tool's 14 px at the 720p reference is 21 px at 1080p); technical, W3C *Understanding SC 2.5.8 Target Size (Minimum)* (24 px, and its own note "provide a mechanism to control the density of layout and thereby change target size or spacing") and the scikit-learn API paper (`W1728842521`, one field, comfortable default).

**Tests.** `tests/density.rs`, 5: a compact button is at least 24 px and shorter than a comfortable one (44 px, with the panel's padding halved) and the resource's `density` restyles live; the floor is still checked (Tool empty, a compact 44 and 30 capped to 24, 20 refused, a comfortable 20 refused as before); Tool is a preset the theme row can show (`SkinPreset::of` finds every preset, every preset's `problems()` empty, a theme step keeps density and text scale); Tool holds every widget inside its box and every button, toggle, choice, binding, tab and text field at least 24 px high at 1x, 1.25x, 1.5x and 2x text (**found the chevron bug**: failed first at 2x with four 1.7 px escapes); a Tool panel of everything is under 0.75 of the dark one's height. `tests/serde.rs` (density through RON, a file without it comfortable, `density: Compact` alone gives a 24 px target), `tests/options.rs` (the four looks). Mutation: `COMPACT_PADDING` 0.5 to 1.0 failed 2 of 5 (the button and the stack); restored and `cmp` clean.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g23m/after/tool.png`, `tool-zoom.png`, `density.gif` frames): the Tool gallery is crisp at 1280x720 and reads as an inspector, not a shrunk menu: 2 px frames, buttons 24 px, blue Audio tab with dark text 6.7:1, the ring 9.8:1 on the panel (7.3:1 on a button), Volume slider and switch aligned. In `density.gif` Compact on shrinks the panel at once from 528 to 456 px (same 20 px text), the rows keep one rhythm, the ring and keys are unchanged, Theme steps recolour without moving a pixel. Flaws: (1) Compact alone at 20 px text is only 14% shorter: the text, not the padding, is most of a row, and the real saving is in the Tool preset's 14 px text (the gallery panel 520 to 294 px), which the README caption says in numbers; (2) the Tool accent against a button is 4.35:1, a recorded trade (above 3:1); (3) the heading sits 5 px from the panel's top, tight but one scale. Verdicts: `tool.png` **GREAT**; `density.gif` **GREAT**.

**Checklist** (Tool, 1280x720, Pillow and the model's maths): text on surface 13.7:1, on a button 10.3:1, muted on surface 6.6:1, accent on a button 4.36:1, text on accent 6.7:1, ring 9.8:1; hit targets: every control at least 24 px at 1x to 2x text (test), 44 px unchanged in the other presets; spacing: one scale (gap 6, padding 5 drawn); nothing leaves its box at 1x to 2x text (test); `problems()` empty for all four presets.

**mean_ms** (gallery from `2fdfe02` against this tree, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs): minimum 22.19 before and 21.84 after, median 26.20 and 26.51: −0.35 and +0.32 ms, noise. The three stills' own lines: 43.97, 34.40, 35.50 ms before (load from the baseline build) and 21.63, 21.85, 21.63 after; Tool 22.04. No regression above 0.5 ms; the added cost is a `match` on a `Copy` enum per `pad()` and `target()` call.

**Gate.** One script, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=2`, on the tree: `cargo fmt --check`, clippy `--all-targets --all-features -D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features` (all green, doctests included: the `Density` example compiles), `cargo check --lib`, the wasm32 library check and `cargo doc --no-deps -D warnings`, 2.2 minutes with a warm cache.

### Ranked backlog

1. **#7 G-03 design tokens** in the skin (spacing, radius, type scale, surface ladder, `ElevationStep`): builds on `pad()`/`target()` being the one read of spacing.
2. #8 G-04 anchored popover, then #9 G-05 menu bar.

4. A controls page (a list of `binding` rows with Reset and conflicts); a name prompt dialog.

The clone's `target/` (`~/target/gooey-loop`) is left. **Left for a person, untouched** (earlier passes'): the worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked` and `~/g17s_base` (clean, detached at `4497376` and `4859669`, both on this repository).

## Iteration 24 — 2026-11-01 — Design tokens and a measured surface ladder (G-03, #7)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 26 open, all `grimoire-ui` (G-03 to G-28), none a `bug`. The owner's rule of 2026-11-01 makes the lowest-numbered one whose `Depends on` is closed the pass's item: **#7 G-03, design tokens** (`Depends on: none`). Skipped by that rule, not blocked: #8 to #32 (G-04 to G-28), each the next pass's item in order. The last audit row on `loop-stats` (pass 51, 14:03Z, v0.10.0) had `missing: -`. Parked work looked up first: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is two days old and not this item; nothing to adopt. Corpus re-scan: `catalog_recent` shows no new game-UI document; not repeated.

**What changed** (research `1a847c0`; build `64777ca`; docs and demo `8e89c40`):
- `GooeySkin` gains `spacing: [f32; 6]`, `radii: [f32; 3]`, `type_scale: [f32; 5]` and `surfaces: Vec<Color>` (lowest first, five in every preset), read with `space(step)`, `corner(step)`, `type_size(step)` (× `text_scale`) and `surface_at(level)`; a step past the end is the last. Each preset's own `gap`, `padding`, `radius`, `text_size` and `heading_size` are entries of its own scales (Tool's are tighter), held by a test.
- `Role::Surface(u8)` and `surface(level)`: a column filled with one step of the ladder and nothing else (padding and corners are the host's, from the tokens; a bundle cannot carry two `Node`s, so a padded one is `(Role::Surface(n), Node{..})`, which the guide shows).
- `SkinProblem::ElevationStep { lower, upper, delta_l }` for adjacent surfaces under `model::ELEVATION_STEP` (CIE L\* 2.0), `SkinProblem::BadScale { field }` for an empty ladder or a falling, negative or non-finite scale, `model::cie_lightness` (re-exported). `with_colours_of` and `colour_distance` carry the ladder, so a theme step brings it and keeps the player's scales.
- `examples/ladder.rs` (four nested `Role::Surface` cards on the skin's tokens, each labelled with its L\* and step, the Theme row in the innermost, capture knobs), `docs/research/tokens.md`, "Design tokens and the surface ladder" in `docs/guide/skins.md`, the `surface` row in the widgets table, the README bullet and caption, `docs/media/ladder.gif` (3.7 s, 660×400, 55 frames, 194 KB) and its row in `docs/media/README.md`, AGENTS, changelog.

**Ranking.** Pick: #7, by the owner's rule (lowest open `grimoire-ui` request with nothing it depends on; the surface ladder is what G-04's popover, G-05's menus and every later panel will stand on). Runner-up: #8 G-04 anchored popover, next pass.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; also in the commit body and on the issue):
1. The issue's `radius: [f32; 3]` cannot sit beside the existing `radius: f32` (the corner every widget draws), so the scale is **`radii`**. Options: rename the existing field (breaks every skin file and literal), or `radii` (picked: additive).
2. `surfaces` is **`Vec<Color>`**, not `Vec<Srgba>`: every other colour of the skin is a `Color`, and `with_colours_of` and the serde form are then the same as for them.
3. The crate's own widgets do not read the tokens (their gap, padding and radius stay the existing fields; a test keeps each preset's values on its scales). `Role::Surface` is the one place the crate draws from them. The tokens are the host's vocabulary, which is what the issue asks for.
4. The ladder check has no 7:1 floor in `high_contrast_problems()`: high contrast separates surfaces by frame, and the check is about fills telling apart, not text. High contrast's step 4 holds text at 6.95:1, which the guide says (a host that puts text on a step checks that pair).

**Sources** (`docs/research/tokens.md`): craft, Wagemans et al. 2012 (stem `10.1037_a0029333`: "all else being equal, the most similar elements ... tend to be grouped together"); shipping, Material 3's tonal surfaces ("Material 3 represents elevation mainly using tonal color overlays. This is a new way to differentiate containers and surfaces from each other", five surface-container roles; fetched by `web_search`, not in home-still) and bevy_grimoire's `chrome.rs` ("`VOID`→`PANEL_BG` was ΔL\* 1.60 — below the JND"); technical, Marschner and Shirley's CIELAB chapter (stem `book_fundamentals_computer_graphics_marschner`: "a perceptually more or less uniform color space, useful, among other things, for the computation of color differences") and the scikit-learn API paper (`W1728842521`). Pending index: the W3C Design Tokens Format Module, Material 3 colour roles, Apple HIG materials, a lightness-JND paper.

**Tests.** `tests/tokens.rs`, 12 (plus one in `tests/serde.rs`): L\* of white, black, mid grey and a near-black (the linear segment) and a monotone ramp; two surfaces 1.6 apart report `ElevationStep { lower: 0, upper: 1 }` with that delta, 2.2, 4 and 30 apart do not; only adjacent pairs compared and a step down valid; one surface valid, none `BadScale`; a NaN surface reported; every preset's steps at least L\* 4 apart with `problems()` empty and `high_contrast_problems()` empty for high contrast; each preset's own sizes on its scales; a step past the end the last and `type_size` × `text_scale`; falling, NaN and zero scales, with zero a fine first step of spacing and corners; a theme step carrying the ladder and keeping the scales; `Surface(n)` in a headless window (fill by level, a level past the top, a new skin restyling the unscoped and a `SkinScope` keeping its own ladder, nothing but the fill drawn); the serde round trip and a file without the fields reading the dark ladder. Mutation: `ELEVATION_STEP` 1.0 fails the 1.6 test, dropping `surfaces` from `with_colours_of` fails the theme-step test; both restored and `cmp`-checked against the copies.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g24/after/ladder-{dark,parchment,high_contrast,tool}.png`, the frames of `ladder.gif`): in all four looks each card is plainly a step above the one it sits in; the drawn fills equal the defined ones (a pixel sample of Surface 3 in dark is `#333845`, the ladder's (0.20, 0.22, 0.27)); labels hold 14.0, 12.1, 10.2 and 7.7:1 on steps 1 to 4 in dark, 5.2, 8.2, 10.7 and 12.7 in parchment, 17.9, 14.8, 10.8 and 6.95 in high contrast, 13.7, 12.0, 10.3 and 7.8 in Tool; the ring on the Theme row shows in each look after a key; stepping Theme restyles the ladder in one frame (no tint, no flash in the GIF). Flaws: (1) dark's ladder is quiet (steps of L\* 5 to 6, above grimoire's "comfortable 4", but a washed-out monitor will lose step 1 over the ground); (2) parchment's step 0 is the window's brown, no ground for text, said in the guide; (3) high contrast's step 4 is 6.95:1 for text, said in the guide; (4) the "+x over y" in a label repeats what the colours show, but it is the measurement the check makes. **GREAT** for the feature, and no existing widget changes.

**Checklist** (1280x720, 2560x1440, dark, parchment, dark 1.5x, dialog): the eight gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`3b20d37`): no widget reads a token. `problems()` empty for all four presets; adjacent steps (L\*) dark 6.5, 12.9, 18.3, 23.6, 31.6, parchment 14.0, 63.0, 77.5, 87.1, 93.5, high contrast 0, 8, 16, 26, 38, Tool 5.9, 10.9, 16.3, 21.3, 29.4; hit targets unchanged (44 px, Tool 24).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `3b20d37`, 12 alternating pairs, Xvfb, host load 2 to 8): run 1 (before first) minimum 22.07 before and 21.81 after, medians 23.48 and 24.56, paired median +0.53 ms; run 2 (after first) minimum 21.73 and 21.78, medians 23.83 and 23.46, paired median +0.42; a null run of the same binary twice gave medians 24.78 and 25.45 (+0.67) and paired median −0.03. So the medians differ by about the noise between two copies of one binary, and the minimum, the least-noise estimate, by −0.26 and +0.05 ms. ## Iteration 25 — 2026-11-01 — Anchored popover (G-04, #8)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 25 open, all `grimoire-ui` (G-04 to G-28), none a `bug`. The owner's rule of 2026-11-01 makes the lowest-numbered one whose `Depends on` is closed the pass's item: **#8 G-04, the anchored popover primitive** (`Depends on: none`). Skipped by that rule, not blocked: #9 to #32 (G-05 to G-28), each the next pass's item in order. The last audit row on `loop-stats` (pass 52, 14:34Z, v0.11.0) had `missing: -`. Parked work looked up first: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is two days old and not this item; nothing to adopt. Corpus re-scan: no new game-UI document; not repeated. home-still: `distill_search` answered (CLI).

**What changed** (research `636ae3a`; build `dbda02e`; docs and demo `d3d67b2`):
- `popover(anchor, placement)` and `popover_modal(anchor, placement)`: one bundle for a panel that is a child of its anchor, placed by Bevy's own `Popover` from a candidate list (the preferred side aligned to the anchor's start, end and centre, then the opposite side the same three), so it flips and slides at the window's edge and is placed again when the window is resized. `Placement { Below, Above, Right, Left, AtPointer }`; `AtPointer` hangs the panel from a zero-size `FixedNode` (`PopoverPoint`) the crate puts at the pointer (over `UiScale`).
- Closing: `PopoverClosed { entity, anchor, reason }` and `CloseReason::{Outside, Cancel}`, then the crate despawns it. Outside is a `PointerInput` press whose `HoverMap` hits are not the panel (nor, for a side placement, the anchor); a panel is armed a frame after it appears, so the press that opened it is never outside. Cancel is Escape or the pad's B: a modal popover is a `Dialog` and a modal `TabGroup` (no second focus scope, so focus-in, Tab trap and focus-back are the dialog's), and a non-modal one is reached by `FocusScopes::popover_of` (focus on its anchor or inside it). A press on the panel stops at it (`stop_press`, `stop_release`, `stop_click`, `stop_acquire`), so it never presses its anchor, a `Button` that handles any press bubbling up to it.
- `Role::Popover` in `apply_skin`: an opaque `solid_surface` card framed in `outline`, padded like a panel, the tooltip's shadow, `max_width` the window less two gaps, `GlobalZIndex(150)` (over a dialog, under a tooltip). The panel is a `FixedNode`: as an absolute child of a narrow button its auto width fitted the button and wrapped a label to a word (measured 142 × 363 px), and no scroll clip reaches it. It is born `Display::None` and hidden, shown the frame after `apply_skin` styles it (`reveal_popovers`): Bevy measures new text a frame late, and the first frame drew a 34 px card (found in the GIF's contact sheet, a failing-first test, mutation-checked).
- `examples/popover` (menu bar, Edit at the window's edge, a right-click context menu, the toggle on the anchor's `Activate`), `docs/research/popover.md`, `docs/guide/popovers.md`, the widgets table, the host API table, the README bullet (the toast one, extended) and caption, `docs/media/popover.gif` (4.6 s, 960×540, 81 KB) and its row, changelog, AGENTS.

**Ranking.** Pick: #8, by the owner's rule (lowest open `grimoire-ui` request with nothing it depends on; G-05 menus, G-06 context menus, G-16 select and G-07 palette all stand on it). Runner-up: #9 G-05 menu bar and dropdown menus, next pass.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; also in the commit body and on the issue):
1. The issue's `popover(anchor, placement)` plus `modal: bool` is **two functions**, `popover` and `popover_modal`: a `bool` argument reads as `popover(a, p, true)` at the call.
2. The crate despawns the popover on a close request (the issue asks for it, where `Cancel` for a dialog only reports), after one `PopoverClosed`; a game's own despawn and an anchor's send nothing.
3. A press on a side placement's anchor is not outside, so the anchor's own `Activate` toggles with one rule (the example's `toggle`); for `AtPointer` the anchor is a region and a press on it closes.
4. Bevy's `Popover` never clamps: "shifts" is the start, end and centre candidates, and a panel no side can hold is clipped, which the host avoids by capping content. Overlapping the anchor to fit was not built.

**Sources** (`docs/research/popover.md`): craft, Lakier et al. 2024 (stem `10.1109_tvcg.2024.3420236`: "sometimes I would bring up the menu and there would be things a few inches from my face" and "This can be beyond arms reach if the menu is first invoked too far from the body"); shipping, XAG 112 on *Halo Infinite* ("cursor focus is automatically brought to the area...", stem in `dialog.md`) and Doucet on *Defender's Quest DX* (`focus.md`); technical, Floating UI's *flip* and *shift* (fetched, quoted) and the WAI-ARIA APG *Menu Button* and *Menu and Menubar* patterns ("Escape: Close the menu that contains focus and return focus to the element or context ... from which the menu was opened"; fetched), with the scikit-learn API paper (`W1728842521`). Pending index: Floating UI, the APG menu patterns, Material 3's *Menus*.

**Tests.** `tests/popover.rs`, 20, each in the headless window with real `PointerInput` and `KeyboardInput`: the acceptance (Right at the right edge flips inside the window, one press outside closes it once with one `PopoverClosed`), each side on its side aligned to the anchor's edge and never touching it, flipped above at the bottom, slid to the anchor's end, `AtPointer` at four positions (flips at the right and bottom edges), the point removed with its popover, a press on the panel closing nothing and never activating or pressing the anchor (mutation: removing the stop observers gives `["Edit", "Edit"]`), a button in the panel activating, a press on the anchor of a side popover not outside, the opening press not outside (mutation: arming at spawn fails it), a despawned anchor taking the popover with it and three window sizes placing it again, nested popovers (inside both, inside the outer only, outside both), a modal one taking focus, keeping it under four arrows, one `Cancel` on Escape and focus back on the anchor, a non-modal one leaving focus and closing on Escape from the anchor, Escape from an unrelated widget closing nothing, the card's display, fill, edge and text contrast in three presets, z order against a dialog and a tooltip, thirty idle frames writing nothing, 3 scales × 4 sides × 5 anchor places inside the window, and the first frames (mutation: dropping `Visibility::Hidden` shows frame 0 at 34 px wide, 600 px from its place).

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g25/after/pop-{menu,edit,context,away}.png`, `pop-menu-{parchment,high_contrast,tool}.png`, `pop-edit-2x.png`, `pop-context-1440.png`, the frames of `popover.gif`): the card sits a gap and a frame (10 px) under File with its left edge on the button's (x 19 both), the ring on the first item; Edit's menu ends at the button's right edge (1261 of 1280); the context menu's corner is at the pointer at 720p and at 1440p (UiScale 2); at 2x text the Edit menu fits. Contrasts from pixels: dark edge 3.68:1 on the card, text 10.3:1; parchment 3.81 and 8.2; high contrast 20.4. Flaws: (1) dark's card is almost the canvas's colour (29,32,40 against 32,35,43): it separates by its frame and a faint shadow, not by lift, as the dialog and the tooltip do; a tonal step above the ground is a design change for all three, logged; (2) the frame is the brightest thing on the card (the dialog's `outline`); (3) the `AtPointer` corner has no gap from the cursor. Verdict: **GREAT** for the popover (side, flip, slide, pointer, modal and not).

**Checklist** (1280x720, 2560x1440, dark, parchment, dark 1.5x): the six gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`9a7dcbd`): no existing widget changed. `problems()` unchanged (no new colour). Hit targets unchanged (44 px, Tool 24).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `9a7dcbd`, Xvfb, host load 6 to 9): three sets of alternating pairs: (12 pairs, before first) minimum 22.03 before and 21.43 after, medians 22.83 and 23.82, paired median +0.80; (12, after first) minimum 21.77 and 22.23, medians 23.19 and 24.28, paired +0.39; (16, before first) minimum 21.41 and 21.75, medians 24.49 and 24.67, paired +0.42; a null run (the baseline against a byte copy of itself, 12 pairs) paired median +0.09. The paired medians are +0.4 to +0.8 and the minimums −0.6 to +0.5, which is the spread two copies of one binary have shown here before (+0.67 in iteration 24), and the added work is three `O(1)` systems measured in isolation (`run_system` ×20 000 in a debug test, throwaway): 3.8, 3.1 and 1.9 µs, about 0.01 ms a frame. Not a regression above 0.5 ms that I can attribute to the change; logged as noise with that bound.

## Iteration 26 — 2026-11-01 — Shortcuts (G-08, #12)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 25 open, all `grimoire-ui` (G-05 to G-29), none a `bug`. The last audit row (pass 53) had nothing in `missing`. The owner's rule picks the lowest-numbered request whose `Depends on` is all closed: #9 G-05 (menu bar) depends on #8 (closed) and **#12** (open), so it waits; **#12 G-08, shortcuts**, depends on nothing and is the lowest such (#13, #16, #19, #21 and the rest of the independent ones are higher).
