**What changed** (research `eb129fd`, `b44876a`; build `ed23705`; docs and demo `d6818fd`, `95747ad`):
- `Shortcut { modifiers, key }`, `Modifiers { ctrl, shift, alt, meta }` (physical keys, either side; `|`; `Modifiers::PRIMARY`, `Shortcut::primary`), `Platform`. `Shortcut::label()` is the platform's words ("Ctrl+Shift+P" in Microsoft's order, "⌃⇧P" in Apple's with glyphs for Return, Delete, Escape, Tab and the arrows), `label_on(Platform)` for a test or an editor, `just_pressed(&ButtonInput<KeyCode>)` the one rule for a chord that went down with exactly its modifiers, `is_valid()`; the arithmetic is engine-free in `model` (`shortcut_label`, `is_modifier_key`, and `shortcut_conflicts`, generic over `PartialEq`).
- `Binding::Chord(Shortcut)`: a `binding` row spawned with a chord captures one (waits through a bare modifier, binds the first non-modifier key with the modifiers held, Escape / a mouse button / a pad button exit; a key row still binds a modifier alone). `BindingSettings::chord_prompt`; a `Prompt` names a chord on its keycap too.
- `Tooltip { text, shortcut }`, `Tooltip::new(..).with_shortcut(..)`: the chord on the prompts' keycap right of the line, spawned and removed as the field changes; the box is never narrower than the cap plus a gap and six text sizes (the first 200% test caught a 596 px cap in a 531 px box; removing the floor fails it).
- `examples/shortcuts`, `docs/research/shortcut.md`, `docs/guide/shortcuts.md`, `docs/media/shortcuts.gif`, README bullet and caption, `tests/shortcut.rs` (19 tests), AGENTS.md notes.

**Ranking.** Pick: #12, by the owner's rule (lowest open `grimoire-ui` request with nothing open it depends on; #9 menus, #11 palette, #20 toolbar and #30 overlay all stand on it). Runner-up: #13 G-09 split panes (the next independent one).

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; in the commit body and on the issue): (1) `model::shortcut_conflicts` is generic over `PartialEq` (the issue's call compiles unchanged) so `model` holds no engine type; (2) "Cmd and Ctrl map to the platform's primary key" is `Modifiers::PRIMARY`, not a rule that treats a held Ctrl as a held Cmd: a mac player's Control-S and Command-S are two chords; (3) `Tooltip(String)` became `Tooltip { text, shortcut }` (the issue's `.with_shortcut` needs a field), breaking, so a minor bump; (4) a chord row exits on a mouse press, since a mouse button is no part of a shortcut.

**Sources** (`docs/research/shortcut.md`): craft, Malacria et al., *Promoting Hotkey use through rehearsal with ExposeHK* (stem `10.1145_2470654.2470735`: "Despite this potential, hotkeys are under-used" and the "performance dip" that discourages them) with Cockburn et al., *Supporting Novice to Expert Transitions* (`10.1145_2659796`); shipping, XAG 107 (remapping on *Grounded* and *Gears 5*: "assign an action to all potential game inputs", the new mapping "represented correctly across any hints"); technical, Apple's *HIG: Keyboards* ("always list them in this order: Control, Option, Shift, Command") and Microsoft's Win32 *Keyboard* UX guide ("Present modifier keys in the following order: Windows logo, Application, Ctrl, Alt, Shift"), plus the scikit-learn API paper (`W1728842521`). Pending index: Apple's and Microsoft's pages are not papers; listed in the note.

**Tests.** `tests/shortcut.rs`, 19: labels in both orders, `primary` per platform, a modifier is not a shortcut, conflicts (one pair, none for Ctrl+S against Ctrl+Shift+S, three on one chord are three pairs, none for one), `just_pressed` exactness (either Ctrl, an extra Shift fails, S alone fails, a held key is not just pressed), a chord row binding Ctrl+Shift+K a key at a time and in one frame (one `ValueChange`, the keycap says it, focus stays), a bare key as a chord with no modifier, Alt let go before K, the same chord changing nothing, Escape with Ctrl held exits and sends no `Cancel`, a click exits, a key row still binding Shift alone, Ctrl+ArrowDown bound and moving no focus, a prompt naming a chord, the tooltip's cap beside its line inside the box on one centre line, gaining and losing the cap by editing in place with the box unchanged, 200% text with a Ctrl+Shift+PageDown cap inside the box and window (mutation-checked), nothing written while idle.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g26m/critique.md`; evidence `after/final.png`, `s_parchment.png`, `s_high_contrast.png`, `s_2x.png`, the GIF frames): the cap in the tooltip is the prompts' cap in dark, parchment and high contrast, centred on the line within 1.5 px, the box inside the window; the conflict line first widened the panel by 40 px and was shortened to "Conflict: A and B". Open: at 200% text in a 720p window the Save tooltip, with no room beside a 1050 px panel, lands over the panel's own labels (the pre-existing placement for a tooltip no side can hold; the cap is whole). Verdict: **GREAT** at 1x in all three looks; the 200% over-the-panel placement is NOT YET, in the backlog.

**Checklist** (1280x720, 2560x1440, dark, parchment, high contrast, dark 1.5x, `GOOEY_KEYS=down` so Options' tooltip is open): the five gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`f81fd74`): no existing look changed, tooltips included. `problems()` unchanged (no new colour; the cap is a `PromptKey`). Hit targets unchanged (44 px).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `f81fd74`, Xvfb, load 4 to 5, 10 alternating pairs): minimum 21.84 before and 21.83 after, median 22.80 and 22.68, maxima 26.10 and 25.33: no change. (A first single run per look differed by up to 6 ms either way: noise.)

## Iteration 27 — 2026-11-01 — Menus (G-05, #9)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 24 open, all `grimoire-ui` (G-05 to G-29), none a `bug`. The last audit row (pass 54) had nothing in `missing`. home-still: `hs status` 18,673 documents, distill up on :7434 (idle), one scribe backend unavailable for memory, the other converting; `distill_search` answered, so research was not blocked. The owner's rule picks the lowest-numbered request whose `Depends on` is all closed: **#9 G-05, menu bar and dropdown menus**, depends on #8 and #12, both closed in iterations 25 and 26. Skipped by that rule, not blocked: #10 (context menus), #11 (palette) and the other 21. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (a Focus checkpoint, old); ignored.

**What changed** (research `c973eb9`; build `8686730`, `3bac76a`; docs and demo `322b12c`):
- `menu_bar(children![menu_title("File", children![..])])` with the rows `menu_item(label).with_shortcut(..)`, `check_item`, `radio_item`, `submenu`, `separator` and `Disabled::new(reason)`; `MenuBar`, `MenuTitle`, `MenuPanel`, `MenuItem`, `MenuOpen`, `MenuMarked` and twelve `Role::Menu*` variants. **A menu is spawned once with its title and shown by opening**: its panel is a persistent child, `Display::None` and in no scope while closed, and open exactly while it has a `PopoverPanel` + `Dialog` + modal `TabGroup`, so #8's placement, outside press, `Cancel`, `reveal_popovers` and focus in and back apply unchanged (`popover::close` hides a `MenuPanel` and despawns any other popover). The items are live entities, so a game's `observe(..)` stays where it is written.
- F10, or Alt pressed and released with no other key, focuses the first title (the focus it left is kept; Escape from the bar, or F10 again, gives it back); Left and Right walk the titles with wrap; Down, Enter, Space, South or a click opens a menu with focus on its first item; inside, Right opens a submenu or moves to the next title's menu, Left closes a submenu or moves to the previous title's, Home and End go to the ends, Escape or B closes one level and returns focus to the opener; a pointer on another title switches menus. `menu_by_input` reads keys and the D-pad from `ButtonInput` and the pads (one path, so the pad has them), and `navigate_by_input` leaves them.
- Check items flip their own `Checked` and radio items set theirs and clear the run to the nearest separator, each sending one `ValueChange<bool>`; a disabled item is focusable, never activates, and its tooltip says why; an empty submenu says "Nothing here"; a menu taller than the window's room is capped (`fit_menus`), scrolls and follows focus (`scroll_menus_to_focus`); the mark slot exists only in a menu that has a check or a radio item.
- `examples/menus.rs`, `docs/research/menu.md`, `docs/guide/menus.md`, `docs/media/menus.gif`, README bullet and caption, widgets and host-API tables, AGENTS notes, `tests/menu.rs` (16 tests).

**Ranking.** Pick: #9 by the owner's rule. Runner-up: #10 G-06 context menus (it now stands on `menu_panel` and the menu rows, needing a pointer-anchored open).

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; on #9): (1) Bevy 0.20's own `bevy_ui_widgets::menu` (`MenuPopup`, `MenuItem`, `MenuButton`) is not wrapped: it closes a menu when focus leaves it, holds one menu open at a time and has a TODO for submenus, so the crate's popover is the one close path; (2) `MenuItem` is a component with a build hook (like `Tooltip`) so `menu_item(..).with_shortcut(..)` chains; `Disabled(String)` is a component whose hooks add `InteractionDisabled` and a `Tooltip`; (3) Right and Left across the bar open the neighbour's menu with focus in it (APG's second option) so every way of opening a menu is one rule; (4) radio items' group is the run between separators, not a named group.

**Sources** (`docs/research/menu.md`): craft, Li, Bengio and Bailly, *Predicting Human Performance in Vertical Menu Selection Using Deep Learning* (stem `10.48550_arXiv.1803.05073`: Hick's Law, "the time required for an expert human to make a decision of choosing among a given number of options"); shipping, XAG 112 on *Sea of Thieves* ("regardless of where the player is in the UI navigation sequence, they're always provided the ability ... to go back") with the Halo Infinite reading from `popover.md`; technical, the W3C WAI-ARIA APG *Menu and Menubar Pattern* (the whole keyboard contract, quoted) and Bevy 0.20's `bevy_ui_widgets::menu` source, plus the scikit-learn API paper. Corpus: no menu-bar document in home-still (searched three ways); listed under Pending index, and the Li et al. paper added to `corpus.md`.

**Bugs found on the way.** (1) Bevy's `FocusedInput` is retargeted at each ancestor as it bubbles, so Enter on a *disabled* item (no handler stops it) reached the enabled title `Button` above it and toggled the menu closed: the panel's `stop_activation_keys` observer stops Enter and Space there (test `a_disabled_item_never_activates_and_says_why` failed before). The same latent trap holds for any disabled button inside a popover whose anchor is a `Button`. (2) Down on a title also moved focus spatially to the widget below for a frame (invisible, but a stray `UiFeedback::Focus`): `navigate_by_input` now leaves a title's Down to the menu (mutation-checked).

**Tests.** `tests/menu.rs`, 16, each in the headless window with real `KeyboardInput`, `PointerInput` and raw gamepad events: the acceptance F10, Right, Down, Enter (the second menu's first item, chosen once, menu closed); a disabled item focusable, never chosen by Enter, Space or a click, its reason showing (mutation: removing the stop observer fails it); chords ending on one edge and labels starting on one; Escape one level, then back to where F10 found focus, and F10 again leaving the bar (mutation: removing the title's Down guard fails it); a submenu opening on Right, closing on Left with focus back on its item, choosing closing the cascade, one `PopoverClosed` for the player's Escape; Right and Left walking the titles with menus open and wrapping (mutation: removing `menu_rows` from `keeps_sideways` fails five tests); a check flipping once and a radio run keeping one on; "Nothing here", closed by Left and by Escape; the pointer opening, switching, closing, a press outside, a click choosing; an Alt tap against an Alt chord; a 40-item menu inside the window with Home and End scrolled into view; the pad's D-pad, South and B; every word's contrast (label at rest and lit, chord, disabled item, title) in all four presets (7:1 in high contrast), the ring against the card, 44 px rows; nothing leaving its parent at 1x, 1.5x and 2x text; the mark slot only in a menu with a mark; nothing written while idle.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g27/critique.md`; evidence `after/{dark-720,dark-1440,dark-2x,parch,hc,tool,b-view,b-2x,b-1440}.png`, the GIF frames): the bar is the panel's colour with one line under it, the open title stays lit, the card sits half a gap under it with the popover's frame and shadow, rows light as one bar with the ring, chords hard against the right edge in muted text, ticks and dots in a column of their own; all four looks read as one family. Fixed after the first capture: a mark slot in every item (57 px of dead space at 1x, 80 px at 200%), and "Open..." nearly touching its chord at 200%. Open: the submenu card overlaps its parent's frame by about 2 px (reads as a cascade), the font lacks "…" (examples use "..."), no hover-to-open submenus, no type-ahead. Verdict: **GREAT** at 1x and 1440p in all four looks; at 200% text GREAT apart from the pre-existing tooltip placement for a disabled item in a 720p window.

**Checklist** (1280x720, dark, parchment, high contrast, dark 1.5x; the menu example in all four presets, 1440p and 200%): the four gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`2b7dcc1`): no existing look changed. `problems()` unchanged (no new colour; every menu text is an existing pair). Rows and titles at least `target()` tall (44 px; 24 px in Tool). Menu text contrast measured from the drawn colours: at least 4.5:1 in dark, parchment and tool, 7:1 in high contrast, in every state.

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `2b7dcc1`, Xvfb, 10 alternating pairs): minimum 22.94 before and 22.86 after, median 23.68 and 23.61, maxima 24.49 and 24.08; paired median −0.06 ms: no change (the new systems return at once while no menu exists or is open).

### Ranked backlog

1. **#10 G-06 context menus** (a menu opened at the pointer on a right-click, on `menu_panel` and the menu rows), then #11 G-07 command palette: the next `grimoire-ui` requests in order.
2. Hover-to-open submenus with a short delay and a safe triangle (the pointer player's cascade), and type-ahead (APG optional).
3. A tooltip no side can hold at 200% text (it lands over its own panel), now also a disabled menu item's reason.
4. #13 G-09 split panes, #16 G-12 virtualized list, #19 G-15 collapsible sections: the independent requests.
5. A safe corner for the legend (a tall panel at 200% text runs under it).

**Merge and release.** The pass committed straight on `main` (research `c973eb9`, build `8686730`, `3bac76a`, docs and demo `322b12c`, the docs-link fix and this log, `release: v0.14.0` `756e95f`) and also pushed `loop/iter-27` at the same commit; `origin/main` had not moved, so the gated tree is the pushed tree. **Gate**, one script, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=3`: fmt (one wrapped `taps(..)` call in `tests/menu.rs`, fixed), clippy `--all-targets --all-features -D warnings` (three `type_complexity` and one `field_reassign_with_default`, fixed first), `cargo test --all-features` (every binary `ok`, the README test and the doctests included), `cargo check --lib`, the wasm32 library check green; `cargo doc -D warnings` failed on three intra-doc links in the new constructors' rustdoc (`Activate`, `ValueChange<bool>`), fixed, then fmt, doc, `readme` and `menu` re-run green. About 3 minutes with a warm cache. **Release: v0.14.0**, https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.14.0 (tag `v0.14.0`; the crates.io API lists it, created 16:32:43Z). `cargo semver-checks check-release` reported one break, `enum_variant_added` on `Role` (twelve `Menu*` variants), a minor bump before 1.0; the changelog says so. Issue #9 closed with the assumed-decision comment and label; bevy_grimoire #224 and #225 (their **Needs** lines name G-05) were told to move the pin to `756e95f`.

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's `~/g27_base` worktree (`git worktree remove`, plain; no `-target` beside it, the build went into the shared `~/target/gooey-loop`), `~/g27_bin` (before and after galleries and the menus example) and `/tmp/g27` (captures, GIF frames, perf and gate scripts and logs). `df -h /home` 261 GB free before and after (the shared target grew by the new test binaries and is left). **Left for a person, untouched** (earlier passes'): the worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked` and `~/g17s_base` (detached, 1.8 MB each, no `*-target`) and the scratch `/tmp/g11` (1.6 GB), `/tmp/g14` (2.9 MB), `/tmp/g17` (1.3 GB), `/tmp/g17b`, `/tmp/g17p`, `/tmp/g17s` and `/tmp/g19` (1.5 GB).

## Iteration 28 — 2026-11-01 — Context menus (G-06, #10)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 23 open, all `grimoire-ui` (G-06 to G-29), none a `bug`. The last audit row (pass 55, v0.14.0) had nothing in `missing`. The owner's rule picks the lowest-numbered request whose `Depends on` is all closed: **#10 G-06, context menus**, depends on #8 and #9, both closed. Skipped by that rule, not blocked: #11 (palette) and the other 21. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (a Focus checkpoint, old); ignored. home-still: `distill_search` answered; no context-menu document in the corpus (listed under Pending index in the note).

**What changed** (research `d2f1ebc`; build `0a41fd7`; docs and demo `14477d5`):
- `context_menu(items)` (a `menu_panel` that is a root, with the rows of #9) and `ContextMenu(menu)` on any entity, a UI node, a sprite or a mesh with `Pickable`. A secondary `PointerClick` that reaches the entity opens the menu at the pointer (`Placement::AtPointer`, flipping at the window's edge) and goes no further, so the nearest `ContextMenu` above the pick wins; Shift+F10 or the Menu key (`context_by_key`) open the nearest `ContextMenu` at or above the focus below the focused widget. Opening is `show_menu`, the one place a menu panel becomes a modal popover (`open_menu` now calls it), so outside press, `Cancel`, focus in and back are #8's. A second right-click moves it (the menu closes itself first, so `Added<PopoverPanel>` fires even when press and release come in one frame), one context menu is open at a time.
- `ContextTarget(thing)` on the panel exactly while it is open, inserted before the menu is shown (an `On<Add<ContextTarget>>` observer enables or relabels items before the first draw); `ContextTarget::of(item, &parents, &targets)` reads it from an item's `Activate`. One menu serves many things.
- Found on the way (each fails a test before): (1) a UI node under a world entity is never laid out and gets no render target (`UiRootNodes` is `Without<ChildOf>`, `FixedNode` roots are only propagated a target through UI parents), so `anchor_pointer_popovers` gives an `AtPointer` popover over an anchor with no `Node` a root point (the first sprite test read a 0x0 card); (2) `fit_menus` capped a menu at the room on its preferred side, so a menu flipped above the pointer in the window's corner was cut to three rows and scrolled (`Delete` came out 31 px below the card; `near_the_windows_edge_the_menu_flips_and_stays_inside` checks every row is whole); it is now the larger side, also for `Below` and `Above`; (3) Shift+F10 also took F10's bar focus: `focus_menu_bar` is F10 with no Shift.
- `examples/context.rs` (crates in the world, an inventory of rows sharing one menu with a submenu, a line saying which thing a command was for), `docs/research/context-menu.md`, `docs/guide/context-menus.md`, `docs/media/context-menu.gif`, README bullet and caption, widgets and host-API tables, AGENTS notes, `tests/context_menu.rs` (13 tests).

**Ranking.** Pick: #10 by the owner's rule. Runner-up: #11 G-07 command palette (needs #12, done; it stands on `Shortcut` and the menu rows).

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; on #10): (1) `ContextMenu(Entity)` names a live `context_menu(..)` panel, where the issue proposes `ContextMenu(Vec<MenuEntry>)` data: items are live entities (#9's decision) so a game's `observe` sits on the item, and one menu can serve every row of a list; (2) the menu is a root and not a child of its thing: a UI node under a sprite is never laid out (found above); the game despawns it; (3) the menu opens on the click (the release, as Windows), not the press; (4) a right-click on a `button` that has a `ContextMenu` also sends that button `Activate`: Bevy's `Button` takes any pointer button, documented and not fixed here.

**Sources** (`docs/research/context-menu.md`): craft, Malacria et al., *Promoting Hotkey use through rehearsal with ExposeHK* (stem `10.1145_2470654.2470735`: "users can still browse the menus and select the items using the pointer only"); shipping, XAG 112 on *Sea of Thieves* (always one step back, with focus given back); technical, Wikipedia's *Context menu* summarising Microsoft's and Apple's guidance ("Application key or Shift+F10 opens a context menu for the region that has focus"; a keyboard-opened menu appears "near the focused widget instead of the position of the pointer"), Bevy 0.20's `bevy_ui` layout source for the root rule, and the scikit-learn API paper's one-way-to-do-a-thing. Microsoft's own UX page returned 404; listed under Pending index.

**Tests.** `tests/context_menu.rs`, 13, in the headless window with real `KeyboardInput` and `PointerInput` and a picked colour-only `Sprite`: the issue's acceptance (a right-click on a picked sprite opens its menu with the card's corner within 1 px of the pointer and focus on the first item; Shift+F10 opens the focused button's menu below it, Enter chooses once with the `ContextTarget` the row, focus back); the Menu key and Escape (one `PopoverClosed`, `Cancel`); the nearest thing's menu with a button inside it focused; a second right-click moving it, one menu for two sprites with the right `ContextTarget` and `Opened` log `[a, a, b]`; the same with press and release in one frame; only a secondary click; a right-click on a UI row and choosing for it; a press outside closing once; the corner flip with every row whole; Shift+F10 not focusing the bar and plain F10 still doing; a despawned menu; nothing written in 30 idle frames closed or open. Mutation checks (copy, edit, run, copy back, `cmp`): dropping the close-before-reopen in `open_context` fails the fast second click; removing the Shift guard in `focus_menu_bar` fails the bar test; making every pointer point a child of its anchor fails four sprite tests; the pre-fix `fit_menus` failed the corner test before it was changed.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g28m/after/c1.png`, `ctx-*.png`, the GIF frames): the card is the menu bar's own in dark, parchment and high contrast (frame, shadow, accent bar with the ring on the first row, the chord I hard right, the tick in its slot) and at 200% text (rows whole, flipped inside the window); with its corner on the pointer it reads as the platform's. At 1x in all three looks and at 200%: **GREAT**. Open: (1) the first frame a menu is drawn is an empty card, the text lands a frame later (the `reveal_popovers` delay, also for a menu bar's menus; one frame at 60 Hz), (2) a tick in the mark slot sat misplaced in one captured frame right at opening (same cause), (3) the example's status line is one line and clips at 200% in a 1280 px window if long (shortened), (4) the Bevy `Button` right-click activation above.

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark, parchment, high contrast, and 2560x1440 dark 1.5x, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`): the four gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`8185516`): no existing look changed. `problems()` unchanged (no new colour). Hit targets unchanged (rows `target()`, 44 px). Menu row contrast is the menu bar's (4.5:1 in dark, parchment and tool, 7:1 in high contrast).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `8185516`, Xvfb, 10 alternating pairs, twice with the order swapped): first run minimum 22.57 before and 22.77 after, median 23.68 and 24.25; second run minimum 23.06 before and 22.66 after, median 23.47 and 23.78: no change beyond noise (the new system returns at once while no key is pressed, and the click observer is one failed query).

### Ranked backlog

1. **#11 G-07 command palette**, then #13 G-09 split panes, #16 G-12 virtualized list, #19 G-15 collapsible sections: the next `grimoire-ui` requests in order.
2. The empty first frame of a popover or menu: the panel's text is measured only once it is `Display::Flex`, so the card is drawn a frame before its words; hold it hidden one more frame or measure while closed.
3. A right-click on a `button` also activates it (Bevy's `Button` takes any pointer button): a crate-wide rule that `button`s take the primary button only.
4. Hover-to-open submenus with a safe triangle, and type-ahead (from iteration 27).
5. A tooltip no side can hold at 200% text (it lands over its own panel); a safe corner for the legend.

**Merge and release.** `loop/iter-28` pushed and merged `--no-ff` into `main` (`15116c1`, `Fixes #10`; `origin/main` had not moved, so the merged tree is the gated tree). **Gate**, one script, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=2`, on the branch: fmt, clippy `--all-targets --all-features -D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features` (all 32 result lines `ok`, the README test and the 95 doctests included), `cargo check --lib`, the wasm32 library check and `cargo doc -D warnings`, green (3.5 minutes). `cargo semver-checks check-release`: one break, `function_parameter_count_changed` on `anchor_pointer_popovers` (a system gained a query), a minor bump before 1.0. **Release: v0.15.0**, https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.15.0 (tag `v0.15.0`, release commit `8099e4b`; `cargo info bevy_gooey@0.15.0` resolves and the crates.io API lists it, created 17:06:31Z). Issue #10 closed by the merge, with the assumed-decision comment and label; the pin comment is on bevy_grimoire#230 (UI-36, whose Needs line names #10).

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's `~/g28_bin` (before and after galleries, the context and menus examples) and `/tmp/g28m` (captures, GIF frames, perf and gate logs, mutation copies); no worktree was made (the baseline was this clone's own release build, copied out before the first edit). `df -h /home` 242 GB free after (the shared `~/target/gooey-loop` grew with the new test binaries and is left). **Left for a person, untouched** (earlier passes'): the worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked` and `~/g17s_base` (detached at `4497376` and `4859669`, 1.8 MB each, no `*-target`) and the scratch `/tmp/g11`, `/tmp/g14`, `/tmp/g17`, `/tmp/g17b`, `/tmp/g17p`, `/tmp/g17s` and `/tmp/g19`.

## Iteration 29 — 2026-11-01 — Command palette (G-07, #11)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 22 open, all `grimoire-ui`, no `bug`. The last audit row (pass 56, v0.15.0) had nothing in `missing`. By the owner's rule the pass's item is the lowest-numbered request whose `Depends on` is closed: **#11 G-07, the command palette** (depends on #8, #12, closed). Skipped by that rule, not blocked: #13 and the other 20. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (old, ignored). home-still: `distill_search` answered; no command-palette or fuzzy-matching document (Pending index in the note).

**What changed** (research `dbca6e9`; build `8dc2573`; docs and demo `9798de9`; gate fixes `c2a116e`; merge `3ffd1af`, `Fixes #11`; release `18de9ed`):
- `command_palette()` (a dialog underneath: scrim, modal `TabGroup`, focus in and back, `Cancel`) holds a text field that fills a `Role::Palette` card and eight reused `Role::PaletteRow`s (Bevy's `Button`; the words are the menu's `MenuText` and `MenuShortcut`, so ink, lit look and contrast are the menu's). `CommandList(Vec<PaletteCommand>)` is the one list (`#[non_exhaustive]`, builders `category`, `shortcut`, `disabled(reason)`), `PaletteHistory` the last ten ids, `RunCommand { id }` the one event, sent after the palette is despawned so focus is already back. `model::fuzzy_score` is engine-free (terms in any order, subsequence, word starts and runs score, gaps and length cost). Up, Down (wrapping), PageUp, PageDown and the D-pad move the lit row (Bevy's `Selected`: hovered fill and the ring) while focus stays in the field; the pointer moving over a row lights it; a click runs it; the card's padding keeps focus; the scrim closes it. A disabled command shows its reason where the chord would be and runs nothing.
- `update_palette` measures the last layout and shows fewer than eight rows when large text or a short window leaves no room (`rows_that_fit`, `CommandPalette::visible_rows()`): at 200% text at 720p the first capture's card ran 50 px off the bottom; it now shows 6 rows and ends above the safe edge. It runs only when the field, the lit row, the list, the history or the card's size changed and writes its own state through `bypass_change_detection`, so an idle palette writes nothing.
- `text_input` is now one private `text_row(label, value, chars)`; `Role::PaletteField` is its label-less row with no side padding or frame, so the field's frame is as wide as the lit bars.

**Found on the way.** (1) Bevy's single-line field answers Up and Down with `TextEdit::Up/Down`, which sends the caret to the start and swallows the key, so the next letter went in front of the text ("xopen"): the palette queues `TextEdit::TextEnd` after an arrow (mutation-checked: a test that types after Up fails without it). (2) A guard in `navigate_by_input` for the palette field's Up and Down turned out dead code (the field is the only stop in its modal scope); its mutation check passed vacuously, so it was deleted. (3) The example's staged keys were written in `Update`, which reached `palette_by_input` a frame before Bevy's own field handler and left the caret at the start in a real run while the headless tests passed: staged keys are written in `First` now (note in AGENTS.md).

**Ranking.** Pick: #11 by the owner's rule. Runner-up: #13 G-09 split panes.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; on #11, label `assumed-decision`): `#[non_exhaustive]` `PaletteCommand` with builders instead of the issue's literal struct; recents first among a query's matches (the issue's order); the palette closes itself and the game only observes `RunCommand`; fewer rows at large text.

**Sources** (`docs/research/command-palette.md`): craft, Malacria et al., *ExposeHK* (stem `10.1145_2470654.2470735`: hotkeys "remov[e] the need to traverse a menu or tab hierarchy", "few users employ any form of shortcut interface"); shipping, Godot 4's editor Command Palette (docs.godotengine.org, *Default editor shortcuts* and `EditorCommandPalette`: "Object that holds all the available Commands and their shortcuts text"); technical, the WAI-ARIA APG *Editable Combobox With List Autocomplete* (focus stays in the textbox, `aria-activedescendant`, Down and Up wrap, the active option scrolled into view), Bevy 0.20 `bevy_ui_widgets::text_input`, and the scikit-learn API paper.

**Tests.** `tests/palette.rs`, 19, in the headless window: the acceptance ("opn prj" first of 200, one `RunCommand`, focus back on the opener), recents first and ahead of a better score, the no-match line, a disabled command (reason, neither Enter nor a click, focus stays) and one switched on while open, Up and Down with focus in the field (wrapping, typing after Up appends), PageDown and PageUp, the count, a changed query lighting the first match, Enter on the lit row, Escape with focus back, the pointer and a click, padding and scrim clicks, the D-pad and South, a shorter list, the card and rows inside the window and a target high at 1x, 1.5x, 2x, large text showing fewer rows with the lit row always among them (mutation: no fit, "card ends at 770"), contrast of every word and the ring in four looks, nothing written while idle. Mutation-checked: caret restore, focus-keeping `AcquireFocus`, the scrim click, recents order, the hover observer, the fit; `fuzzy_score` has two unit tests and a doctest.

**Critique and verdict** (evidence `opn.png`, `empty.png`, `scroll.png`, `off.png`, `nomatch.png`, `p_parch/p_hc/p_tool/p_x2b.png`, the GIF frames): a card at the top of the dimmed screen in the menus' card look, the field in the focus ring, the lit row a bar with the ring, chords muted at the right, a disabled row dimmed with its reason, the count under eight rows; parchment, high contrast and Tool read as one family, and 200% text fits in six rows. At 1x in all four looks and at 200%: **GREAT**. Open: the field's text stands about 5 px left of the rows' (frame aligned with the bars instead), two English sentences, no highlight of matched letters, no wheel scroll, a long reason does not wrap.

**Checklist** (1280x720, dark, parchment, dark 1.5x at 2560x1440): the three gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`c14d899`). `problems()` unchanged (no new colour). Rows `target()` high (44 px, 24 in Tool).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `c14d899`, Xvfb, 10 alternating pairs): minimum 22.44 before and 22.49 after, median 22.88 and 22.88: no change (`update_palette` and `palette_by_input` return at once while no palette exists).

### Ranked backlog

1. **#13 G-09 split panes**, then #16 G-12 virtualized list, #19 G-15 collapsible sections, #14 G-10 dock layout: the next `grimoire-ui` requests in order.
2. Palette polish: the matched letters drawn in the accent, the mouse wheel over the rows, the two sentences from a `PaletteWords` resource, a long reason ellipsised.
3. A right-click on a `button` also activates it (Bevy's `Button` takes any pointer button); the empty first frame of a popover or menu (from iteration 28).
4. Hover-to-open submenus with a safe triangle, and type-ahead (iteration 27).

## Iteration 30 — 2026-11-01 — Split panes (G-09, #13)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 21 open, all `grimoire-ui`, no `bug`. The last audit row (pass 57, v0.16.0) had nothing in `missing`. By the owner's rule the pass's item is the lowest-numbered request whose `Depends on` is closed: **#13 G-09, split panes** (no dependencies). Skipped by that rule, not blocked: #14 to #33. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (old, ignored). home-still: preflight answered; `distill_search` found nothing on resizable panes or docking (Pending index in the note); `catalog_recent` (60 rows) listed no game-UI document, so the corpus is unchanged.

**What changed** (research `02c56ea`; build `dd770db`; docs and demo `03dc191`; rustdoc links `630e0b1`; merge `620659d`, `Fixes #13`; release `8db1a60`):
- `split(SplitAxis, first, second)` is a flex row or column filling its parent: two `Role::SplitPane`s (a clipped one-cell `1fr` grid, so an auto-sized `panel()` fills the pane on both axes and a wider child is cut) and a `Role::SplitDivider` between them, a focusable channel `MIN_TARGET_PX` (24 px) wide on the list rail's colour with a `Role::SplitGrip` pill (`text_muted`, `text` hovered or focused, `accent` held) and the focus ring as its border. `SplitFraction(f32)` (serde, reflected, 0.5; the first pane's share of the room the divider leaves) is the state, `SplitMin { first, second }` (logical px) the least sizes; both are required components of `Split`, so `(split(..), SplitFraction(0.3), SplitMin::new(200.0, 320.0))` works in one tuple. A player's change is one `ValueChange<f32>` on the split (`is_final` false while dragging, a final one at the drag's end and for every key); a game's write moves the panes and says nothing.
- The ratio is pure flex (`flex_grow` of `f` and `1 - f` from a basis of nothing, no measuring); `update_splits` only keeps each pane's least size from the last layout (`model::split_mins`: a room smaller than both minimums takes the second pane first). A drag is `model::split_drag` of the grab fraction plus `distance / UiScale` over the room, so 100 px is 100 / extent exactly at any scale.
- Keys: the arrows along the axis step 16 px (`SPLIT_KEY_STEP_PX`, held keys repeat), Home and End take the first pane to its smallest and largest, Enter, South and a double click collapse it to its minimum and restore it (WCAG 2.5.7's way that is not a drag); the D-pad steps it (`split_by_pad`, repeating like a slider's); `navigate_by_input` leaves a focused divider the arrows along its axis (`FocusScopes::dividers`); `InteractionDisabled` fixes the layout. `EntityCursor` gives the resize cursor.
- `Role` gains four variants (minor bump). New public items: `Split`, `SplitAxis`, `SplitDivider`, `SplitFraction`, `SplitMin`, `SplitGeometry`, `split_by_pad`, `update_splits`, `split`, `SPLIT_KEY_STEP_PX`, `split_mins`, `split_clamp`, `split_drag`.

**Found on the way.** (1) Bevy sends `PointerClick` to the divider the pointer lets go over after a drag (it moved under the pointer), and the count counts any press within `multi_click_interval`: the second drag in a row collapsed the pane as a "double click" (the test `letting_go_of_a_drag_is_not_a_click` failed first); the click now counts only for `count == 2`, `Pressed` and no `SplitGrab`. (2) Bevy's prelude exports `Axis<T>`, so the issue's `Axis` would be ambiguous in a glob import: `SplitAxis`. (3) While a divider is dragged the pointer, a frame ahead of the layout, passes over the other divider and Bevy's hover lights it (hover is not suppressed during a drag); the example grabs away from the other divider. (4) `cargo doc` stops at the first unresolved link: three of mine in `widgets.rs` needed `crate::` paths, found in two rounds. (5) A test that clicks a divider twice must aim at where it is after the first click moved a pane.

**Ranking.** Pick: #13 by the owner's rule. Runner-up: #16 G-12 virtualized list.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; on #13, label `assumed-decision`): `SplitAxis` not `Axis`; a fraction, not a pixel offset; a 24 px channel, not a hairline; a double click (and Enter, South) as the non-drag way, not arbitrary sizes; "collapse" goes to the minimum; fraction and minimums are required components.

**Sources** (`docs/research/split.md`): craft, W3C *Understanding SC 2.5.7 Dragging Movements* ("All functionality that uses a dragging movement for operation can be achieved by a single pointer without dragging"); shipping, Godot 4's `SplitContainer` ("Offsets for each dragger in pixels", `clamp_split_offset`, `minimum_grab_thickness` 6, a touch dragger because a thin grabber is hard to hit); technical, the WAI-ARIA APG *Window Splitter Pattern* (the arrows, Home, End, Enter), Bevy 0.20's `slider.rs` and `scrollbar.rs` drag maths, and the scikit-learn API paper. The corpus has nothing on splitters.

**Tests.** `tests/split.rs`, 29, in the headless window with real pointer, keyboard and gamepad input: the ratio, a vertical split, content filling or cut by its pane, a smaller split keeping state, the issue's acceptance (a 100 px drag moves the fraction by 100 / extent, at 1x and at 1.5x UI scale and along a vertical axis), minimums under drag and in a squeezed split (mutation: dropping the second's room limit in `split_mins` fails it), reports (non-final then one final; a game's write sends none), a nested split, keys (a button beside the divider proves focus does not move: mutation in `navigate_by_input` fails it), held repeat, Home, End, Enter, the D-pad and South, the double click and a drag not being one, a disabled divider, the grip's colours, the ring and 3:1 grip contrast in all four presets, Tool and 200% text, nothing written while idle. Plus a model unit test (never NaN, minimums held in every room), the serde round trip and 5 doctests.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g30m/after/dark.png`, `focus.png`, `parchment.png`, `hc.png`, `tool.png`, `large.png` (2560x1440, 1.5x), `small.png` (640x480), the drag strip and `split.gif`): two panels and a log with quiet rails between them, a short grip in each, the ring complete around a focused divider in all four looks, the grip amber while held, panels never jumping while resizing, the sidebar's 240 px minimum holding when dragged and in a small window. At 1x in all four looks, at 1.5x text at 1440p and at 640x480: **GREAT**. Open: a collapsed or squeezed pane's widgets are clipped but still tab stops; the resize cursor cannot be seen on Xvfb; the 24 px channel is heavier than an editor's hairline (a recorded trade); hover lights the other divider while one is dragged.

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark, parchment, dark 1.5x at 2560x1440, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`): the three gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`8f08eea`): no existing look changed. `problems()` unchanged (no new colour). The divider is 24 px (the WCAG 2.5.8 minimum) in every look, ring 3 px (2 in Tool, the skin's border).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `8f08eea`, Xvfb, 10 alternating pairs, a 40 s capture ran beside the first pairs): minimum 22.78 before and 22.70 after, median 23.31 and 23.16: no change (`update_splits` and `split_by_pad` return at once with no split). The example's own run is 22.4 to 23.9 ms.

## Iteration 31 — 2026-11-01 — Docks (G-10, #14)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 20 open, all `grimoire-ui` (#14 to #33), no `bug`. The last audit row (pass 58, v0.17.0) had nothing in `missing`. By the owner's rule the pass's item is the lowest-numbered request whose `Depends on` is closed: **#14 G-10, dock layout as data** (depends on #13, closed). Skipped by that rule, not blocked: #15 to #33. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (old, ignored). home-still: `hs status` answered (distill idle, Qdrant up); `distill_search` found nothing on docking or workspace layouts (Pending index in the note); the corpus is unchanged.

**What changed** (research `e715693`; build `5294192`; palette fix `0f0cabe`; `UiScale` fix `3a72196`; rustdoc links `f049cdc`; merge `4798a37`, `Fixes #14`; release `f7ad4e3`):
- `dock(DockLayout)` is a workbench: `DockLayout` is `Split { axis, fraction, first, second }` or `Group { panels, selected }` (panel ids as `String`s, serde with defaults, RON round-trips, a file may name only `panels`), with plain-data edits `panels`, `contains`, `prune` and `moved(panel, &DropTarget)` tested without an app. `PanelRegistry` maps an id to a title and `fn(&mut EntityCommands)` that fills the panel's page; a page is spawned once and moved, never rebuilt, when the layout changes (`remove::<ChildOf>()` before the old tree is despawned, `adopt(entities)` into the new one). `Dock(DockLayout)` is the state; an unknown or repeated id is left out and sent once as `DockPanelSkipped`, an emptied group collapses.
- Dragging a tab (`PointerDragStart/Drag/DragEnd` on a `DockTab`): a card with the title follows the pointer (`Role::DockGhost`) and the part of the dock the panel will take is lit in the ring's colour (`Role::DockDrop`) before it is let go: a tab strip (the place by `dock_tab_slot`), a group's middle half (as a tab), an outer quarter (a group of its own, half each), the dock's own 24 px edge (a quarter of the whole). Nothing is lit where a drop changes nothing. Escape puts it back. One `DockLayoutChanged { entity, layout }` for a drop, a picked tab and a divider let go.
- WCAG 2.5.7: Ctrl+Shift and an arrow on a focused dock tab moves its panel to the nearest group that way or to that edge of the dock, focus following and the ring shown.
- `tabs_in(.., fill)` (a wrapping strip of label-wide tabs and a page area that grows and clips) and `split_in` are the private forms `tabs()` and `split()` are now built from; `Role` gains `Dock`, `DockBody`, `DockDrop`, `DockGhost` (a minor bump); `model` gains `DockZone`, `dock_zone`, `dock_edge_zone`, `dock_drop_rect`, `dock_tab_slot`, `dock_neighbour` and three constants.

**Found on the way.** (1) Bevy's `tab_on_key_input` is a GLOBAL observer, so a tab's own observer cannot stop the arrow with `propagate(false)`: Ctrl+Shift+Right also selected the neighbouring tab, and that select arrived after the move and wrote the old structure over it (the first keyboard test showed `selected: 1` and no move). The dock ignores edits while a move waits for its rebuild, and `land_dock_focus` waits for the rebuild too (it first focused the old tab, which the rebuild then despawned). (2) The tab strip as a `1fr` grid made one tab a full-width amber bar and clipped tabs in a narrow group; a dock's strip is a wrapping flex row. (3) The dock's edge band (24 px) sits over the strip's first slot (the strip starts 14 px from the edge): the strip is decided first. (4) A drop into a group's own body with no place is no change, or every body drop reordered the tab to the end. (5) **A palette test failed on an unrelated change.** Adding a resource (`init_resource::<PanelRegistry>`) made `tests/palette.rs`'s 300 px window show six rows where five were counted; bisecting by file in a scratch copy (not by guess: reverting my systems, the nav hunk and `lib.rs` one at a time did not move it) showed the cause was a latent instability in `rows_that_fit`: it divided by the FIRST shown row's height, which layout rounds to 18 or 19 px by where the row stands (exact 5.93 then 6.31 at the same room). It now uses the mean of the rows shown and keeps the last answer within 1.5 px of rounding (`ROUNDING_PX`); the unedited palette test is the regression (it failed before the change and passes after; nothing else changed). (6) `DockGeometry` took `Res<UiScale>`: a host without the scale plugin panicked `tests/scope.rs` (8 tests), now `Option`. (7) `cargo doc` stops at the first unresolved link batch: widgets.rs needs `crate::` paths for types it does not import.

**Ranking.** Pick: #14 by the owner's rule. Runner-up: #15 G-11 tree view (a tab-less list with expansion), #16 G-12 virtualized list.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; on #14, label `assumed-decision`): ids are strings; a new split is half and half (a quarter at the dock's edge); the keyboard's way moves by direction, no pad way; an unknown id is skipped and reported, not an error; panes at least 96 px.

**Sources** (`docs/research/dock.md`): craft, W3C *Understanding SC 2.5.7 Dragging Movements* and the corpus's input study `10.1016_j.ijhcs.2011.11.002` ("finger input was the fastest method for tapping tasks, but the slowest for dragging": a drop zone is half a group and is lit before the pointer is let go); shipping, Dear ImGui's *Docking* wiki ("Dock into an existing window or node", "Split existing node", the layout saved as data) and `egui_dock` ("tabs and splits are represented with a binary tree"; floating windows are out of scope); technical, Bevy 0.20's `bevy_picking` drag events, `TabList` and `split()`, and the scikit-learn API paper. The corpus has nothing on docking.

**Tests.** `tests/dock.rs`, 19, in the headless window with real pointer and keyboard input: the layout built, a page filling its group, an unknown and a repeated id reported once, a panel registered later, a layout the game writes keeping the pages (same entity, no report), the acceptance (a tab dragged to the other group: one report with the tab moved, the same page entity), a collapsed group, edge and dock-edge drops, the strip's place, a drop that changes nothing, the landing and card while carrying, Escape, Ctrl+Shift+arrow twice with focus following, the dock's edge when no group is that way, a lone tab, a picked tab and a divider reported, the 96 px minimum, a narrow strip wrapping every tab inside its group, nothing written while idle. `tests/serde.rs`: a layout in RON and a file of only `panels`. `src/dock_layout.rs`: 10 unit tests; `model`: 4. Mutation checks (copy, edit, run, restore, `cmp`): dropping the `refocus.is_some()` guard in `commit` fails the keyboard test (the select overwrote the move), and letting `land_dock_focus` run before the rebuild fails it too.

**Critique and verdict** (stills `h_dark`, `h_parchment`, `h_hc`, `f_tool`, `f_large` (1920x1080, 1.5x text), `f_x2` (2560x1440, 2x text), `hold`, the 108 GIF frames and a 2x crop of the ring on a moved tab): groups are quiet panels with a strip of label-wide amber/grey tabs, the landing a blue tint framed in the ring, the card carrying the tab's title, the ring complete on the tab after a keyboard move; the four looks read as one family. 1x in all four looks and 1.5x text: **GREAT**. 200% text in the example's narrow panes: **NOT YET** (a pane cuts its content: "Inspector" and "Snap to grid" clip at 2560x1440 and 2x; the split's documented rule, the host sizes its panes). Open: a divider lights as hovered while a tab is carried over it, the landing tints the text under it, no strip scroll, no tab close, no pad way.

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark, parchment, dark 1.5x at 2560x1440, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`): the three gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`619e623`): no existing look changed (`tabs()` and `split()` are the same widgets built through the new private forms). `problems()` unchanged (no new colour: the landing is `focus_ring` at alpha 0.22). Dock tabs 44 px high, dividers 24 px.

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `619e623`, Xvfb, 10 alternating pairs at load 7): minimum 22.71 before and 22.63 after, median 24.19 and 24.67: no change (`update_docks`, `update_dock_drag` and `land_dock_focus` return at once with no dock). The example's own run is 22.4 to 27.6 ms.

### Ranked backlog

## Iteration 32 — 2026-11-01 — Tables (G-12, #16)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 19 open, all `grimoire-ui` (#15 to #33), no `bug`. The last audit row (pass 59, v0.18.0) had nothing in `missing`. By the owner's rule the pass's item is the lowest-numbered request whose `Depends on` is closed: #15 G-11 (tree view) depends on #16, so it waits, and **#16 G-12, the virtualized list and table** (`Depends on: none`) is the item. Skipped by that rule, not blocked: #15, #17 to #33. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (old, ignored). home-still: `hs status` answered (18,673 documents, distill idle on Cuda); `distill_search` on virtualization, data tables and grid keyboard navigation found nothing relevant (Pending index in the note). The owner pushed a README commit to `origin/main` meanwhile (79ba46f, the editor is the Cauldron); it merged cleanly.

**What changed** (research `0a361f5`; build `5dee954`; docs `d6d4453`; release `2c38392`; merge `ef0ac50`, `Fixes #16`, then `b779ef6` with the owner's README commit):
- `table(columns, rows)`: a header over a scrolling body, **virtualized**. `RowSource` is the one trait a game implements (`len`, `cell -> Cow<str>`, defaults `key` = the index and `compare` = natural order of the cells' text); `Column { title, width (body text sizes), sortable }`. Every row is one height (`GooeySkin::table_pitch`, a button's, whole pixels), so the viewport is as tall as all the rows and only `ceil(rows in view) + 1` row entities exist (the rest is `padding.top/bottom` of the rows column); the viewport is the list's (`ScrollArea`, `ListView`, the rail), so the wheel, the thumb and `update_lists` are unchanged.
- State a game reads and writes, each its own component: `TableSort`, `TableSelection` (by key, so it follows a row through a sort), `SelectMode` (Single by default), `TableCursor`, `TableWidths`, `TableRows`. The player's choices are `SortBy`, `ValueChange<TableSelection>` and `RowActivated`; a game's own write sends none.
- Header: a Bevy `Button` per column that sorts (ascending, then turned round, with a chevron of two borders), a resize edge between columns (a 24 px target dragged, or Ctrl+Left/Right on the focused header, 16 px a press, for WCAG 2.5.7). Rows change through `Table::source_mut` or `set_source`; the table sorts them as before and keeps the row at the top of the view where it was, by key (`model::table_anchor`). A new sort scrolls to the cursor row.
- Keys: one tab stop with a cursor row (APG's active-descendant for rows): Up, Down, Page keys, Home and End (the first and last row of the *data*), Space, Ctrl+A, Enter, the D-pad (repeating), triggers, South. `navigate_by_input` leaves Up and Down to a table only while it has a row that way (`TableCache::takes`), so focus leaves from the first and last rows.
- `model::{natural_cmp, table_window, table_step, TableStep, table_anchor, column_resize, TABLE_LEAST_COLUMN}`; `Role` gains eleven variants (a minor bump).

**Found on the way.** (1) `Overflow::clip` does not turn off a flex item's automatic minimum width (only `hidden`/`scroll` do, and Taffy treats `clip` as not a scroll container): a 61-character name made its cell 774 px wide in a 240 px column; an explicit `min_width: 0` on the cell fixes it (the first test run caught it). (2) Bevy paints a background inside the node's transparent border, so header cells and banded rows are inset by the 3 px line on each side: the gap between header pills is 6 px and the resize grip sits in it, which reads well and was kept. (3) Edges as children of the *strip*, not of their header cell, because a later sibling's header would cover half of a cell child's 24 px target. (4) A pool row's `Selected` and the cursor are two facts: `Selected` is Bevy's marker and the cursor is read in `StateQuery` from a public `TableRow` (the cursor row of the *focused table*), so a table is restyled when focus moves without making the row a focus target. (5) `table_by_input` runs *after* `navigate_by_input` and both read the same pre-move state (`TableCache::takes`): the other order sends focus away on the very press that moved the cursor. (6) Parallel tool calls that create a script and run it in the same batch race: the script did not exist for the first run.

**Ranking.** Pick: #16 by the owner's rule. Runner-up: #15 G-11 tree view (depends on #16, now unblocked: a tree is a table with one column, depth and expansion), #19 G-15 collapsible sections.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; on #16, label `assumed-decision`): the table reorders its own view on a sort and also sends `SortBy`; selection is by key, Single by default, plain arrows move and select, Ctrl+arrow moves only; widths in text sizes, the last column fills, no horizontal scroll; one row height and one line per cell; one tab stop with a cursor row, cells not focusable.

**Sources** (`docs/research/table.md`): craft, the W3C WAI-ARIA APG *Grid Pattern* ("Only one of the focusable elements contained by the grid is included in the page tab sequence", Page Down "typically scrolling so the bottom row ... becomes one of the first visible rows", and the warning that Ctrl+End may reach "the last row in the DOM rather than the last available row in the back-end data") and WCAG 2.5.7 for the edge; shipping, Unity UI Toolkit's `MultiColumnListView` (`FixedHeight` virtualization: "Collection view won't wait for the layout to update items, as the all have the same height"; horizontal layout breaks virtualization); technical, Bevy 0.20's `ScrollArea`/`ScrollPosition` and the scikit-learn API paper (one small trait, defaults for the rest). The corpus has nothing on tables.

**Tests.** `tests/table.rs`, 24, in the headless window with real pointer, keyboard and gamepad input: the acceptance (10,000 rows at most visible + 2 row entities at any scroll and at 200% text, and no more entities than 20 rows; a header click sends one `SortBy` and reorders, a second turns it round), the pool at row 5,000 and at the last row, a game's sort sending nothing, the anchor kept when 100 rows come in above, a removed selected row leaving the selection, keys with the cursor row always whole in view, leaving the table from its last row and back, Enter and a double click, Single, Multi (Ctrl, Shift, Ctrl+A) and None, selection surviving a sort, a dragged edge (+40 px) and Ctrl+Right (+16 px) with focus kept and the 3-text-size floor, a long cell cut on one line, every row state at 4.5:1 in all four looks, the ring only while focus is visible, a disabled table, nothing written while idle, a pad, titles in their headers and rows in the table at each `TEXT_SIZE_STEPS` step. Mutation checks: a pool of +5 fails the entity test and dropping `table_anchor` fails the anchor test (both restored, `cmp`).

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g32m/d_dark.png`, `d_parch.png`, `d_hc.png`, `d_tool.png`, `d_large.png` (1920x1080, 1.5x text), `d_x2.png` (2560x1440, 2x), `sorted.png`, `cursor.png`, `end.png`, `drag.png` and the 59 GIF frames): a quiet header of raised pills with the sort chevron beside the title, rows with a half-strength band on every second one, the accent with inverted text on the selected row and the blue ring on the cursor row, a rail of the list's on the right; the four looks read as one family and nothing overlaps at 1x. 1x in all four looks: **GREAT**. 1.5x and 2x text in the example: **NOT YET** only because the example's panel (9.5 rows) is taller and wider than a 1080p or 1440p window at 3x and 4x of the 720p reference, so the screen cuts it; the table itself passes at every step in `tests/table.rs`. Open flaws: the band is nearly invisible in parchment (a hint by design); a hovered row that is also selected shows the selected fill only; plain Down in `Multi` replaces the selection (Explorer's rule) so a keyboard multi-select is Ctrl+arrow then Space; no type-ahead; no horizontal scroll; a cell cannot hold a widget.

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark, parchment, dark 1.5x at 2560x1440, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`): the three gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`617e7f9`): no existing look changed. `problems()` unchanged (no new colour: the band is `surface_raised` at alpha 0.5, the selected row the accent, the ring the focus ring). Header 44 px or more, row pitch 44 px, resize edge 24 px.

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, baseline `617e7f9`, Xvfb, 10 alternating pairs at load 2 to 7): minimum 23.69 before and 22.97 after, median 26.3 and 26.2: no change (`update_tables` and `table_by_input` return at once with no table). The table example's own run: 21.5 to 23.3 ms with ten thousand rows.

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 18 open, all `grimoire-ui` (#15 to #33, minus #16 closed), no `bug`. Last audit row (pass 60, v0.19.0): nothing in `missing`. The owner's rule picks the lowest-numbered request whose `Depends on` is closed: **#15 G-11, the tree view** (`Depends on: #16`, closed by iteration 32). Next in line: #17 G-13 (`Depends on: #8`, closed), #19 G-15, #21 G-17, #22 G-18; skipped by that rule, not blocked: #17 to #33. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (old, ignored). home-still: `hs status` answered (18,673 documents); `catalog_recent` (200): psychology and medicine only, no game-UI, HUD, navigation or accessibility source, so no `Research: corpus` commit; the corpus has nothing on trees (Pending index in the note).

**What changed** (research `Research: tree`; build `ee00a3d`; docs `549f13f`; merge `101c67c`, `Fixes #15`; release `c6c30f6`):
- `tree(nodes)`: nodes that open and close, **the table's pool with one column and no header**, so the pool, scroll and rail, the anchor, the cursor, the selection by key (`TableSelection`, `SelectMode`, `ValueChange<TableSelection>`) and `table_by_input` are the table's code, not a second copy. `Tree` is the table's `RowSource` (flat depth-first lists of key, label, depth and each node's **own** open flag, `model::tree_ends`, `model::tree_visible`); `TreeNode::new(key, label).expanded().child(..).children(..)` builds it; a game changes it through `table.source_mut::<Tree>()` (`insert`, `remove`, `set_nodes`, `set_open`, `is_open`, `open_keys`).
- A row's first cell gets a `Role::TreeTwisty` box (margin-left = depth × `model::tree_indent`, a text size and a half, never under 24 px; `align_self: Stretch`, so a row high and a click target) holding a `TreeClosed` and a `TreeOpen` chevron shown by `Display` (the sort mark's trick), drawn in the row's ink (inverted on the accent).
- Keys: Right (opens, or goes to the first child), Left (closes, or goes to the parent), the D-pad's Left and Right, `*` (opens the siblings), type-ahead (`model::type_ahead`, a one second window, the same letter again goes on to the next node, a Ctrl, Alt or Super chord types nothing); the table's own for the rest. `TreeToggled { key, open }` is the player's opening or closing only. A removed node's selected key, cursor and range anchor go to its heir (`model::tree_heir`: next sibling, else the one before, else the parent) in `update_trees`, with no `ValueChange`. A closed node keeps its descendants' state and selection (`update_tables` keeps a hidden selected node: `Tree::contains`); a cursor hidden by a closed ancestor goes to it.
- `model::{tree_indent, tree_ends, tree_visible, tree_parent, tree_heir, type_ahead, TYPE_AHEAD_SECS}`; `Role` gains three variants (a minor bump).

**Ranking.** Pick: #15 by the owner's rule. Runner-up: #17 G-13 (editor fields: number scrub, vector, colour), #19 G-15 (collapsible sections).

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; on #15, label `assumed-decision`): the API is a `TreeNode` builder with a key, not `tree_node(label, expanded, children)`, because selection, cursor and scroll anchor are by key and the sibling rule needs it; `TreeSelection` is the table's `TableSelection` (one path); the data lives in the table's source and the game mirrors its hierarchy into it.

**Found on the way.** (1) `align_items: Center` on a cell leaves a child its natural height: a row-high click target needs `align_self: Stretch` on both the cell and the chevron box. (2) A selection pruned to the shown rows would lose a collapsed node's selected children on every toggle (the table prunes against its listed keys): the tree needs `Tree::contains` in that filter (mutation-checked: `a_closed_node_keeps_its_selected_descendants` fails without it). (3) Type-ahead as the APG words it ("the string of characters typed") makes "c", "c" a prefix "cc" that matches nothing; a prefix of one repeated letter is that letter. (4) The readout label sized the example's panel, so the GIF breathed as the count changed: a fixed-width format fixed it. (5) `edit` on very long AGENTS.md lines needs an anchor that was shown in full: inserting before a short neighbouring line works. (6) A push to `main` was refused after the publish (the owner pushed `.omp/commands/build-loop.md`): merged `origin/main` (no force) and pushed; the tag already on origin points at the release commit, now on `main`.

**Sources** (`docs/research/tree.md`): craft, the W3C WAI-ARIA APG *Tree View Pattern* ("`Right arrow`: When focus is on a closed node, opens the node; focus does not move. When focus is on a open node, moves focus to the first child node", "Type-ahead is recommended for all trees", "the selected state is always independent of the focus"); shipping, Unity UI Toolkit's `TreeView` (a fixed item height with recycled, rebound elements, an id on every item, the indent and the toggle as parts in front of the content); technical, this crate's own table note, Bevy 0.20's `ScrollArea` and the scikit-learn API paper. home-still has nothing on trees.

**Tests.** `tests/tree.rs`, 25, in the headless window with real pointer, keyboard and gamepad input on a clock the test sets: 10,110 nodes at most visible + 2 row entities (at any scroll, at 200% text, and no more entities than twenty nodes), Left and Right and their one event each, first child and parent, end nodes and closed roots, Up, Down, Home, End over the shown nodes, a closed node's state and selection kept, a hidden cursor moved, type-ahead in five ways, `*`, the D-pad, Left and Right never leaving the tree (mutation-checked), a disabled tree, a chevron click and a label click, Multi ranges, the heir in four cases, the top row staying put, indent and chevron geometry at 1x and 2x, the chevron's contrast in all four looks, idle writes, the data; doctests for the six `model` functions. Two mutations failed their tests as they should (`KeepsSideways` without `TreeView`; the prune without `Tree::contains`).

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g33m/after/t_keys.png`, `c_dark.png`, `c_parchment.png`, `c_high_contrast.png`, `c_tool.png`, `t_x2.png` (2560x1440, 2x text), a 4x crop of the chevrons and the 97 GIF frames): a quiet one-column list, each level a text size and a half in, a chevron before every node that has children (right when closed, down when open) in the text colour, labels of end nodes aligned with their siblings', the accent with inverted text on the selected row, the blue ring on the cursor row, the table's rail on the right; the four looks read as one family and the chevron is as legible as the text. My first chevron was the sort mark's 0.8 size and read small at 1x in the zoom; it is the choice arrow's full size now. Flaws left: at 2x text on a 1440p window the example's panel (9.5 rows) is taller than the window (the table example's flaw too: the host sizes its panel); a very deep tree cuts its labels (no horizontal scroll); a Ctrl+click on a chevron toggles like a plain one. 1x in all four looks and 1.5x: **GREAT**; 200% text in the example's panel: **NOT YET**, for the panel's height only, not the widget (`tests/large_text.rs`-style placement is the host's).

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark, parchment, dark 1.5x, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`): the three gallery stills are `compare -metric AE` **0** against the baseline (`24e6914`): no existing look changed. `problems()` unchanged (no new colour). Row 44 px, chevron box 30 px wide at the dark look's text (`tree_indent`), the chevron 3:1 or more in all four looks (test).

## Iteration 35 — 2026-11-01 — Number fields (#17, slice one)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 17 open, all `grimoire-ui` (#17 to #33), no `bug`. Last audit row (pass 62, v0.20.1): `missing: demo?`, answered by iteration 34's log line (no visible change). Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, ignored). The pass's item is the lowest-numbered open `G-NN` whose `Depends on` is closed: **#17 G-13** (editor fields). `catalog_recent` is not an `hs` subcommand and home-still has nothing on numeric entry (`hs distill search` returned a paper on a game of office tasks), so no `Research: corpus` commit; home-still is up (18,673 documents).

**Slice.** #17 asks for `number_field`, `vec_field`, `color_field` with a picker and `MixedValue`. Too big for one verified pass, so this one is **number field, vec field and mixed value**; the colour field and its `#ff8800` acceptance line are the next slice, so #17 stays open (commented, label `assumed-decision`).

**What changed** (research `db25008`; implementation `009e162`; docs `deca941`; merge `fdfa9ea`; release `c40a785`, tag `v0.21.0`):
- `number_field(label, value, step, range)` is the text input's row (`text_row_as`) plus `NumberValue`, `NumberStep`, `NumberRange`. A press on the label or the row's padding and a drag scrubs it: `start + distance / UiScale × step` (50 px at 0.1 is 5.0, Shift a tenth, Ctrl ten times), rounded to the digits of the rate and held to the range; non-final `ValueChange<f32>` per change and one final. A typed number applies on Enter or on leaving (held to the range, shown as the field shows numbers, `2.50` is `2.5`); an expression (`1.5*2`), a word or nothing is refused: the text goes back and `NumberRefused { text, reason: model::NumberError }` is sent; Escape puts the old number back and sends nothing; focusing the field selects its number (`SelectAllOnFocus`), so typing replaces it. Up and Down step (Shift ten, held keys repeat; the APG spinbutton), the D-pad's Left and Right step and its Up and Down move focus (`FocusScopes::numbers`, `KeepsSideways`, `gamepad_adjust`).
- `MixedValue`: the field is empty with the muted hint `MIXED_HINT` (`--`); any change the player makes is the one value. `vec_field(label, [f32; N], step, range)`: a row of number rows with X, Y, Z, W letters as scrub handles, `VecValue`, `VecMixed(Vec<bool>)` (one flag a component) and `VecChange { index, value, values, is_final }`.
- `model::{parse_number, format_number, number_decimals, scrub_value, step_value, NumberError}`; `Role::NumberPart` and `NumberParts` (a minor bump); `update_numbers` and `update_vecs` (Style, before `update_text_inputs`, one nested `.chain()` in the 20-system tuple).
- **A bug fixed on the way:** a text field left with its text selected drew it in the accent's dark ink on no highlight, so the number vanished (Bevy applies `TextCursorStyle::selected_text_color` without focus; found at 3x zoom of a GIF frame after a scrub). `apply_skin` sets it only on the field that has focus; `a_selected_number_is_drawn_in_its_selection_ink_only_while_the_field_has_focus` fails without it. It also hit a `text_input` a player Tabs away from with a word selected.
- README: bullet extended in place, the numbers GIF block added after text input; to keep the README at 250 lines the Back GIF block (`cancel.gif`) moved to `docs/guide/navigation.md` (the file is not moved).

**Ranking.** Pick: #17 by the owner's rule (lowest open `G-NN`, `Depends on #8` closed). Runner-up: #19 G-15 collapsible sections.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; on #17, label `assumed-decision`): the label and the row's padding scrub, not the field (Blender drags the field; Unity the label); `VecChange` with the axis, not `ValueChange<Vec<f32>>`; expressions refused with a reason, not evaluated; Up and Down step instead of leaving the field (Tab and the D-pad's Up and Down leave it); a mixed value is `--`.

**Sources** (`docs/research/number-field.md`): craft, W3C *Understanding SC 2.5.7 Dragging Movements* ("providing a text input can be an acceptable single-pointer alternative to dragging"); shipping, Blender's *Input Fields* manual (drag, Shift precision, Ctrl snap, Return applies, Esc cancels) and Unity's `FloatField` (`labelDraggerVariantUssClassName`, `mixedValueLabelUssClassName`, `support-expressions`); technical, the WAI-ARIA APG *Spinbutton Pattern* (Up and Down, "Focus remains on the text field"). None is in home-still (listed under `Pending index`).

**Found on the way.** (1) The text row's own `text_self_update` observer and mine on the same row run in an order the crate does not control: a direct `TextInputValue` write in `number_on_text` was overwritten by the typed text; the write is queued through `commands` now. (2) `scrub_value` rounded to the digits of the *rate*, so a Ctrl drag (rate 1.0) from 0.5 gave 6 not 5.5; it rounds to the finer of the rate's and the step's digits. (3) `original_event_target() != entity` guards in the drag observers were dead (mutation passed): removed. (4) A em dash is a missing-glyph box in the default font: `--`. (5) The refusal line widened the example's panel: a shorter reason. (6) `update_vecs` needed `Without<VecPart>` on the roots query (B0001). (7) The detached `setsid nohup` gate left an empty log again: use `bash` with `async: true`.

## Iteration 36 — 2026-11-01 — Colour fields and a picker (#17, slice two)

**Triage.** `gh issue list`: 17 open, all `grimoire-ui` (#17 to #33), no `bug`. Last audit row (pass 63, v0.21.0): nothing `missing`. The one-test-binary job of the brief was done in iteration 34 (`tests/all.rs`, `autotests = false`): nothing to do. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, ignored). The pass's item is the lowest-numbered open `G-NN` with its `Depends on` closed: **#17 G-13**, whose acceptance line (`hex #ff8800 sets the colour`) is the last one left. `catalog_recent` (limit 200): psychology and domestic-violence papers only, nothing on game UI, so no `Research: corpus` commit; home-still was up (18 073 docs embedded), the corpus has nothing on colour pickers, so the research note's sources are web pages (WCAG 1.4.11, Blender's manual, Bevy's own `bevy_feathers` and `bevy_ui::gradients`).

**What changed** (research `aac34d6`; implementation `963e9ce`; docs `db52cca`; merge `2d1418e`, `Fixes #17`):
- `color_field(label, Srgba)`: a text row (label, swatch, hex field). The field shows `#RRGGBB` (`#RRGGBBAA` only when the alpha is not full) and takes `#ff8800`, `f80`, `#ff880080`; Enter or leaving applies it as one final `ValueChange<Srgba>`, Escape puts the colour back, anything else is refused as a `ColorRefused { reason: HexError }` and the text restored. State is `ColorValue`; `MixedValue` shows `--` and clear swatch halves.
- The swatch is a button (Enter, Space, South, click) that opens a `popover_modal` below it and closes it again. The picker is a saturation/value **area** (pointer press and drag held to the edge, one final at the end; the arrows a hundredth, Shift a tenth; the D-pad's Left and Right step saturation, Up and Down move on) over **Hue, Brightness and Alpha** strips (Bevy `Slider`s). Everything is drawn with `BackgroundGradient`, so there is no shader and the picker lays out in the headless test window. `ColorPicker` keeps hue, saturation and value, so a drag to black and back or a typed grey keeps the hue.
- `model::{parse_hex, hex_text, hsv_to_rgb, rgb_to_hsv, area_values, HexError}`; `Role::{ColorSwatch, ColorSwatchHalf, ColorArea, ColorAreaMarker, ColorAreaMarkerInner, ColorStripBox, ColorRail}` (a minor bump); `update_colors` joins the nested chain before `update_text_inputs`; `navigate_by_input` and `gamepad_adjust` learn the area. No `GooeySkin` field and no new colour: `problems()` is unchanged.
- README: the bullet names `color_field` and the numbers GIF block gives its place to `colors.gif` (the numbers GIF is in `docs/guide/numbers.md`, which already showed it); the README stays at 250 lines.

**Ranking.** Pick: #17 by the owner's rule. Runner-up: #19 G-15 collapsible sections.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; to be posted on #17): (1) HSV with three strips, not RGB/HSL (a pad needs each channel as a slider; Blender offers all three, this crate one); (2) the colour applies live, no OK button (the picker is a popover; Escape does not revert); (3) alpha shown by splitting the swatch, not a checkerboard (an image or a shader); (4) the hex field is the swatch's neighbour and the typed alternative to the picker (WCAG 2.5.7); (5) `ColorPicker` is public so a game can find which row an open picker belongs to.

**Sources** (`docs/research/color-field.md`): craft, W3C *Understanding SC 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast* ("Ensure meaningful visual cues achieve 3:1 against the background"; "it will still need to have a sufficiently contrasting focus indication"); shipping, Blender 5.2 manual *Color Picker* (a field for two components, a gradient slider for the third, "Hex ... shorthand notation can be used (for example, FC0 for FFCC00)", an extra alpha slider); technical, `bevy_feathers::color_plane` (a `UiMaterial` and a WGSL shader: why this crate uses `BackgroundGradient` instead) and `bevy_ui::gradients`, with this crate's own `number-field.md`, `popover.md`, `controls.md`.

**Found on the way.** (1) `SliderValue` is an immutable component: `update_colors` inserts a new one, `&mut SliderValue` does not compile. (2) `ComputedNode::border()` is a `BorderRect` with `min_inset` and `max_inset`, not `left/top`. (3) The picker's corner pixels are outside the area's rounded frame and are not hit: the corner test presses in the middle and drags out. (4) A strip whose label is the slider's header had its focus ring run through the label: the label is outside the slider now. (5) Nine characters of `#RRGGBBAA` scrolled the `#` away with the caret at the end: the field is ten wide (mutation-checked). (6) The area's height from text sizes pushed the Alpha strip off a 720p window at 200% text: 136 px at any size. (7) `FocusScopes` type complexity again: `Steppable` and `Adjustable` aliases. (8) In the omp bash tool a command that outlives 60 s moves to a "temporary shell" whose file writes are not visible to `read` until the job ends: write captures under `~/`, read them after.

**Tests.** `tests/color.rs`, 24: the issue's acceptance (`#ff8800` and Enter is `Srgba::hex("#ff8800")`, one final change, the swatch the colour), `f80`, a missing `#`, an alpha, three refusals with their reason and the text put back, Escape, leaving applies once, a game's colour shown with no event, thirty idle frames writing nothing closed and open, the swatch opening and closing the picker (Enter, click, Escape, focus back), a disabled row, the area (held ends, press not final, one final, ring under the pointer), the hue kept through black and through a typed grey, the open picker following a game's colour, each strip changing only its channel, the arrows and the D-pad on the area, Tab inside the picker, a mixed colour, the swatch and row sizes, the ring's tones and frames at 3:1, the focus ring, the picker inside the window in four looks at 1x and 1.5x, a nine-character hex not scrolling. Mutations checked: no reconcile of `ColorPicker` (3 fail), no final from a drag (3 fail), alpha strip setting the value (1 fails), a field 9 wide (1 fails). `tests/large_text.rs` gains a `color_field` in its every-widget panel and its one-line sweep (additive).

**Critique and verdict** (`~/g36_tmp/critique.md`; captures `open.png`, `open2.png`, `open3.png`, `parchment.png`, `high_contrast.png`, `tool.png`, `1440.png`, `200.png`, the 178 GIF frames): swatch row and picker at 100% to 150% text in four looks: **GREAT**; `colors.gif` (374 KB, sierra2_4a dither because `bayer` bands the gradients): **GREAT**; the picker at 200% text in a 720p window: **NOT YET** at that one size (the Alpha strip is below the window; Bevy's `Popover` never shortens a panel), documented in `docs/guide/colors.md` and below as the top open item. Eight flaws listed in the critique, four fixed this pass.

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark): swatch 44x44; row 52 to 53 px, a number field's; area and swatch frames the `outline` at 3:1 on the panel; ring two tones at 3:1; focus ring on swatch, area and strips; picker 450 px tall, whole in the window at 1x and 1.5x; `problems()` empty for all four looks (no new colour).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs): before min **22.46** median 23.43, after min **22.06** median 24.09, paired median difference **+0.18 ms** (within 0.5: `update_colors` queries are empty without a colour field). The colors example: 21.9 to 25.6 ms with the picker open.

**Gate.** One script, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`, on the merged tree: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features` (55 unit, 521 integration in the one `all` executable, 149 doctests), `cargo check --lib`, wasm32 and `cargo doc -D warnings`: green first time on the merged tree (4 minutes). A first run on the branch failed clippy `type_complexity` in `nav.rs` (two aliases) and `dead_code` in the test file; fixed.

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks`: `Role` is exhaustive, seven variants added, so a minor bump pre-1.0: **v0.22.0** (`Cargo.toml`, `CHANGELOG`, README compat row, commit `release: v0.22.0`). `cargo publish --dry-run` passed; `cargo publish` was **refused with 429** ("You have published too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours"). Not retried; **no tag `v0.22.0` was made** (nothing is published). The next pass publishes the commit at `main` as 0.22.0 (`cargo publish`, then `git tag -a v0.22.0`) before its own item; the audit will say `missing: release`. #17 closed by the merge's `Fixes #17`; bevy_grimoire #236 and #242 (their Needs line names it) were told to move their pin to `2d1418e`.
