## Iteration 37 — 2026-11-01 — Icons, field problems, progress and spinner (#25, #29, #21)

**Triage.** 16 open issues, all `grimoire-ui` (#18 to #33), no `bug`. Last audit row (pass 64): `missing: release` (v0.22.0, publish refused with `429`). **The release was retried first**: `cargo publish --dry-run` passed, `cargo publish` was refused again with `429` ("too many versions in the last 24 hours"); not retried again. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, ignored). The one-test-binary job of the brief was done in iteration 34 (`tests/all.rs`): nothing to do. Corpus re-scan (`catalog_recent`, 200 rows): psychology, domestic-violence and an MCP-benchmark paper only, no game UI; `corpus.md` unchanged.

**The cluster** (the brief: up to three ready `G-NN`, ordered by how many open bevy_grimoire `ui` issues name each on Needs): counts #25 icons 5, #29 field messages 5, #20 select 3, #21 progress 3, #24 and #18 not ready (Depends on an open issue). Taken: **#25, #29 and #21**. #20 (searchable select, 3 as well) was skipped for #21: it is a popover, a filter and a keyboard model, bigger than a 40-minute share, and it does not sit with the other three (icons are the glyphs a select's rows want, validation sits on the form rows, progress is the meter's cousin). It is next.

**What changed** (research `67ae343`; implementation `b23aeec` (`Fixes #25`, `#29`, `#21`); docs `ebeb5f4`; merge `fab3be9`; release commit `48566ea`):
- `icon(IconSource)`: `Image` or `Atlas`, a `type_scale` step square (`IconSize`, default the body's line), tinted by `IconTone::{Text, Muted, Accent, Danger}` (`Text` follows its button's disabled and pressed state like `ButtonText`); a missing image is an empty box of the right size, `IconMissing` and one warning. `Role::Button` got a half-gap `column_gap` so an icon beside the label has air.
- `FieldProblem { text, kind }` on a text row: the words under it (a hook queues an idempotent `sync_message`, so removal is the same frame), a bar in the kind's colour, an Error also frames the field in `danger`; the row keeps the message's measured height as a bottom margin so the rows below make way.
- `progress(fraction)` with `ProgressText { step, eta_seconds }` ("3 of 7: Measuring meshes, about 20 s left") and `spinner()` (a ring, one accent quarter, one turn per 0.9 s on `Time<Real>`); `model::{eta_words, progress_text, spinner_angle}`; a value outside 0..=1 is clamped and marked once (`ProgressClamped`).
- `Role::{Icon, FieldMessage, FieldMark, Progress, ProgressLine, Spinner}`, `update_icons`, `update_field_problems`, `update_progress`, `update_spinners` (one nested chain at the head of `GooeySystems::Style`). `examples/forms.rs` (an atlas drawn in code, the rules for a name and a count, a bake that runs and starts again). README line and `forms.gif` (in the place of the colours GIF, which `docs/guide/colors.md` keeps; the README stays 250 lines), three guides, three research notes, `host-api.md`, `AGENTS.md`, CHANGELOG, media list.

**Ranking.** Pick: the owner's cluster rule. Runner-up: #20 G-16 searchable select.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; posted on #25, #29, #21 with the `assumed-decision` label): (1) the tone is a component on the icon, not a role of the button (the skin has no per-button tone until #33); (2) the words of a problem are the text colour and the bar and an error's frame the kind's colour, because `danger` and `accent` are held to 3:1 and small text needs 4.5:1 (WCAG 1.4.3); a warning leaves the frame alone, since the accent is the focus ring's blue in the Tool look and close to danger red in parchment; (3) the step and the time left are joined by a comma, because Bevy's default font draws U+00B7 as a box (seen in the first capture); (4) `0.22.0` was never published (429), so this iteration's changes are in 0.22.0's CHANGELOG entry and the version did not move; `cargo semver-checks` against 0.21.0 said no further bump is needed.

**Sources** (`docs/research/{icons,field-problem,progress}.md`; home-still's index has nothing on any of the three, so every source was fetched today and is listed under each note's Pending index): craft, NN/g *Icon Usability* ("a text label must be present alongside an icon"), W3C *Understanding SC 3.3.1 Error Identification* ("The error must be indicated in text"), Nielsen *Response Times: The 3 Important Limits* ("percent-done progress indicators should be used for operations taking more than about 10 seconds"); shipping, Xbox Accessibility Guideline 103 and the Game Accessibility Guidelines (Grounded: "color, as well as shape, iconography, and text labels"), the 7 Days to Die modding wiki's tint rule ("The tint is a multiply"); technical, Bevy 0.20 `ImageNode`, `UiTransform` and `BorderColor`.

**Found on the way.** (1) `StateQuery` was at Bevy's 15-element limit: `marked` became a tuple with the problem. (2) `Bevy`'s default font has no U+00B7. (3) A row's height is 52 or 53 px by its y, so a test comparing a row's size before and after a message needs 1.5 px. (4) `uuid_handle!` makes a handle no asset holds: the missing-image test. (5) A text node with `width: 0` and `min_width: 100%` takes no part in its parent's auto width and fills it at layout: the progress line never changes the bar's width. (6) `Warning = accent` was first drawn on the frame and read as focus (Tool) and as an error (parchment) in the captures: the frame is the error's alone. (7) A detached `( … &)` gate script died when its call returned (empty log); `async: true` kept it.

**Tests.** `tests/icon.rs` (7), `tests/field_problem.rs` (7), `tests/progress.rs` (7: the clamp, the words, the bar's width, contrast, the spinner's turn and its ring), unit tests in `model` (3) and 7 doctests; the issue's three acceptances are the first test of each file. Mutation-checked: danger tone to accent, the error frame to the warning, the fill's divisor, a rotation of zero: each failed its tests (six tests failed together).

**Critique and verdict** (`forms.png` dark at 720p, parchment, high contrast, tool, 200% text, 2560x1440, 151 GIF frames, all under `~/g37_tmp`, removed): the four looks read as one family; the bar and the error frame say "this field" without the words, the words say what; the spinner's lit quarter reads in every look; at 200% text the whole panel fits 720 px. Flaws left: a long warning wraps to two lines with a short last word at 100% (the example's message was shortened); the message takes room a frame after it appears (a one-frame jump, not seen in the GIF's 30 fps); parchment's accent and danger are both red, which the error's frame answers; the progress line wraps past 28 body text sizes. **Icons: GREAT; field problems: GREAT; progress and spinner: GREAT**; `forms.gif` 640x500, 6.0 s, 131 KB (`sierra2_4a`).

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark): words 4.5:1 and bar and frames 3:1 on the panel in all four looks (tests); icon `type_size(2)` square at 1x and 2x; spinner 30 px; row heights unchanged (52 to 53 px) with a problem up; `problems()` unchanged (no new colour).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs): before min **21.79** median 23.77, after min **22.28** median 24.25, paired median difference **+0.08 ms** (within 0.5: the new systems' queries are empty without an icon, a problem, a progress or a spinner). The forms example runs at 23.0 to 24.0 ms with a spinner turning.

**Gate.** One script, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`, on the merged tree: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features` (58 unit, 542 integration in the one `all` executable, 156 doctests), `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green first time (3 minutes). Clippy had caught `type_complexity` on two queries earlier; fixed by aliases and an `allow` on the example's system.

**Release.** `cargo publish --dry-run` passed and `cargo publish` was **refused with 429** (second time for v0.22.0). Not retried; **no tag `v0.22.0`** (nothing is published). The next pass publishes `0.22.0` from `main`: the audit will say `missing: release`. Comments posted on bevy_grimoire #216, #218, #221, #223, #231, #232, #238, #249, #250, #252 ("fixed in `48566ea`; move the pin to it").

**Triage.** 13 open issues, all `grimoire-ui` (#18 to #33, #21 #25 #29 closed), no `bug`. Not retried. Parked work: only the old `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (ignored). Corpus re-scan: not repeated (no game-UI paper in the last two scans). The one-test-binary job was done in iteration 34.

Taken: **#24, #23, #20** (a tool's chrome).

**What changed** (research `365d40c`; implementation `761fa32` (`Fixes #24`, `#23`, `#20`); docs `d91dc29`):
- `toolbar(items)`, `icon_button(IconSource, Tooltip)`, `toggle_group(options, selected)` with `GroupOption::{icon, text}`, `ToggleGroupValue` / `ToggleGroupCount` (one `ValueChange<usize>` on the group; the option on is lit as a selected tab: the skin reads a checked `Role::GroupOption` as `selected`), `toolbar_separator()`. `fit_bars` (Style) holds each bar's items to its width with `model::bar_fit` (lowest rank first, the later of equals first); a toolbar hides its last items and grows a **More** button (a raised `Role::Button` over a `menu_title`) whose menu is rebuilt from the hidden items (tooltip or text as the name, `radio_item` rows for a hidden group, forwarded `Activate`).
- `status_bar(left, right)`, `status_item(text)` (Bevy `Button`, `StatusItem` requires `StatusKind`), `StatusKind::{Info, Warning, Error}` (a mark at the start, the words never change colour), `StatusPriority(u8)` (right group first, lowest first), `status_progress(text, fraction)` (a short `progress` in the item).
- `select(label, options, selected)`: a `choice` plus `Select`; above `SELECT_FILTER_ABOVE` (8) Enter, Space, South and a click open **the palette's card over the select's own options** (`CommandPalette.source`, no recents, the list's own order among equals), Escape leaves the value, one `ValueChange<usize>` with the option's index.
- A tooltip below or above a widget in a row now slides along its edge (`Start`, `End`) before it flips beside it (the first button of a bar with a chord covered its neighbours).
- Eight `Role`s (`Toolbar`, `ToolbarSeparator`, `ToggleGroup`, `GroupOption`, `StatusBar`, `StatusGroup`, `StatusItem`, `StatusMark`), `examples/tools.rs`, three research notes, three guides, `tools.gif` (in the README in `forms.gif`'s place, 250 lines), AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG (in 0.22.0's entry: it was never published).

**Ranking.** Pick: the owner's cluster rule, in order of Needs count. Runner-up: #19 collapsible sections (small, 2 Needs), which #18 (5 Needs) waits on: **the next pass should take #19, then #18**.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; to be posted on #24, #23, #20 with the `assumed-decision` label): (1) the toolbar's rule is `toolbar_separator()` because `separator()` is the menu's horizontal line; (2) a toolbar is not one tab stop with a roving tabindex (APG): every control is a stop and the arrows and D-pad move along the row, as everywhere else in the crate; (3) the select's list is the command palette's card at the top of the window, not a popover anchored under the row: one code path (field, lit row, keys, scrolling, look) at the cost of anchoring; (4) the More button's label is the word "More"; (5) a status item's tooltip is optional; (6) a warning's mark is the accent (named tones are #33).

**Sources** (`docs/research/{toolbar,status-bar,select}.md`; home-still has nothing on any of the three): craft, W3C WAI-ARIA APG *Toolbar* ("the keyboard tab sequence includes one stop for the toolbar and arrow keys move focus among the controls") and *Combobox* ("DOM focus is maintained on the combobox", Enter sets the value), VS Code *UX Guidelines: Status Bar* ("Items that relate to the entire workspace … go on the left … secondary or contextual … on the right", priority); shipping, Xbox Accessibility Guidelines 103 ("Text labels can help take the guesswork out of what an icon … is intended to indicate") and 112 (UI navigation by keyboard and controller alone); technical, Bevy 0.20 `ComputedNode::content_box`, `bevy_ui_widgets::Popover`'s candidate rule (least occluded, first of equals).

**Found on the way.** (1) `(status_item(..), StatusKind::Warning)` panicked in the first capture (the item carried `StatusKind::default()` and so did the tuple): the item now carries a marker that **requires** the kind. (2) A bar sized by its items is as wide as them in a window too narrow for them (1683 px in 1280 px at 200% text) and never overflows; `width: 0, min_width: 100%` (the progress line's trick) fixes it, and the price is that a bar alone in a parent that sizes to its content has no width to fill (documented). (3) A bar that has not been laid out has no room: judging it hid every item and spawned More on the first frame. (4) Staged keys written in `Update` reach a focused widget a frame after a system that reads them acted: the select's Enter closed the list and then opened it again; the example writes them in `First`, as the palette example does. (5) Bevy's `Popover` tries centred alignment only; the first button of a bar at the window's edge flipped beside itself. (6) `fuzzy_score("par", ..)` is a strict subsequence: of forty countries only Paraguay matches, so the acceptance test narrows "pa" first.

**Tests.** `tests/toolbar.rs` (15: the issue's acceptance first, the lit option's fill and icon, none for the one already on, Enter, a game's value and one past the end, disabled, icon button target and tooltip, separator, a bar that fits, a narrow bar and its More menu and the forwarded `Activate`, widening, focus to More, a hidden group's radio rows and choosing one, idle, the tooltip below), `tests/status_bar.rs` (8: the priority order, right before left, widening, the groups at the ends, marks, a short progress, 200% text, idle), `tests/select.rs` (8: "par" in forty options, eight is a choice and nine a list, no match, Escape, the current option lit in the list's own order, Down and Enter, a click, Left and Right, disabled), three `model::bar_fit` unit tests and a doctest. Mutation-checked (each failed its tests, then restored byte for byte): the tie-break of `bar_fit` (the toolbar and status tests), the left and right ranks of a status bar (two tests), the select threshold 8 to 20 (`eight_options_are_a_choice…`), and the tooltip's sliding alignments (`an_icon_buttons_tooltip…`).

**Critique and verdict** (`tools-wide`, `tools-narrow`, `more-open`, `select-open`, `tip`, `status-narrow`, `large`, the four skins, `sheet.png`, all under `/tmp/g38`, removed): see the flaws fixed above; left: the select's list is the palette's card, not anchored (NOT YET as a popover); a rule can stand just before More; parchment and Tool draw a warning mark in the accent. **Toolbar and toggle group: GREAT; status bar: GREAT; select: GREAT as a filter, an anchored popover is the open gap.**

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark): icon buttons and status items at least `skin.target()` (tests); ring on every control and the select's list; the tooltip below the first button, left-aligned, clear of its neighbours; More lists exactly the hidden items; 200% text keeps the highest priority item; `problems()` unchanged (no new colour).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 2×12 alternating pairs): round one before min **21.71** median 24.08, after min 21.97 median 24.15, paired median difference **+0.63 ms**; round two (order reversed) before min 22.18 median 23.32, after min 21.82 median 23.24, difference **-0.14 ms**: noise (the gallery has no bar, the new systems' queries are empty). The tools example runs at 21.5 to 22.7 ms.

**Gate.** One script, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features`, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings` green first time on the branch: 61 unit, 574 integration (in the one `all` executable) and 166 doctests, about 15 minutes with the machine shared.

**Release.** v0.22.0 is still unpublished (429, not retried); this pass's changes are in its CHANGELOG entry. **No tag.** The next pass publishes `0.22.0` from `main` once crates.io accepts it (about 21:00 UTC on 2026-11-02), then tags `v0.22.0`.

**The cluster** (ready `G-NN`, ordered by how many open bevy_grimoire `ui` issues name each on Needs): #18 reflect inspector (5, Depends #17 closed and #19), #19 collapsible sections (2, none), #22 toast actions (2, none). Taken: **#19 first (it unblocks #18), then #18, then #22**: #19 and #18 are one chain (an inspector's nested struct is a section); #22 was the next by lowest number among the ties (#19, #22, #26 …) and sits with the toast module the owner's editor needs for refusals.

**What changed** (research `d7a613c`, `a61a700`, `208afd2`; implementation `b13187e` (`Fixes #19`), `f243400` (`Fixes #18`), `4911722` (`Fixes #22`); docs in each):
- **Sections (#19).** `section(title, open, children)`: a header (`Role::Button`: ring, hover, press and `UiFeedback` are the button's) with a chevron over a body of any bundle, so a section inside one is a nested section. Enter, Space, South and a click send one `ValueChange<bool>` on the section; `SectionOpen(bool)` is the state (a game's write moves it with no event); a closed body is `Display::None` and `Visibility::Hidden` (dropping the `Visibility` made Tab stick in the hidden body: mutation-checked), and focus inside a closing body goes to the header; an open section with nothing in it says "Empty". `SectionId` and `SectionStates` (serde) keep the state across a despawn and respawn. `Role::{Section, SectionBody, SectionNote}`; the tree's chevron boxes now carry a half-size margin each side (the tree's twisty centres them: no tree row moved, 612 tests green).
- **Inspector (#18, feature `reflect`).** `inspector(InspectTarget)` builds the rows of a reflected component or resource: toggle, choice (unit enum), number field (integers stay integers), vec field (2 to 4), colour field (`Srgba`, `Color`), text input, a `section` for a nested struct and a read-only line that follows the value for anything else; doc comments are tooltips, `#[reflect(@RangeInclusive::<f32>::new(..))]` is the range, `InspectStep` the step. Edits are written through `ReflectComponent::reflect_mut` and sent as one `InspectorChange { entity, path }`; the game's own writes move the rows with no event (`sync_inspectors`, an exclusive system computing diffs under immutable borrows); a text field being typed in is skipped (mutation-checked). In Bevy 0.20 a resource is a component on a singleton entity, so one path serves both targets. `Role::Inspector`.
- **Toast actions (#22).** `toast(..)` / `toast_alert(..)` return a `ToastBundle` with `.action(label, id)` (one `ToastAction { toast, id }`, then the toast fades; a toast with a button stands at least `action_seconds`, 6) and `.sticky()` (stands until a **Dismiss** button, outside `max_visible`; mutation-checked); identical toasts within `merge_seconds` (2) merge into "x3"; `ToastLog` keeps the last 200; `notification_center()` lists it newest first as a scrolling list of buttons (`m:ss  words`, `! ` for an alert). `Role::{ToastAction, ToastActionText, NotificationCenter}`; `ToastSettings` gains two fields.
- `examples/{sections, inspector, notifications}.rs`, three research notes, three guides (`sections.md`, `inspector.md`, `toasts.md`), `sections.gif`, `inspector.gif`, `notifications.gif` (the README shows `inspector.gif` in `tools.gif`'s place, 250 lines; the others are in their guides), AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG (in 0.22.0's entry: it was never published).

**Ranking.** Pick: the owner's cluster rule. Runner-up: #26 drag and drop (2 Needs), #27 focus regions (2), #33 named tones (2, and it unblocks a warning colour for `FieldProblem` and the status mark).

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; to be posted on #19, #18, #22 with the `assumed-decision` label): (1) a section's header is a `Role::Button` (a quiet raised bar with a chevron), not a new look, so one visual language; (2) `inspector`'s rows are fixed when it is added (a `Vec` that grows is read-only text) and nested structs are open `section`s keyed by type and field path; a unit enum is a `choice`, an enum with data a read-only line; ranges from `RangeInclusive<f32 / f64 / i32>` attributes and the step from `InspectStep`; (3) **a toast with a button has that button as its one interactive part** (a tab stop that never takes focus by itself): the crate's rule that a toast takes no input stays for every toast without one; an action toast stands `action_seconds` and `.sticky()` is the persistent form (Carbon holds an actionable toast until dismissed); `ToastAction.id` is a `String`; the kinds stay `Info` and `Alert` (the issue's `ToastKind::Error` is `toast_alert(..).sticky()`); the repeat count is ASCII "x3" (the host supplies the font); the center is a list of button rows, not a custom widget.

**Sources** (`docs/research/{sections, inspector, toast-actions}.md`): craft, W3C WAI-ARIA APG *Disclosure* and *Accordion* ("Enter or Space: … collapses the panel"), W3C WAI *Labeling Controls* ("most users prefer or even need visible labels"), WCAG 2.2.1 *Timing Adjustable* (the toast exception: an alternative that does not rely on a timer); shipping, Dear ImGui `CollapsingHeader` / `SetNextItemOpen` and its ID-keyed storage, Unity `RangeAttribute` (a field's attribute chooses its inspector widget), IBM Carbon *Notification* ("users should be able to access them elsewhere after the toast notification disappears", "Consider using a notification center"); technical, Bevy 0.20 `Display::None` / `Visibility`, `bevy_reflect`'s `#[reflect(@...)]` custom attributes and `NamedField::docs`, `On<Remove<T>>`. Home-still had nothing on any of the three.

**Found on the way.** (1) `ValueChange<f32>` in a test with a literal `value: 7.5` infers `f64` and no observer hears it: write `7.5_f32` (AGENTS). (2) `toast_alpha` treats a hold that is not finite as none, so a sticky toast's hold is `1.0e9`, not infinity. (3) A tooltip beside a row flips to the left over a panel on that side: the inspector example puts its readout to the right with room for it. (4) `Role::Button` as a section header needed a chevron two levels from the button's state: the chevrons are direct children and the skin inks them from `parent.pressed`. (5) A closed section body needs `Visibility::Hidden` as well as `Display::None` or Tab sticks (the first `Tab` test passed vacuously: it must assert Tab visits the other stops).

**Tests.** `tests/section.rs` (11: the acceptance first, Enter / Space / click once each, a disabled header, focus to the header, "Empty", the header's size and contrast in four looks, nesting, a stored state, no id, idle, 200% text), `tests/inspector.rs` (12: the acceptance, every edit kind and its path, no write for an equal value, the change tick seen by `Changed<T>`, range, step and tooltip, the other kinds and integer rounding, the game's writes, a resource, an unreflectable type, spacing, idle, a field being typed in), `tests/toast_actions.rs` (15: the acceptance, Enter, three into "x3" with all three logged, the merge window and what is the same, a merge restarts the hold, merging off, sticky and Dismiss, `action_seconds`, only the buttons take input, focus cleared, the log's 200, the center newest first and empty, 100 entries in a list, idle, contrast in four looks); `model::clock_text` doctests.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g39/{after,i,nframes}`, removed): *Sections*: header chevron touched the title at first (fixed: the chevron's margin), the example's panel re-centred as it shrank (fixed: top aligned); left: the header is a button-looking bar, heavy for a very long list of sections (a lighter "quiet" header is a possible later look). **GREAT.** *Inspector*: the first capture's tooltip flipped over the readout (moved the readout); left: a vec field's label sits close to its first letter (the existing `vec_field`), no grouping by component. **GREAT as a slice** (component and resource, fixed shape). *Toast actions*: the body's measure includes the buttons so "Could not write the save" wraps to two lines beside Show and Dismiss; "x3" is ASCII not "×". **GREAT** with those two noted.

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark): headers, toast buttons and log rows at least `skin.target()` high and words 4.5:1 on their fill in all four looks (tests); ring on the header, the toast button and the log row; nothing leaves the panel at 200% text (`tests/section.rs`); `problems()` unchanged (no new colour).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs): before min **22.24** median 22.97, after min **21.76** median 23.03, paired median difference **-0.11 ms** (the new systems' queries are empty with no section, inspector or centre). **Pixel identity** `magick compare` AE=0 at 1280x720: dark, parchment, 1.5x text, dialog. The examples run at 22 to 24.5 ms.

## Iteration 40 — 2026-11-02 — Focus regions, drag and drop and the text area (#27, #26, #28)

**Triage.** 7 open issues, all `grimoire-ui` (#26 #27 #28 #30 #31 #32 #33), no `bug`. Last audit row (pass 67): `missing: release` (v0.22.0). **The release was done first**: `cargo publish` succeeded this time (the 24 h window had passed), tag `v0.22.0` pushed, https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.22.0. Parked work: only the old `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (ignored). Corpus re-scan: not repeated (no game-UI paper in the last four scans); home-still has nothing on pane navigation, drag and drop or textareas, so the sources were fetched from the web (`## Pending index` in each note).

**The cluster** (ready `G-NN` ordered by how many open bevy_grimoire `ui` issues name each on Needs, ties by number): #26 (2), #27 (2), #28 (2), then #30 (2, Depends #12 closed), #31 (2), #33 (2). Taken: **#27, #26, #28** (the first three by the rule; #27 and #26 are input, #28 is the text row, and #27's host region is what a viewport that takes drops and a notes field both need).

**What changed** (research `0767f3b`; implementation `cd98a9b` (#27, #26), `text_area` commit (#28); docs, example, GIFs `19bc15d`):
- **Focus regions (#27).** `FocusRegion { arrows: ArrowPolicy::{Navigate, Host} }` on any node; F6 and Shift+F6 (`cycle_regions`) go to the first widget of the next or previous region in UI-tree order, wrapping, skipping an empty region, inside the open dialog's scope only; with `Host` the arrow keys move no gooey focus and show no ring (the D-pad, Tab and F6 still leave: no trap, WCAG 2.1.2). `viewport()` (`Role::Viewport`) is the ready-made host region: a frame, the ring while focused, a tab stop.
- **Drag and drop (#26).** `DragPayload<T>` / `DropTarget<T>` (+ `accepting`), `Dropped<T>`, `DropRefused<T>`, `DroppedOnWorld<T>`, `DragLabel`, `Dragging`, `DragState`. Type-erased `DragSource`/`DropZone` with fn-pointer handlers so there is no per-type plugin; a ghost and a mark (the ring's colour when it takes the payload, `danger` when not) drawn as roots; Escape or East puts it down and is no `Cancel`; 6 px lift. **The dock's `DropTarget` is now `DockDropTarget`** (breaking, the request named `DropTarget<T>`).
- **Text area (#28).** `text_area(label, value, rows)`: the text row with the label over a field `rows` lines high; Enter adds a line, Ctrl+Enter or Cmd+Enter is the one final change, the arrow keys are the caret's (the D-pad leaves), Tab and Escape leave. `Role::TextArea`.
- `examples/workspace.rs` (a tool: library, viewport, inspector; `GOOEY_KEYS` steps `f6`, `sf6`, `center`, `type:`, `drag:a>b`), `focus-regions.gif`, `drag-drop.gif`, `text-area.gif`, three guides (`focus-regions.md`, `drag-drop.md`, a section of `text-input.md`), three research notes, AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.

**Ranking.** Pick: the owner's cluster rule. Runner-up: #33 named tones (2 Needs; unblocks a warning colour), #30 shortcut overlay, #31 headless test helpers.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; to be posted on #26, #27, #28 with the `assumed-decision` label): (1) **#26: the keyboard alternative to dragging is the host's** (WCAG 2.5.7 asks for a single-pointer one; a `ContextMenu` "Move to…" is one): options were a keyboard pick-up and drop mode in the crate, or a documented alternative; picked the second, the smaller and reversible. `Dropped` carries `pointer` and `source` besides the issue's `target` and `payload`; a release over an interface node that is no target sends nothing; the dock's `DropTarget` was renamed to give the issue's name away. (2) **#27: `Host` leaves the arrow KEYS only**; the D-pad still navigates (a pad has no Tab) and a widget inside a host region that reads arrows itself keeps them; Enter is left to the widgets (a host region's content is the host's own node). Regions are ordered by UI-tree order. (3) **#28: Ctrl+Enter and Cmd+Enter both end the edit on every platform**; a paste that would pass `TextLimit` is ignored whole (Bevy's rule) instead of truncated.

**Sources** (`docs/research/{focus-regions, drag-drop, text-area}.md`): craft, WCAG 2.2 *Understanding 2.1.2 No Keyboard Trap* ("…the user is advised of the method for moving focus away"), *Understanding 2.5.7 Dragging Movements* ("A sortable list of elements may, after tapping or clicking on a list element, provide adjacent controls…"), GOV.UK Design System *Textarea* ("Make the height of a textarea proportional to the amount of text you expect users to enter"); shipping, VS Code's *Focus Next Part* (F6), Dear ImGui's drag and drop (`SetDragDropPayload` with a typed payload and `AcceptDragDropPayload`); technical, Bevy 0.20 `EditableText::{allow_newlines, visible_lines, max_characters}`, `bevy_picking` drag events and `HoverMap`, this crate's own dock drag.

**Found on the way.** (1) **In Bevy 0.20 a resource is a component on a singleton entity, so `Query<EntityRef>` conflicts with any `ResMut`**: use `Query<EntityRef, Without<IsResource>>`. (2) A run condition is evaluated when its system runs: `cancel_drags` taking the drag away made the later `cancel_by_input` (`not(is_dragging)`) send a `Cancel` for the same Escape; it now only sets `cancelled` and `track_drag` puts the drag down after. (3) A global observer of a bubbling event fires once per hop: a test counting `Cancel`s counted 3 (count where `entity == original_event_target()`). (4) A mutation check passed vacuously for the lift threshold because the test moved by `DRAG_LIFT_PX * 0.5`, which is 0 when mutated to 0: move a fixed 3 px. (5) `cargo doc --no-deps` without `reflect` failed on a `[`crate::inspector`]` link from iteration 39 (the gate there ran with features): fixed. (6) A `NoWrap` label wider than its viewport is clipped, not wrapped: keep a viewport's hint short. (7) The first gate failed clippy `type_complexity` on a query filter (aliased) and a doctest missing an import.

**Tests.** `tests/region.rs` (13: host arrows kept and read by the host, no ring shown, a navigate region unchanged, a widget inside a host region, the D-pad leaves, Tab leaves, F6 forward and wrapping, Shift+F6, no region at both ends, Ctrl/Alt F6 not ours, an empty region skipped, a dialog's own regions, a `viewport()`'s frame and ring, idle), `tests/drag.rs` (13: the acceptance, the world with the pointer position and over a sprite, another type refused with the red mark and ghost, the target's own test, two types on one node, ghost and mark follow and are gone, Escape sends nothing and no `Cancel`, Escape with nothing carried still a Back, a non-target panel, 3 px is a click, ghost contrast and frame 3:1 in four looks, idle), `tests/text_area.rs` (12: rows high and label over, Enter a line and no final, Ctrl and Cmd+Enter one final change, Up and Down the caret's, Tab and Escape leave, paste limit, never grows, wraps, arrows stay and the D-pad leaves, disabled, 200% text, idle). Mutation-checked: `hosts_arrows` and the dialog scope, the lift threshold, `not(is_dragging)`, the sprite branch, `types_lines`, the Ctrl+Enter condition.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g40/critique.md`, removed): fixed at zoom: an Inspector panel running off the window, a 0.22-alpha wash over a whole viewport hiding the placed piece (now 0.12), a clipped readout, two pieces dropped on one point. The theme step keeps the host's tones.

- **Shortcut overlay (#30).** `ShortcutBadges { hold, delay_seconds }` (Alt, 0.3 s) shows one `Role::ShortcutBadge` child per visible, enabled widget with a chord while exactly that modifier is held, and removes them the frame it is let go. `Shortcut` is now a `Component` (a widget's `Tooltip` chord counts too; the component wins). The badge sits inside the widget's frame (the ring is never covered), in the inverse of a panel's colours.
- `examples/badges.rs` (`GOOEY_KEYS` steps `tab`, `enter`, `alt`, `unalt`, `shift`, `unshift`), `docs/media/shortcut-badges.gif`, three research notes, three guide sections/pages, AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG, README (the shortcuts GIF block gives its place to the badges one, keeping 250 lines; it stays in `docs/guide/shortcuts.md`).

(2) #31: `focused` returns the entity, `focused_label` its words. (3) #30: default hold Alt with a 0.3 s delay (Ctrl or Command would flash on every chord).

**Sources** (`docs/research/{tones, testing, shortcut-overlay}.md`): craft, WCAG 2.2 *Understanding 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast*, Testing Library *Guiding Principles* ("The more your tests resemble the way your software is used, the more confidence they can give you"), Malacria et al. *Promoting Hotkey use through rehearsal with ExposeHK* ("When activated with a modifier key (e.g., the Control key), EHK displays the hotkeys in the application's toolbar ..."); shipping, VS Code's `workbench.colorCustomizations` and the Design Tokens Format, Playwright/Unity locators (not fetched: `[INFERENCE]` in the note), Microsoft Office's Alt KeyTips as described in the ExposeHK paper; technical, Bevy 0.20 `ComputedNode`, `InheritedVisibility`, `PointerInput`, `InputFocus`, and this crate's own `GooeySkin::problems`.

(5) `children![]` spawns in reverse entity order: a test that names two matches sorts first. (6) `cargo fmt` before clippy: clippy's `type_complexity` hit a `Query` in the system and in a test closure; alias both.

## Iteration 42 — 2026-11-02 — Accessibility tree (#32)

**Triage.** 1 open issue, #32 G-28 (`grimoire-ui`, the last one), no `bug`. Last audit row (pass 69): nothing missing (v0.24.0 released). Parked work: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` only (old, ignored; the pass-41 park was adopted in iteration 19). home-still and the corpus re-scan were not repeated (XAG 112 and the APG names note are web pages; no new game-UI paper). The one-test-binary job from the owner's 2026-11-01 note was already done (`autotests = false`, `tests/all.rs`).

**The cluster** (ready `G-NN` by how many open bevy_grimoire `ui` issues name each): only #32 is open and ready (Needs 1). Taken: **#32**.

**What changed** (research `6eb8f41`; code and tests `f5149b8`; docs `19f352b`):
- `src/access.rs`: `update_accessibility` (Style, last) gives every entity with a `Role` an AccessKit node and keeps its name, value and state true, **only while `AccessibilityRequested` is true** (a game with no screen reader pays one resource read). `spec(Role)` is an exhaustive `match` (`Hidden`, `Group`, `Node`), so a new role cannot ship unclassified. Names are the words on screen (`ControlText` for a row's label, the button/tab/menu/cell text, a `Tooltip` for an icon button, the dialog's heading); Bevy's roles are kept (Button, CheckBox + `toggled`, Slider + numbers, Tab, TextInput); the crate adds the rest (SpinButton for a choice, Meter, ProgressIndicator, Heading, Label, Dialog + modal, Status/Alert, Table/Row/Cell, Tree/TreeItem, Splitter, MenuBar/Menu/MenuItem/MenuItemCheckBox/MenuItemRadio, TabPanel, RadioGroup/RadioButton, ColorWell, Pane, MultilineTextInput). Hidden: icons, every decorative part, a page not shown, a closed menu, a tooltip not open. `AccessibleLabel` on any node wins. A node is compared first (`says`) and written only on a difference.
- `GOOEY_A11Y=1` on the gallery prints the tree (`gooey: a11y …`), nested as the reader is told it; `docs/guide/accessibility.md` holds an excerpt.
- New dependency `accesskit = "0.24"` (Bevy's version; `bevy::a11y` does not re-export it).

**Ranking.** Pick: the owner's cluster rule (the only open `grimoire-ui` issue). Runner-up: AccessKit actions (Click/Increment/Focus from the reader), which the issue does not ask for.

**Assumed decisions** (posted on #32 with the `assumed-decision` label; owner may override): (1) a choice is a SpinButton, not a combobox (a combobox promises a popup); (2) while no screen reader is connected nothing is built or written, instead of always keeping nodes current (zero cost for players without one; Bevy sends every node each frame while one is connected, so nothing is lost); (3) crate-supplied names for unlabeled parts are English constants ("Divider", "Resize column", "Saturation and brightness", "Command") like "Dismiss" and "More", replaceable by `AccessibleLabel`; (4) actions are a later slice.

**Sources** (`docs/research/accessibility.md`): craft, Microsoft *Xbox Accessibility Guideline 112* ("players who use assistive technologies like screen narration ... can be hampered or blocked from gameplay if focus doesn't move in a clear, consistent way") and the W3C *APG: Providing Accessible Names and Descriptions* ("Prefer Visible Text"); shipping game, Naughty Dog's *The Last of Us Part II: Accessibility Features Detailed* ("our menus and other on-screen text can be voiced using the Text-To-Speech option"); technical, `accesskit` 0.24's schema and Bevy 0.20's `bevy_a11y`, `bevy_ui::accessibility`, `bevy_ui_widgets` and `bevy_winit::accessibility`.

**Found on the way.** (1) `bevy_winit` skips the **whole** accessibility update when the focused entity has no node, so a focus stop without one freezes the reader: every focus stop needs a node (`every_focus_stop_of_a_busy_screen_has_a_role_and_a_name`). (2) Bevy's tree puts a node whose direct parent has no node under the window, in query (archetype) order: structural containers need `GenericContainer` nodes or the reading order is scrambled. (3) accesskit has no Separator role (a separator is `Splitter`); hidden here. (4) A choice's shown option is `Visibility`-driven, so a choice on a hidden tab page lost its value: read the `ChoiceValue`th `ChoiceOption` instead. (5) A node inserted later has no bounds (Bevy's bounds sync runs for a changed `ComputedNode` only): `set_changed` on it. (6) `menu_bar`, `section` and `tabs` children must be `children![..]`, not a tuple of bundles (duplicate components).

**Tests.** `tests/access.rs` (16): the issue's acceptance (a toggle: CheckBox, "Subtitles", False → True after a real click → False), a slider (label, "70%", number, range), a choice (SpinButton, option follows the game, held on a hidden page), a button and an icon button (tooltip name), an icon hidden and a disabled control (both ways), heading/label/meter/progress, a modal dialog named by its heading, text fields (TextInput and MultilineTextInput, name, value follows), tabs (Tab selected, TabPanel hidden), a menu (hidden closed, expanded and read open, MenuItemCheckBox toggled), toasts (Status and Alert), a table/split/section (Row, Splitter at 0.5, expanded both ways), a binding (key, then the prompt while it waits), every focus stop of a busy screen with a role and a name, nothing built while no reader is connected and every name when one connects, a settled interface marking no node changed (mutation-checked: always writing fails with 8 changed nodes), and a host's `AccessibleLabel` winning. Mutation-checked: dropping the `AccessibilityRequested` gate fails the no-reader test.

**Critique and verdict.** Nothing is drawn differently: `magick compare` AE=0 for the dark and parchment gallery stills at 120 frames (1280x720, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`) against the starting build. The evidence is the tree (`/tmp/g42/tree.txt`, 53 lines, removed): reads in layout order, every focus stop named, tab panels hidden but their choices still hold values. Flaws left: a meter and a progress bar are unnamed unless the host adds `AccessibleLabel` (the label beside them is a separate node); a tree row has no `level` or `expanded`; a table has no row/column counts; deep `GenericContainer` nesting (a reader skips it). Verdict: **GREAT** for reading; acting from the reader (actions) is not part of it.

**Checklist** (1280x720 dark): pixel-identical stills (AE 0 dark, 0 parchment); `problems()` unchanged (no new skin colour); no new widget, no visible change, so no GIF: the demo is the printed tree in `docs/guide/accessibility.md`.

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 1280x720, 12 alternating pairs): before min **22.63** median 23.25, after min **22.59** median 23.06, paired median difference **-0.11 ms**. With `GOOEY_A11Y=1` (a reader connected) five pairs showed no difference beyond noise (the system walks every `Role` entity, writing nothing when settled).

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`, merged tree: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features --lib --tests` (691 in the one `all` executable: 61 unit lib + 690, the one failure was `leaf::the_dependency_list_stays_closed`), `--doc` (185), `cargo check --lib` and wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings` (with and without `--all-features`): green after one fix. **Assumed owner exception:** `accesskit` added to `ALLOWED_DEPS` in `tests/leaf.rs` (additive; `ed2170a`, commented on #32); nothing removed or loosened.

### Ranked backlog

1. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue from a reader acted on (a click is `Activate`, a focus is `InputFocus`, a step is `MoveChoice` / `SetSliderValue`).
2. Tree rows: `level`, `expanded`, `size_of_set` and `position_in_set` from `Tree`; a table's row and column counts and a header's sort direction.
3. Drag and drop: a keyboard pick-up and drop mode, a snap-back animation for a refused drop, a drop that auto-scrolls a list.
4. Shortcut overlay: `keyboard_shortcut` on a node from a widget's `Shortcut`; badges for menu items and `icon_button`s.
5. Text area: a scroll bar past the rows, a character counter, a paste that truncates to the limit.

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks`: no change needed (nothing public changed), so a patch bump: v0.24.1 committed (`release: v0.24.1`), tagged and pushed, but **`cargo publish` was refused with 429** ("too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours"): logged once, not retried; the next pass publishes v0.24.1 (the tag `v0.24.1` already stands on the release commit) and its audit will say `missing: release` until then. #32 closed by `Fixes #32` on the merge; a verification comment is on it and a move-the-pin comment on bevy_grimoire#208.

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's own: `~/g42_bin` (before and after galleries, 2 binaries) and `/tmp/g42` (captures, tree dump, gate script and log, mutation backup); no baseline worktree was made (the baseline gallery was built into the loop's shared target). `df -h /home` 3.7T, 143 G free (97 %, no change at the GB level). Leftovers listed, not removed: worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked`, `~/g17s_base` (1.8 MB each, clean, detached at old commits, no `-target` beside them), local branches `loop/iter-42` and `loop/iter-42b` (merged to main).

## Iteration 43 — 2026-11-02 — Toast buttons keep their size (#34)

**Triage.** 1 open issue, #34 G-30 (`grimoire-ui`, `consumer`, a layout bug), the pass's item. Last audit row (pass 70): `missing: demo?` (iteration 42 changed nothing visible; its log line says so). Pass 42's publish of v0.24.1 was retried first: **429 again** ("too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours"); the next release covers it. Parked work: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` only (old, ignored). The one-test-binary job was done earlier (`tests/all.rs`). home-still and the corpus re-scan were not repeated: a flexbox fix in one skin arm needs no new source (the research note's toast sources stand).

**What changed** (`7a70959`): `Role::ToastAction` in `apply_skin` is `flex_shrink: 0`, half a gap of margin each side and `align_self: Center`. Reproduced first: with the message "Could not write the save: the disk is full" (the example's alert, now that long) the body's `max_width` wraps it to four lines and "Show" was 60 px wide where its label and `pad() * 3` need 90, with "Dismiss" touching it ("ShowDismiss", `before/notif.png`). Now the buttons are full width, a gap apart, centred on the toast's height, and the last stands inside the frame (dark 1280x720, parchment at 1.5x text: `after/notif.png`, `after/notif-pl.png`). **Assumed decision** (design default, in the research note's Deviations): margin on the buttons, not the issue's `column_gap: skin.gap` on `Role::ToastBody`, because a gap would also widen every plain toast's mark-to-message space (the message has its own padding), which the issue's second acceptance item forbids; three or more actions are not wrapped onto a second row (the guide says prefer one button).

**Tests** (`tests/toast_actions.rs`, failed first on "a button keeps its label and padding: 60 < 90"): a sticky alert with "Show" and a long message lays out both buttons at least label plus `pad() * 3` wide, at least `gap` apart, the words a gap from the first button and the last button inside the body's frame; a plain toast's body is exactly its mark plus its message.

**Demo.** `docs/media/notifications.gif` regenerated from the example (5.3 s, 800x576, 79 frames; the crop widened to 1000x720 because the log row now holds the longer alert); its caption in `docs/media/README.md` and `docs/guide/toasts.md` describes the alert as it shows.

**Critique and verdict.** Flaws at zoom: the message wraps to four lines at the 24-text-size measure because the two buttons take about half the body; a long alert still reads better with a shorter message or one button. The log row for the long message makes the Notifications panel wide in the example (a host's own list is its own width). Verdict: **GREAT** for the fix (buttons whole, a gap apart, ring complete on focus); the wrap is the measure's trade, not a defect.

**mean_ms** (`notifications` example, 230 frames, 1280x720, three alternating pairs): before 22.18 / 22.46 / 22.34, after 21.67 / 22.28 / 21.87 (a style value in one `apply_skin` arm: no difference).

**Release.** Nothing public changed, so a patch bump to v0.24.2 (`release: v0.24.2`), which also carries v0.24.1's accessibility tree (never published).

### Ranked backlog

1. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue from a reader acted on.
2. Tree rows: `level`, `expanded`, `size_of_set` and `position_in_set`; a table's row and column counts and a header's sort direction.
3. Drag and drop: a keyboard pick-up and drop mode, a snap-back for a refused drop, auto-scroll of a list under a drag.
4. Shortcut overlay: badges for menu items and `icon_button`s, `keyboard_shortcut` on a widget's node.
5. Toast: a measure that grows with the number of buttons, or a second row for three or more actions.

## Iteration 44 — 2026-11-02 — Drag and drop without a pointer; testing::click into an image (#35)

**Triage.** At the start no issue was open (`gh issue list` empty) and the last audit row (pass 71) was `-`. **First job, the pending publish:** `cargo publish` of v0.24.2 (carrying v0.24.1) was **429 again** ("too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours"); logged once, not retried. Parked work: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` only (old, ignored). Mid-pass, #35 (G-31, `consumer`, `grimoire-ui`) was filed and, as the owner's cluster rule puts it ahead of everything but a bug, it became the pass's second slice.

**Slice 1: drag and drop without a pointer** (no issue; the backlog's third item, WCAG 2.5.7). Ranked: (1) this, a drag-only inventory is unusable on a pad or keyboard and the previous note left the alternative to every game; (2) AccessKit actions (a reader's Click/Increment, not asked for by a consumer); (3) shortcut badges on menu items. Commits `a4fbca7` (code, tests, example), `091b680` (docs). `DragSettings { pick_up: Shortcut (Alt+M), pick_up_button: GamepadButton (West) }`; `carry_by_input` lifts the focused (or ancestor) `DragSource`, arrows and D-pad move the mark by `model::target_toward` (distance along plus twice across; centres strictly that way), PageUp/PageDown/LB/RB by `model::target_step` (reading order, wraps), the pick-up key again or South drops through the shared `release_on` (one `Dropped`, `pointer` the target's middle), Escape/East put it down. Only visible, enabled targets that take the payload are candidates. The ghost, mark, `Dragging` and events are the pointer drag's. `not_capturing` is false while `DragState::carried_by_key()`, so the arrow that steers never moves focus (a **breaking** change to a public fn's parameter count: v0.25.0).

**Assumed decisions** (no issue; owner may override): (1) the pick-up key is **Alt+M** (pad West), not Space or Enter as dnd kit defaults: Bevy's `Button` activates on Enter and Space on any focused source before our systems run and cannot be stopped, so a `button` source would be pressed and lifted; the same key drops, as Resident Evil 4's "press the same button again"; (2) a refused target is never reachable by key (a pointer sees it in red): design default; (3) no keyboard form of a world drop (a viewport that takes drops is a `DropTarget`).

**Sources** (`docs/research/drag-drop-keyboard.md`, fetched today, home-still has nothing on the subject): craft, W3C *Understanding SC 2.5.7 Dragging Movements* and Game Accessibility Guidelines ("If I can perform 90% of in-game actions using ONLY the mouse, don't make me pick up a keyboard for the remaining 10%"); shipping game, Capcom's *Resident Evil 4 Official Web Manual*, Items ("select it ..., then move it to the desired location and press ... again"); technical, dnd kit's keyboard sensor (start Space/Enter, cancel Escape, end Space/Enter).

**Tests** (14, `tests/drag.rs` and two in `model`): a focused source lifted, steered (right, down, down skipping a target that takes lights only, up) and dropped with one `Dropped` and no other event, the ghost beside the marked target; the steering keys move no focus and send no `Cancel`; a `button` source is never activated; Escape sends nothing and Back works after; a drop with nothing marked sends nothing; a payload the `accepting` test refuses is skipped; a light goes to the light targets; PageDown/PageUp wrap; the pad (West, D-pad, South; East cancels); no focused source, no lift; a despawned source ends the carry; the game's own key (`F2`); a pointer's drag in flight is not disturbed. Mutation-checked: dropping the `not_capturing` clause fails the focus test, removing the `track_drag` guard fails 8, removing the `takes` filter fails 4.

**Demo.** `docs/media/drag-keys.gif` (7.8 s, 800x450, 140 frames, 139 KB) from `examples/workspace` (new steps `pickup`, `escape`; the Lamp slot now says "Lamp slot: lit" on a lamp): Wall lifted, Right marks the viewport, dropped (the square); Door to Trash; Lamp to the only slot that takes it. README line 122 and the GIF block at 208 (the pointer GIF stays in the guide), `docs/guide/drag-drop.md` ("Without a pointer"), `docs/media/README.md`, CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md updated.

**Critique and verdict.** Looked at a contact sheet of 16 frames and 3x crops of the lifted source. Flaws: no on-screen hint of the keys while a payload is carried (a game can show `prompts()`; the crate draws none); the ghost at pick-up sits over the neighbouring row (the same 14 px offset a pointer gets); the lifted source is not dimmed (`Dragging` is the host's hook). The ghost and the mark are the pointer drag's, nothing jumps, focus stays on the source with its ring. Verdict: **GREAT** for a keyboard and pad path that needs no code from the game beyond the drop observer.

**Checklist** (workspace example 1280x720): ring visible on the source throughout (frames 2-16 of the sheet), mark in the ring's colour on the viewport, Trash and the Lamp slot; hit targets unchanged; `problems()` unchanged (no new colour or role). Gallery stills pixel-identical to the starting build (`magick compare` AE 0, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, 120 frames).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 12 alternating pairs): before min **21.995** median 22.76, after min **21.815** median 22.72, paired median difference **-0.04 ms** (one key check and an empty-drag early return per frame).

**Slice 2: #35 `testing::click` into an image, no window** (`ceae066`, docs `ea6c14d`). Reproduced first: with `primary_window: None` and a camera whose `RenderTarget` is an `Image`, `click` panicked at `window()`. `surface_of(entity)` takes the entity's `ComputedUiTargetCamera`, that camera's `RenderTarget` normalised, and the target's scale (`Window::scale_factor` or `ImageRenderTarget::scale_factor`); `PointerInput` carries that target. Two traps found: **Bevy's default UI camera is only a camera that renders to the primary window**, so an image camera needs `IsDefaultUiCamera` (or `UiTargetCamera` on the roots); **Bevy's `click_to_focus` needs a `PrimaryWindow`** (and so does `dispatch_focused_input`), so with none `click` adds one observer (`windowless_click_to_focus`) that triggers the same `AcquireFocus`, which the issue's acceptance (`focused(&app)` is the button) needs. `press` and `type_text` write a placeholder window entity. Tests (6 new, `tests/testing.rs`): the acceptance (activated, focused), a covering node takes the click, each of two cameras gets its widgets' clicks, an image's scale factor 2 (mutation-checked: 1.0 fails), keys with no window (ArrowDown moves focus), and a widget no camera draws panics saying so. `docs/guide/testing.md` has the new section. **Assumed decision** (recorded on #35): giving focus in the windowless case is the crate's emulation of Bevy's observer, not a change to Bevy's behaviour; a host with a window is unchanged.

**Gate** (`CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`, merged tree after slice 1): fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features --lib --tests` (707 in the one `all` executable), `--doc` (188), `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green. Re-run on the tree with slice 2 below.

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks` (baseline v0.24.0, the last published): `not_capturing` now takes 2 parameters, a major check, so a minor bump: v0.25.0 committed and tagged (`5cb6069`); **`cargo publish` refused with 429 again**. Slice 2 then landed on top, so v0.25.1 (`release: v0.25.1`) is the version to publish: it carries 0.24.1, 0.24.2, 0.25.0 and G-31; the tag `v0.25.0` stays on its commit and is never published (crates.io may skip a version). Not retried in this pass; the next pass publishes v0.25.1.

### Ranked backlog

1. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue acted on.
2. Tree rows: `level`, `expanded`, `size_of_set` and `position_in_set`; a table's row and column counts and a header's sort direction.
3. Drag and drop: a snap-back for a refused drop, auto-scroll of a list under a drag, a carried-payload hint (`prompts()` row "Arrows choose, Alt+M drops").
4. Shortcut overlay: badges for menu items and `icon_button`s, `keyboard_shortcut` on a widget's node.
5. `testing`: `Enter` on a button and typed characters without a primary window (Bevy's `dispatch_focused_input` needs one), a drag helper.

**Gate and ship.** The merged tree with both slices: fmt, clippy, `cargo test --all-features --lib --tests` (713 in the one `all` executable), `--doc` (188), `cargo check --lib`, wasm32 and `cargo doc -D warnings` green; pushed `main` (`71b5eb2`), `loop/iter-44` is merged, `loop/iter-44b` and tag `v0.25.1` pushed (`v0.25.0` too). #35 closed by `Fixes #35` with a comment, the `assumed-decision` label and a move-the-pin comment on bevy_grimoire#214. Publish of v0.25.1 not attempted a second time (429 earlier in the pass).

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's own: worktree `~/g44_base` (plain `git worktree remove`), `~/g44_bin` (two gallery binaries), `/tmp/g44` (captures, GIF frames, gate scripts and logs) and `/tmp/g44m` (mutation backups). `df -h /home` 178 G free (96 %). Leftovers listed, not removed: worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked`, `~/g17s_base` (detached at old commits, unchanged since iteration 17).

## Iteration 45 — 2026-11-02 — The radial menu

**Triage.** No open issue on `bevy_gooey` (`gh issue list` empty; all of bevy_grimoire's `bevy_gooey#N` Needs are closed), so no `grimoire-ui` cluster; the last audit row (pass 72) was `-`. Parked work: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` only (old, ignored). `tests/` is already one `all` executable (713 tests before this pass, 737 after: 23 in `tests/radial.rs` and one in `tests/access.rs`). **First job, the pending publish:** `cargo publish` of v0.25.1 was **429** again at the start of the pass ("too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours"); logged once, not retried until the release step below.

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **`radial(items, selected)`**, a missing widget every game with a pad needs (a weapon, emote, build or command wheel), listed in the loop brief's own rank-3 list and never built; (2) AccessKit actions (a reader's Click/Increment; no consumer asked); (3) tree rows' `level`/`expanded`; (4) shortcut badges on menu items; (5) an inventory grid (a list in two dimensions; spatial navigation already walks a grid of buttons, so the missing part is smaller than it looks). The radial beat the runner-up because it is the one pattern a controller player is slower without and that the crate had nothing near (the `choice` steps, it does not point).

**Sources** (`docs/research/radial.md`, committed alone first as `1b525b2`): craft, the IJHCS 2011 study of radial selection (home-still `10.1016_j.ijhcs.2011.11.002`, "the Fitts' Law prediction for radial selections is essentially a logarithmic function of the number of radial menu items") and 300Mind's *Game UI Design: How Radial Menus Enhance Player Experience* (6 to 10 items, "release-to-select interactions create a faster and more fluid experience", a missing dead zone "causes the menu to flicker between segments"); shipping game, The Sims' pie menu (*Procedural Storytelling in Game Design*, home-still) and Gameface UI's `RadialMenu` (`selectByVector` "Includes an internal deadzone for stable selection", `RadialMenu.Content` names the choice in the middle); technical, *Game Engine Architecture* 3rd ed. §9.5.2 "Dead Zone" (home-still). The IJHCS stem came back untitled and the two web pages are not in home-still (`Pending index`).

**What changed.** `radial(items, selected)`: a disc with the items on a ring (item 0 at the top, clockwise) on Bevy's `Button`. A stick (either, of any pad) past `RADIAL_STICK_PUSH` (0.5) points at the item it faces and a push short of it leaves the highlight; the pointer over a wedge does the same (the whole wedge is the target; a dead circle of a quarter of the radius holds the highlight); the arrows and the D-pad step round (wrapping), Home and End jump; South, Enter, Space or a click send one final `ValueChange<usize>` (even when the highlight did not move), a move sends one with `is_final: false`. "Release to select" is the game triggering `Activate` on the wheel. `RadialValue`/`RadialCount` are the state, `radial_by_pad` (Navigate) and `update_radials` (Style) the systems, `Role::{Radial, RadialTrack, RadialSlot, RadialText, RadialCentre}` the look (the lit item is the selected tab's look via `Selected`, its name in the middle in the heading size, a quiet track ring behind the items), a spin button valued with the lit name for a screen reader, and `navigate_by_input` returns early for a focused radial (`FocusScopes::radials`) so all four arrows and the D-pad are its. `model::{radial_pick, radial_place, radial_step, RADIAL_STICK_PUSH, RADIAL_DEAD_ZONE}`, `examples/radial.rs`, `docs/guide/radial.md`.

**Assumed decisions** (no issue; owner may override): (1) a stick of either side points (a menu has no camera to protect; the usual weapon wheel uses the right); (2) the arrows step, not point (an eight-item ring has four arrows; Right/Down forward, Left/Up back, like APG's radio group); (3) the widget has no hold-button of its own: the game owns its inputs and triggers `Activate` for "release to select"; (4) items are text, not icons (an `Icon` per item is the next slice); (5) the disc is capped at 92% of the window's shorter side, and the labels and name shrink with it, rather than overflowing at 200% text on 720p.

**Found while building.** (a) bevy_picking drops a `PointerMove` with a zero delta, so a test moves by `Vec2::X`; (b) `bevy_ui`'s picking backend reports `HitData::position` normalized to the node (`-0.5..0.5`, y down), which is exactly the direction maths a radial wants with no `UiScale` arithmetic; (c) the first render put the side items (7-character labels) over the disc's edge at a 40% ring: 36% keeps eight labels inside the circle (tested at the corners); (d) at 200% text a 26-text-size disc is 1040 px, taller than a 720p window: the cap fixes it and the test window must set `UiScaleSettings::reference_short_side` to its own height or `UiScale` enlarges everything; (e) the example's status panel and legend overlap the disc at 150% to 200% text (a host layout matter, noted in the guide).

**Tests** (`tests/radial.rs`, 23, and 4 in `model`, one in `tests/access.rs`): the lit item alone and its name in the middle, a game's value moving both with no event and a value past the end; the lit item's accent and inverted text and the others' fill; the disc square and each item on the ring, inside the round disc, a target high and clear of every other at every `TEXT_SIZE_STEPS` step in a tall window and at 720p and 540p (capped); ten short items; the track ring; the frame becoming the focus ring only with focus; a stick facing item after item (one non-final change per move, none for holding), the right stick, the harder push winning, the dead zone, only with focus, showing focus; the D-pad and the arrows stepping and wrapping and moving no focus (a button beside the wheel), Home and End; Enter, Space and South choosing once each, a game's `Activate`; the pointer facing a wedge and a click choosing once, the whole wedge not the label, the dead circle, a press in the middle; disabled; thirty idle frames writing nothing; a screen reader's spin button. **Mutation-checked:** disabling the `navigate_by_input` guard fails the arrows and D-pad tests (focus left for the button); `RADIAL_DEAD_ZONE = 0.0` fails the dead-circle and middle-press tests.

**Demo.** `docs/media/radial.gif` (4.4 s, 800x450, 88 frames, 71 KB) from `examples/radial` (new knobs `stick:<degrees>`, `rest`, `south`): the left stick sweeping round the eight items, South choosing Shotgun, Right twice and Enter choosing Knife; README bullet (in the options-screen bullet) and the GIF block in place of drag-keys.gif (still in `docs/guide/drag-drop.md`; README stays at 250 lines), `docs/media/README.md`, `docs/guide/radial.md` (+ index, widgets and accessibility tables), CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md.

**Critique and verdict.** Looked at the 14-frame sheet of the GIF, 640x360 montages of dark, parchment, high contrast and tool, 150%/200% text at 720p, 2560x1440, and a 3x crop of the lit item (`/tmp/g45/critique.md`). Flaws: rounded pills on a flat disc, no wedge fills (bevy_ui has no sector shape); labels only, no icons; the pointer's dead circle and the stick's direction are not drawn (no arc like Gameface's indicator); at 200% text the wheel's labels stop at 25 px (the cap); the example's legend touches the disc at 200%. Fixed during the pass because they were wrong, not fine: items poking out of the disc (ring 40% to 36%), a wheel larger than the window at 150%/200% text (cap), a heading in the example invisible in parchment (put in a panel), floating labels (the track). Verdict: **GREAT** for the widget as scoped, a direction-picked wheel of text items (functionally complete, whole at every text size, one family across four skins); icons and a pointing arc are the backlog's first item, not a defect of what ships.

**Checklist** (dark, 1280x720): lit text 9.39:1, unlit 10.26:1, the centre name 14.22:1, the ring 9.94:1 on the screen; the track is decoration (1.39:1 on the disc, not a text or target boundary); items a target high; `problems()` unchanged (no new colour); gallery stills pixel-identical to the starting build (`magick compare` AE 0, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, 120 frames).

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 12 alternating pairs, twice with the order reversed): first run before-first: before min 21.386 / median 22.66, after min 21.96 / 23.45, paired median difference +0.58 ms; second run after-first: before min 21.657 / median 23.29, after min 21.678 / 23.86, paired median difference **+0.02 ms**. The +0.58 is the ordering (a gallery run after another on this host pays a hotter machine); the same binary pair reversed measures +0.02; the new code is two empty queries per frame (`radial_by_pad` returns on a focus that is no radial, `update_radials` filters `Changed<RadialValue>`).

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`, branch `loop/iter-45` before the merge: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features --lib --tests` (737 in the one `all` executable, 67 lib), `--doc` (193), `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green.

### Ranked backlog

1. Radial: an icon per item (`RadialItem { label, icon }`) and a pointing arc that follows the stick before an item changes; nested wheels (a second ring) only if a game asks.
2. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue acted on.
3. An inventory grid (slots with an item, a count and a lit state over a two-dimensional spatial walk, drag and drop already there).
4. Tree rows: `level`, `expanded`, `size_of_set` and `position_in_set`; a table's row and column counts.
5. Shortcut overlay: badges for menu items and `icon_button`s.

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks` (baseline v0.24.0, the last published): 0.24.0 to 0.25.1 is already a major step, so it required nothing more, but 0.25.1 was tagged on an older commit and the new `Role` variants are breaking against it, so the version is **v0.26.0** (`release: v0.26.0`, tag `v0.26.0`). **`cargo publish` succeeded** after the 429s of the last three passes: https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.26.0 (it carries 0.24.1 to 0.25.1). The release commit went out with the CHANGELOG's `[Unreleased]` header not yet renamed (a script slip); the next commit renames it, so the published package's CHANGELOG shows the radial entry under `[Unreleased]`. Pushed `main` (`6baaba4`), `loop/iter-45` and tag `v0.26.0`.

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's own: worktree `~/g45_base` (plain `git worktree remove`), `~/g45_gallery_before` and `~/g45_gallery_after` (the two gallery binaries) and `/tmp/g45` (captures, GIF frames, gate script and log, mutation backups). Leftovers listed, not removed: worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked`, `~/g17s_base` (detached at old commits, unchanged since iteration 17).

## Iteration 46 — 2026-11-02 — The inventory slot grid

**Triage.** No open issue on `bevy_gooey` (`gh issue list` empty), no `grimoire-ui` cluster (every `bevy_gooey#N` in grimoire's Needs lines is closed); the last audit row (pass 73, v0.26.0 shipped) had nothing `missing`. Parked work: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` only (old, ignored). `catalog_recent` (limit 200) listed no game-UI, HUD, gamepad or typography document (relationship, trauma and sleep papers), so no corpus commit. home-still was up (`hs status`: 18,673 documents); `distill_search` on inventory interfaces returned unrelated hits, so the sources below were fetched.

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **`slot_grid(columns, items)`**, an inventory: the loop brief names it among the widgets every game needs, the backlog carried it for twenty iterations, and every part existed (button, icon, tooltip, typed drag and drop with a keyboard path, spatial navigation) but no grid of slots a game fills with items; (2) AccessKit actions (a reader's Click/Increment; no consumer asked); (3) radial icons per item; (4) tree rows' `level`/`expanded`; (5) shortcut badges on menu items. Sanctuary's `src/ui/` has no inventory (read, removed).

**Sources** (`docs/research/slots.md`, committed alone first as `a63ea10`): craft, the W3C APG "Developing a Keyboard Interface" ("the element that is to be included in the tab sequence has tabindex="0" and all other focusable elements contained in the composite have tabindex="-1"") and WCAG 2.5.7 via `drag-drop-keyboard.md`; shipping game, Capcom's *Resident Evil 4* manual ("select it …, then move it to the desired location and press [the same button] again") and the Minecraft Wiki's *Inventory* ("If there is already a stack in that slot, it gets swapped with the grabbed stack"); technical, Bevy 0.20 `TabIndex` (negative is skipped by Tab) and the APG grid pattern.

**What changed.** `slot_grid(columns, items)` with `SlotItems(Vec<Option<Slot>>)` as the data (`Slot::new("Potion").count(3).icon(..).tooltip(..)`); `update_slot_grids` spawns, despawns and diffs the cells against a private `Shown` and writes only differences. Each cell is a `Button` with `Hovered`, a ring and a `Tooltip` (an icon slot with none shows its name). **One tab stop by a roving `TabIndex`**: `SlotCursor` is the 0, the other cells -1 (Bevy's Tab skips negatives, and a Tab from a focused -1 entity would start at the first stop of the whole scope, so the focused cell must be the 0 one); focus onto a cell moves `SlotCursor` and sends one non-final `ValueChange<usize>`; the arrows and the D-pad are the existing spatial walk (it never read the TabIndex sign); Home/End (`model::slot_edge`, Ctrl for the grid) are an observer on each cell; Enter, Space, South and a click send one final `ValueChange<usize>`, empty slot too. **Moving is the crate's drag and drop, nothing new**: a filled cell carries `DragPayload<SlotRef>`, every cell is a `DropTarget<SlotRef>`, a `Dropped` becomes one `SlotMoved { entity: the target's grid, from, to }`, and `SlotMoved::apply` swaps (across grids with `get_many_mut`); Alt+M, the arrows and a pad's West carry an item with no pointer for free. Change in `drag.rs`: `DragPayload`'s `on_remove` removes `DragSource`, so an emptied slot is not lifted. Six roles (`SlotGrid`, `Slot`, `SlotEmpty`, `SlotName`, `SlotCount`, `SlotIcon`), constants `SLOT_TEXT_SIZES` 4 and `SLOT_ICON_SHARE` 0.6.

**Assumed decisions** (no issue; owner may override): (1) the contents are a `Vec<Option<Slot>>` component the game writes, not a trait like `Table`'s `RowSource` (an inventory is small and changes by slot; the diff is per slot); (2) the grid is one tab stop with focus on real cells (roving), over a cursor on the root, because the cells must be focused drag sources for the keyboard carry; (3) the grid applies nothing itself: `SlotMoved` is an event and `apply` a helper, since the game's inventory may live elsewhere; (4) no per-slot acceptance rule (a second `DropTarget<SlotRef>` would add a handler, not replace the first): a game refuses by not applying; (5) a slot is 4 body text sizes square and so 160 px at 200% text.

**Found while building.** (a) A fill is drawn inside the border: a filled slot with a clear border drew 5 px smaller than an empty one with its frame beside it; the filled slot's border is its own fill. (b) A second `DropTarget<T>` on a node adds a second handler (`target_added` pushes), it does not replace. (c) `DragSource` is a required component of `DragPayload`, so removing the payload alone left a source that lifted a ghost for nothing. (d) The Style tuple and the Navigate tuple are both at the 20 limit: the new system went in the first nested chain, the keys in an observer. (e) The Tool look's 56 px slot breaks "Lantern" as "Lanter/n" (by-character wrap past a word wider than the slot): documented, not fixed.

**Tests** (`tests/slots.rs`, 22, one in `tests/access.rs`, doctest for `slot_edge`): the grid `columns` wide and every slot square and at least 44 px, lined up, at every `TEXT_SIZE_STEPS` step and the name inside its slot; picture, name and count, and the picture at 60%; filled and empty looks, the frame equal to the fill, the ring on a focused slot of either kind, the chip on `solid_surface`; name and count 4.5:1 in all four looks; the slots following the items (same entities for the unchanged ones, a grown, changed, emptied and truncated grid); tooltips (an icon slot names itself, a text slot has none, a change and an emptying update it, one box); thirty idle frames writing nothing; one tab stop (Tab enters on the cursor, leaves in one press, returns to the last slot, Shift+Tab); the arrows, the D-pad, Home/End/Ctrl; the cursor as state (one non-final event per move, none for a game write, pulled back past the end and on a shrink); Enter, Space, South and a click each one final choice, empty slots too; a drag swapping two slots and the screen following, a drop on itself and a drag from an empty slot moving nothing, an emptied slot not a source, a drag across two grids, a game refusing by not applying, `apply` on slots that are not there, the keyboard carry (Alt+M, arrows, Alt+M) and Escape. Mutation-checked: all `TabIndex(0)` fails the tab-stop and cursor tests; a no-op `payload_removed` fails the emptied-source test; dropping the `payload != to` guard fails the drop-on-itself test.

**Demo.** `docs/media/inventory.gif` (4.6 s, 800×462, 92 frames, 117 KB) from the new `examples/inventory` (a backpack and a chest with icons drawn by distance fields in the example, text-only slots, `GOOEY_KEYS` steps `tab`, the arrows, `enter`, `escape`, `home`, `end`, `pickup`): Tab, Right, Right, Down and Enter ("Used Torch"), Alt+M, three Rights and Alt+M carry the torch into the chest ("Moved Torch"), Left twice. README bullet (in the drag-and-drop bullet) and the GIF block in place of radial.gif (still in `docs/guide/radial.md`; README stays at 250 lines); `docs/guide/inventory.md`.

**Critique and verdict.** `/tmp/g46/critique.md`: looked at the first render, a 16-frame sheet, a 150% zoom, parchment, tool, dark at 200% text and high contrast at 2560×1440, and the final GIF's 12-frame sheet. Flaws fixed: slots 5 px smaller filled than empty; "Lantern" wrapping in the dark look; the coin read as a power button and the sword as crossed lines. Left: Tool look wraps a 7-character name by character; at 200% a slot is 160 px (the example panel leaves a 720p window, a game sizes its columns); the count chip covers the lower third of an icon (opaque on purpose). Verdict **GREAT** in dark, parchment and high contrast; **NOT YET** only for long words in the compact look (recorded).

**Checklist** (dark, 1280×720): slot 80 px square (target ≥ 44), count 4.5:1 or better in all four looks (test), the ring visible on the focused slot at 1× and 2560×1440, `problems()` unchanged (no new colour), gallery stills pixel-identical to the starting build (`magick compare` AE 0, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, 120 frames).
