## Iteration 47 — 2026-11-02 — Motion: panels that arrive, and a switch that turns it off

**Triage.** v0.27.0 had been tagged but refused with 429 in pass 46; this pass published it first (`cargo publish`, https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.27.0, 6.8 s; the audit's `missing: release` is closed). No open issue on `bevy_gooey`; every `bevy_gooey#N` in grimoire's `ui` Needs lines is closed (33 open `ui` issues, none naming an open one). Parked work: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` only (old, ignored). `catalog_recent` was not re-scanned for the corpus (no new game-UI document was asked for; the earlier pass's scan stands). home-still `distill_search` answered (Hicks et al. in the corpus).

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **motion**: nothing in the crate moved but a toast's room and the spinner, so every dialog, menu and picker appeared whole in one frame; `gooey.md` deferred animation from slice 1, and Sanctuary's `src/ui/juice.rs` hand-rolls `Pop`, `FadeIn` and `SlideIn` because the crate has none (read, shallow clone removed); loop brief section 4 rank 4, "looks flat next to shipping games", the first thing on that rung now that nothing above it is open; (2) slot grid acceptance rule and focus following an item after a keyboard move; (3) AccessKit actions; (4) radial icons; (5) tree `level`/`expanded`. Runner-up (2) is a refinement of a widget nobody has reported on; (1) touches every screen a player sees.

**Sources** (`docs/research/motion.md`, committed alone first): craft, Hicks et al., *Good Game Feel* (juice is feedback where the player acts; "the level of exaggeration … can overwhelm the player") and WCAG 2.2 SC 2.3.3 ("Motion animation triggered by interaction can be disabled, unless the animation is essential"); shipping game, *Downwell* as analysed in Hicks et al. ("All player actions lead to immediate visual and auditory response") and Sanctuary's own juice tokens; technical, NN/g *Executing UX Animations* ("100–500 ms … look for the shortest time that an animation can take without being jarring", ease-out, "respect those users who have set … 'reduce motion'") and Bevy 0.20's `UiTransform`/`Popover`. The Game Accessibility Guidelines page and the Destiny GDC talk could not be fetched or indexed (Pending index).

**What changed.** `MotionSettings { duration_scale }` (the one switch; `off()` is "reduce motion"), `SlideIn { from, seconds }` (public token on any `Node`: the `UiTransform` translation eases out on `Time<Real>`, then the component leaves), `begin_entrances` and `update_motion` (Style, first in the first chain), `PopoverSlide`, `model::ease_out` and four constants. A dialog's card (a `Panel` or the palette's card whose parent is the scrim) rises 12 px over 0.18 s; a popover's panel (menus, select lists, the colour picker) opens 6 px closer to its anchor over 0.12 s; nothing else moves. Only the picture moves; the clock waits while a popover is `Visibility::Hidden`.

**Found while building.** (a) **A popover cannot slide by `UiTransform`**: Bevy's `position_popover` is a closed loop (measure the panel's centre, write the translation that puts it at its best candidate) and undid my first build every frame (the test read the popover's own `(59, 52)` back). It slides by its gap instead: `apply_skin` writes `gap − reach` while a `PopoverSlide` runs (`Styled`'s popover slot became `(PopoverPanel, Option<PopoverSlide>)`, `Changed<PopoverSlide>` restyles), which makes a flipped panel move the right way and, since a smaller gap never adds occlusion, never flips because of it; the component is removed a frame after progress hits 1 so the final restyle is seen. (b) The whole suite (760 tests) passed with motion on at first, because a test's six frames last a few real milliseconds; only the gate's run caught `a_popover_is_drawn_only_once_it_stands_at_its_final_size_and_place` (frame 1 drawn 3 px off "its final place", the slide): it now sets `MotionSettings::off()`, **assumed owner exception: one additive line, no assertion changed**. (c) clippy `type_complexity` on the new query: aliased.

**Tests** (`tests/motion.rs`, 12; `tests/serde.rs` one; doctests for `MotionSettings`, `SlideIn` and `ease_out`; a `model` unit test): the card's first frame is partway and every later one lower than the one before, the card is exactly zero after 0.18 s and the component is gone; motion off is in place in the first frame; turning it off ends a running slide the next frame; scale 2 takes twice as long; a paused game's dialog (`Time<Virtual>` paused) still slides; the palette's card rises; a panel on a screen does not; a popover is born hidden, its clock waits, the frame it is drawn it is already moving and a spent slide leaves; it lands at the same centre and gap as with motion off, only along its side, from the anchor; Above, Right and Left start on the right side; a game's own `SlideIn` works and a card's own is never replaced; thirty idle frames after a slide write no `UiTransform`. Mutation-checked: dropping the scrim-parent test fails the screen-panel test; dropping the Hidden check fails the popover clock test.

**Demo.** `docs/media/motion.gif` (3.6 s, 600×300, 25 frames, 202 KB) from the gallery (new knob `GOOEY_MOTION=<f>`; recorded at 3, three times slower than shipped, so a 12 px rise reads in a GIF): the Quit dialog opens, rises, closes on Escape and opens again; README bullet (in the menus bullet) and GIF block in place of inventory.gif (still in `docs/guide/inventory.md`; README stays at 250 lines), `docs/media/README.md`, `docs/guide/motion.md` (+ index), CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g47/critique.md`, removed with the scratch): looked at the Quit dialog's frames 44 to 110, a 1:1 crop of four, the File menu in frames 12 to 18 with a 3x zoom, and the final GIF coalesced. Flaws: the card is full-opacity in its first frame, 12 px low (no fade: Bevy has no group opacity; the open gap); the rise is 1.7% of the window height at 1x, so it needs the 3x slow-down to show in a GIF; a menu's first drawn frame touches the title's lower edge; closing is instant; the GIF is 202 KB. Fixed because wrong: the transform popover slide. Verdict **GREAT** for the card's rise, the popover's opening and the switch (reduced motion is the old screen frame for frame); **NOT YET** for a scrim and card fade (recorded).

**Checklist** (dark, 1280×720 and 2560×1440): gallery stills pixel-identical to the starting build (`magick compare` AE 0 at both sizes, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, 120 frames); card rise measured from the frames' diff bounding box, 7 px over seven rendered frames after the first; `problems()` unchanged (no new colour); targets and the ring unchanged.

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 10 alternating pairs): before min **21.773** median 22.29, after min **21.765** median 22.44, paired median difference **+0.22 ms** (two empty queries per frame; under the 0.5 ms bar).

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`, branch `loop/iter-47` (main unmoved, so the merge has the identical tree): fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo test --all-features --lib --tests` (773 in the `all` executable: 760 before, 12 in `tests/motion.rs`, one in `tests/serde.rs`), `--doc` (201), `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green on the second run (the first failed clippy and the popover test above), 3 minutes.

### Ranked backlog

1. Motion: a scrim and card fade (a `State::alpha` read for any ancestor's fade, as the toast does), an exit for a dialog, a press and focus pop on a button (Sanctuary's `Pop`), and a meter that eases to its new value.
2. Slot grid: a per-slot acceptance rule the drop mark can show before the player lets go, a locked slot, and the focus following an item to its new slot after a keyboard move.
3. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue acted on.
4. Radial: an icon per item (`RadialItem { label, icon }`) and a pointing arc that follows the stick.
5. Tree rows: `level`, `expanded`, `size_of_set` and `position_in_set`; a table's row and column counts.

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks` (baseline v0.27.0, published this pass): no breaking change, so a patch: `release: v0.27.1`, tag `v0.27.1` pushed, `cargo publish --dry-run` green. **`cargo publish` was refused with 429** ("You have published too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours": v0.27.0 went out at the start of this pass), logged once and not retried; the next pass publishes v0.27.1 (the audit shows `missing: release` until then). Pushed `main`, `loop/iter-47` and the tag.

## Iteration 48 — 2026-11-02 — Meter motion: a trail of what was lost, a heal that eases up

**Triage.** `v0.27.1` was tagged in pass 47 but its publish was refused with 429; this pass tried it first and crates.io refused again ("too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours": 0.23.0, 0.24.0, 0.26.0 and 0.27.0 all went out today), logged once, not retried; the next pass publishes, and this pass's change goes out in the version after it. No open issue on `bevy_gooey`; every `bevy_gooey#N` in grimoire's 33 open `ui` issues' Needs lines is closed; parked work is `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` only (old, ignored). home-still `distill_search` answered; `catalog_recent` was not re-scanned.

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **meter motion** (backlog #1's first slice): `meter()` is the most-seen HUD widget and still jumped, so a hit that takes a third of the health read as a flicker; Sanctuary's `src/ui/widgets.rs` has only a plain `progress_bar` (read, clone removed), so nothing is hand-rolled to copy; loop brief section 4 rank 4 ("looks flat next to shipping games"); (2) a scrim and card fade; (3) slot grid acceptance rule and focus following an item; (4) AccessKit actions; (5) radial icons. Runner-up (2) needs group opacity Bevy lacks; (1) is visible in every game.

**Sources** (`docs/research/meter-motion.md`, committed alone first): craft, Pichlmair and Johansen, *Designing Game Feel: A Survey* (stem `10.1109_tg.2021.3072241`: "medium and high levels of juiciness outperformed extreme levels and the absence of juiciness"); shipping game, Hicks et al., *Good Game Feel* and its *Downwell* analysis ("All player actions lead to immediate visual and auditory response"); technical, NN/g *Executing UX Animations* (100 to 500 ms, ease-out) and taffy's percentage margins. **Gap:** no stem in home-still describes the lost-health trail convention itself; the note says it is practice, not a citation (`## Pending index`).

**What changed.** `meter()` spawns a second child, `Role::MeterTrail` (`danger`), after the fill. `update_meters` now steps `model::meter_motion` per frame on `Time<Real>` and `MotionSettings::duration_scale`: a loss sets the fill to the value in that frame and keeps the old length as a trail that holds 0.45 s (a second loss in the hold does not restart it) and drains at 1.5 bars a second; a gain eases up with a 0.08 s time constant (about 0.24 s) and leaves no trail; the first frame is the value; `MotionSettings::off()` makes both instant; a settled, unchanged meter is skipped before any node is touched. New public items: `Role::MeterTrail` (so a minor bump), `MeterShown`, `meter_motion`, `METER_GAIN_TAU_SECONDS`, `METER_TRAIL_HOLD_SECONDS`, `METER_TRAIL_RATE`, `METER_SNAP`; `Meter` requires the new `MeterMotion` state. Gallery: `GOOEY_KEYS` tokens `hit` and `heal` (±40, held).

**Found while building.** (a) The trail is **not** an absolute lane: percentages of an absolute node are of the padding box, the fill's are of the content box (the frame's padding differs). A flex item after the fill with a negative percentage `margin_left` equal to the fill's length starts at the bar's left edge and, with the fill at `ZIndex(1)`, shows only past the fill; the fill stays `Children[0]`, which `tests/meter.rs` reads. (b) My first build wrote the trail's width even with no trail (equal to the fill, hidden under it); the test's "no trail at rest" read 0.6 and it would have fringed the fill's rounded corners in red, so a trail-less meter writes `Val::ZERO` for width and margin.

**Tests** (`tests/meter_motion.rs`, 12; five in `model`; doctests for `meter_motion`): the first frame is the value; a loss is exact in its first frame and the trail stands, holds for the first eight frames and then only falls to zero within 7 to 11 frames; a gain is partway in its first frame, monotonic, arrives in 0.6 s with no trail; the trail starts at the fill's left edge, is the fill's height, inside the frame, `danger`, rounded like the fill; a second loss in the hold does not restart it; a gain lifts a standing trail; `MotionSettings::off()` and turning it off mid-trail; scale 2 doubles the hold; a paused `Time<Virtual>` still settles; thirty idle frames write no `Node`; a bad maximum reads empty. Mutation-checked: dropping the "no trail standing" guard fails the layout test and the model test.

**Demo.** `docs/media/meter.gif` (6.95 s, 466×64, 5 KB, `GOOEY_MOTION=2 GOOEY_KEYS=hit,hit,heal,hit`); README GIF block (in place of `motion.gif`, now in `docs/guide/motion.md`, README still 250 lines), `docs/media/README.md`, `docs/guide/motion.md`, `widgets.md`, `host-api.md`, CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g48/critique.md`, removed with the scratch): looked at a 24-tile contact sheet of the crop (hit, hold, drain, second hit, drain, heal, hit) and a 6x zoom of both ends of a trail. Flaws: the fill's two left corner pixels carry a faint red tint at 6x (the trail's rounded corner under the fill; the default radius is ~0 so no notch); no per-meter opt-out (a cast bar that fills would ease 80 ms behind); the red trail on a stamina bar reads as "lost", which is right but is the skin's `danger` for every meter. Verdict **GREAT** for the trail, the heal ease and the reduce-motion switch (reduced motion is the old bar frame for frame); **NOT YET** for an exit and a press pop (not built).

**Checklist** (dark and parchment, 1280×720 and 2560×1440, large text, the Quit dialog): gallery stills pixel-identical to the starting build (`magick compare` AE 0 at all six, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`); `problems()` unchanged (no new colour: the trail reuses `danger`); targets and the ring unchanged.

**mean_ms** (gallery, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down`, 240 frames, 10 alternating pairs): before min **21.501** median 22.042, after min **21.456** median 21.910, paired median difference **−0.14 ms** (under the 0.5 ms bar).

### Ranked backlog

1. Motion: a scrim and card fade, a dialog exit, a button press pop (Sanctuary's `Pop`), and a per-meter opt-out (`MeterSteady`).
2. Slot grid: a per-slot acceptance rule the drop mark can show before the player lets go, a locked slot, and focus following an item after a keyboard move.
3. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue acted on.
4. Radial: an icon per item and a pointing arc that follows the stick.
5. Tree rows: `level`, `expanded`, `size_of_set` and `position_in_set`; a table's row and column counts.

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks` (baseline v0.27.0): `Role::MeterTrail` (enum variant added) and `update_meters` (two more parameters) are breaking, so a minor: `release: v0.28.0` (it also carries the 0.27.1 changes, which were never published), tag `v0.28.0` pushed, `cargo publish --dry-run` green. **`cargo publish` was refused with 429** again ("too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours"), logged once and not retried; the next pass publishes v0.28.0 (the audit shows `missing: release` until then). Gate: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 785 tests in the `all` executable (773 before, 12 in `tests/meter_motion.rs`), 202 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green on the second run (the first failed `skin::a_meter_fills_by_fraction`, which pinned a gain landing in one frame; it now runs with `MotionSettings::off()`, one line added, assertions unchanged, commit `assumed owner exception: …`), 2.5 minutes. The gate ran on `loop/iter-48`; main had not moved, so the merge has the identical tree.

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's own: worktree `~/g48_base` (plain `git worktree remove`), `~/g48_gallery_before` and `~/g48_gallery_after`, `/tmp/g48` (captures, frames, the Sanctuary shallow clone, gate script). `df -h /home` 150 G free before and after. ## Iteration 49 — 2026-11-02 — Radial items can draw pictures

**Triage.** Audit row 76 had nothing `missing`, but v0.28.0 (tagged, never published: 429 in passes 47 and 48) was the first job: `cargo publish` was refused with 429 again ("too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours"; crates.io lists 0.27.0 at 06:52Z as the latest), logged once, not retried. No open issue on `bevy_gooey`, no `grimoire-ui` issue open, parked work only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, ignored). home-still `hs status` answered (18 673 documents, 488 894 chunks); `catalog_recent` was not re-scanned.

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **radial items with pictures** (backlog #4's first half): a weapon, emote or build wheel is pictures, and `radial` took text only, the one pointing widget that could not draw `IconSource` while `slot_grid` could; (2) motion: a scrim and card fade (needs group opacity Bevy lacks) and a button press pop; (3) slot grid acceptance rule; (4) AccessKit actions; (5) tree rows' `level`/`expanded`. (1) beat (2) because a wheel with only words is slow to scan in a fight and every game with a weapon wheel needs it; (2)'s fade is blocked on Bevy. Nothing hand-rolled to copy (Sanctuary has no wheel).

**Sources** (`docs/research/radial-icons.md`, committed alone first): craft, 300Mind's radial-menu guide ("Using distinctive icons alongside concise labels improves readability, particularly for new players"); shipping game, Gameface UI's `RadialMenu` (a weapon wheel whose slices hold "icons, labels, or contextual info" and whose centre shows the selected item); technical, W3C WCAG 2.2 SC 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast (3:1 for meaningful visuals). **Gap:** no home-still stem states the icon-and-label rule; `distill_search` returned only a VR marking-menu paper (`10.1109_tvcg.2024.3420236`), which the note names and does not quote.

**What changed.** `RadialItem { label, icon: Option<IconSource> }` (`new`, `icon`; `From<&str>` and `From<String>`), and `radial` takes `impl IntoIterator<Item: Into<RadialItem>>` so every existing call compiles. An item with a picture spawns a `Role::RadialIcon` child (`Icon(source)`, 3 wheel text sizes square, 48 px by default) instead of the `Text`; its name stays on `RadialSlot` (now `RadialSlot(index, label)`, no longer `Copy`, `label()`), which is what the middle of the disc reads, so a picture item is named when lit and the access tree still says the lit label. Tint is the existing `Role::Icon` arm: `text`, `accent_text` on the lit item, `text_muted` disabled. The item is a square: `RadialIcon` has a negative side margin of `pad() * 0.9`, the difference between a word item's side and top padding (the first build was a 92×68 pill around a 40 px picture). `examples/radial.rs` draws eight weapon glyphs with distance fields and shows them with `GOOEY_PICTURES=1`.

**Found while building.** (a) A private label component on the slot made `update_radials` (public) fail `private-interfaces`; folding the label into the already-public `RadialSlot` is simpler than exporting a second type. (b) 2.5 text sizes (40 px) looked small and the first glyphs were crowded left; 3.0 with the square item reads at 1280×720 and at 200% on 1440p (parchment). (c) `GOOEY_KEYS=stick:..` needs `plug` first or the stick does nothing (my first capture showed no highlight move).

**Tests** (`tests/radial.rs`, five new, 28 in the file; mutation-checked: dropping `RadialIcon` from the tint arm fails the tint test): a picture item has an image node and no `Text`, the centre names it and follows a key and the game's own `RadialValue`; a mixed wheel keeps words where there is no picture; the picture is `text`, `accent_text` lit and `text_muted` disabled; eight pictures are 3 text sizes square, held by a square item at least a target high, inside the round disc and clear of one another at every `TEXT_SIZE_STEPS` step and at 720p 200%; thirty idle frames write nothing. Doctest for `RadialItem`.

**Demo.** `docs/media/radial-pictures.gif` (2.5 s, 800×450, 50 frames, 28 KB) in `docs/guide/radial.md`'s new Pictures section (README stays at 250 lines; its radial bullet names `RadialItem::new("Pistol").icon(source)`), `docs/media/README.md`, CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md.

**Critique and verdict** (read at full frame 1280×720 dark, 2560×1440 parchment at 200% text, and a contact sheet of the GIF's frames): flaws: the example glyphs are line art I drew in 48 px cells (the rifle sits left of its cell), so the showcase is only as good as the art a game brings; a picture alone gives the player no word until the item is lit (by design: the centre names it, the ring has no room for both); at 200% text the example's "Equipped" panel touches the disc's corner (the example's layout, as before); the item square's negative margin is a layout trick that assumes the slot's padding. Verdict **GREAT** for the wheel's look and behaviour (lit item in two cues, name under the thumb, square targets 76 px, in both skins); **NOT YET** for a picture-and-label ring item (not built) and per-item tint.

**Checklist** (dark and parchment, 1280×720 and 2560×1440): text wheel pixel-unchanged in structure (same tests, 23 existing radial tests pass untouched); picture items 76 px square at 1x, ≥ 44 px target, inside the disc at every step; lit picture ink `accent_text` (same pair `problems()` already checks at 4.5:1 and 7:1 in high contrast); no new skin colour.

**mean_ms** (`examples/radial`, 120 frames, software-rendered Xvfb, single runs): text wheel 22.5, picture wheel 21.5 to 22.9 (run-to-run noise is ±1 ms on this display; the changed code is one query field and one `apply_skin` arm for picture items only; a settled wheel writes nothing, tested), under the 0.5 ms bar.

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 790 tests in the `all` executable (785 before) plus 73 lib, 203 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green first run, 3 minutes. Branch `loop/iter-49`, merged `--no-ff` into an unmoved `main` (identical tree).

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks` (baseline v0.27.0) ran 0 checks (it reported "no semver update required", which cannot be believed with `Role::RadialIcon` added and `RadialSlot` no longer `Copy`), so by the pre-1.0 rule a minor: `release: v0.29.0` (it also carries 0.27.1 and 0.28.0, never published), tag `v0.29.0` pushed, `cargo publish --dry-run` green. **`cargo publish` was refused with 429** again, logged once, not retried; the next pass publishes v0.29.0 (the audit shows `missing: release` until then). The 24-hour limit counts versions: with one publish a pass the queue keeps lengthening, so the next pass should check `crates.io` first and publish before anything else.

### Ranked backlog

1. Radial: a pointing arc that follows the stick and an item that shows picture and label together; per-item tint.
2. Motion: a scrim and card fade, a dialog exit, a button press pop, a per-meter opt-out (`MeterSteady`).
3. Slot grid: a per-slot acceptance rule the drop mark can show before the player lets go, a locked slot, focus following an item after a keyboard move.
4. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue acted on.
5. Tree rows: `level`, `expanded`, `size_of_set` and `position_in_set`; a table's row and column counts.

## Iteration 50 — 2026-11-02 — Slot rules: a slot says no before the player lets go

**Triage.** v0.29.0 (tagged, never published) was the first job: `cargo publish` refused with 429 again ("too many versions of this crate in the last 24 hours"; crates.io's newest is 0.27.0 at 06:52Z), logged once, not retried. No open issue on `bevy_gooey`, no `grimoire-ui` issue open, parked work only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, ignored). home-still: `distill_search` answered but had nothing on drop feedback; `catalog_recent` not re-scanned.

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **slot rules** (backlog #3): the iteration-46 note deferred "no per-slot acceptance rule; the mark does not say no beforehand", and an equipment panel, a shop or a locked slot is in every inventory; (2) motion (a scrim fade needs group opacity Bevy lacks; a press pop); (3) AccessKit actions; (4) radial pointing arc; (5) tree row `level`/`expanded`. (1) beat (3) because the player sees it in every inventory drag, and the refused look, the `takes` filter for the keyboard carry and `DropZone` already existed, so the slice was the missing rule and its plumbing.

**Sources** (`docs/research/slot-rules.md`, committed alone first): craft, Laubheimer (NN/g), *Drag–and–Drop: How to Design for Ease of Use* ("feedback previewing what will happen before the user 'drops' the object"); shipping game, Path of Exile's equipment slots ("slots turn red when the item is either over the wrong slot or the requirements are not met"); technical, MDN *Drag operations* (a conditional drop target; `dropEffect` `none`). **Gap:** home-still has no stem on inventory or drop feedback (`## Pending index`).

**What changed.** `SlotRules { accepts: fn(&Slot, usize) -> bool }` (new, on the grid, required by `SlotGrid`, default takes everything; each cell carries a copy because a drop test sees only the two `EntityRef`s), `Slot::kind` (a free label for the rule), `SlotRefused { entity, from, to }`. `DropTarget` gained a private `judge` that `accepts::<T>` ANDs with `accepting`; a slot is `DropTarget::<SlotRef>::judged(slot_takes)`: the target's rule takes the carried item and a filled target's item must be taken at the origin (a swap needs both ends; across grids both grids' rules apply). A refusal reuses the crate's refused look (red mark and ghost) while the item is still in the hand, the keyboard and pad carry skip the slot, a release sends one `SlotRefused` and moves nothing. Focus follows a moved item (`slot_on_dropped` sets focus on the target when the source had it). Found while building the example: a `slot_grid` in a column wider than its slots spread them 120 px apart (its auto tracks stretched); `Role::SlotGrid` now sets `justify_content`/`align_content` `Start` (own test, failed before, passes after).

**Tests** (`tests/slots.rs`, seven new, 29 in the file; mutation-checked: dropping the back rule fails the swap test, dropping the focus move fails two): the refusal shows while held and clears on moving to a slot that takes the item, a release sends one `SlotRefused` with nothing moved; a slot that takes it gets the move and focus and cursor follow; a swap refused by the origin's rule; both grids' rules across two grids; a locked slot, a rule written later governs the next drag, an item leaves a locked slot for an open one; the keyboard carry skips a refusing slot and focus follows the item; the stretched grid keeps half a gap. Doctest for `SlotRules`.

**Demo.** `docs/media/slot-rules.gif` (2.8 s, 800×404, 42 frames, 161 KB) in `docs/guide/inventory.md`'s new Rules section, `docs/media/README.md` (recipe), CHANGELOG, README line (README still 250 lines), AGENTS.md. The example has an Equipment row and `GOOEY_KEYS=drag:b0>e1` (staged pointer drags, three steps' time).

**Critique and verdict** (full frame 1280×720 and a 16-tile contact sheet of the GIF): flaws: the refused slot's brown fill with a red frame is the crate's existing refused look, so the icon under it loses some contrast (the torch stays legible); the ghost shows the item's whole tooltip line ("Iron sword, +3 damage"), wide and noisy while dragging (existing, not changed); the hover tooltip of the slot under the pointer ("Torch") opens under the ghost; no "valid slot" hint at pick-up (Path of Exile lights the matching slots; the crate marks only the one under the pointer, in Deviations). Verdict **GREAT** for the refusal itself (red before release, status line says why, the ring stays on the sword); **NOT YET** for lighting every valid slot at pick-up.

**Checklist.** No new skin colour or size; `problems()` unchanged; existing slot look unchanged (28 earlier slot tests pass untouched); the equipment grid now sits flush (gap 5 px).

**mean_ms** (`examples/inventory`, `GOOEY_KEYS=down,right,pickup,right,pickup`, 200 frames, software-rendered Xvfb, 8 alternating pairs): before min 22.829 median 24.9, after min 21.724 median 24.0; no regression (±1 ms noise).

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 797 tests in the `all` executable (790 before) plus 73 lib, 204 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green (first run after one clippy fix in the example, `clone_on_copy`). `cargo semver-checks --baseline-rev v0.27.0` again ran 0 checks ("no semver update required"); a new public field on `Slot` is breaking, so a minor by the pre-1.0 rule.

**Release.** `release: v0.30.0` (also carries 0.28.0 and 0.29.0, never published), tag `v0.30.0`, `cargo publish --dry-run` green. `cargo publish` was refused with 429 at the start of this pass (for 0.29.0) and is not retried; the next pass publishes v0.30.0 first (the audit shows `missing: release` until then).

### Ranked backlog

1. Slot grid: a "valid slot" hint on every slot that takes the carried item at pick-up, a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.
2. Motion: a scrim and card fade (needs group opacity), a dialog exit, a button press pop, a per-meter opt-out (`MeterSteady`).
3. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue acted on.
4. Radial: a pointing arc that follows the stick, an item showing picture and label together.
5. Tree rows: `level`, `expanded`, `size_of_set`, `position_in_set`; a table's row and column counts.

**Cleanup.** Removed this pass's own: worktree `~/g50_base` (plain `git worktree remove`), `~/g50_inventory_before` and `~/g50_inventory_after`, `/tmp/g50` (captures, frames, gate script and log). Leftovers listed, not removed: worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked`, `~/g17s_base` (detached at old commits, unchanged since iteration 17).


## Iteration 51 — 2026-11-02 — A plain-key reveal, `BadgesShown` and the shortcut legend (#36, G-32)

**Triage.** First job done: `cargo publish` of v0.30.0 succeeded (the 429 had cleared). Open issues: only #36 (`grimoire-ui`, `consumer:bevy_grimoire`), so it was the pass's item. Parked work only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, ignored). Corpus not re-scanned (home-still not queried; the issue's design rests on the iteration-41 note's ExposeHK source, extended by an addendum in `docs/research/shortcut-overlay.md`).

**What changed.** `ShortcutBadges::hold` is a `Reveal { Modifier(Modifiers), Key(KeyCode), Never }` (default `Modifier(ALT)`). A `Key` reveals only with no modifier down, no `TextEditing` and no `Capturing` row; `Never` holds no time; writing a new `ShortcutBadges` drops the badges in that frame. `BadgesShown(bool)` (a separate crate-written resource, only written on a change) is true once the reveal has been held for the delay, with or without any badge. `ShortcutLegend { rows: Vec<LegendRow> }` + `update_shortcut_legend` + `Role::ShortcutLegend` + `ShortcutLegendCard(rows)`: the issue's optional legend, a centred opaque card of keycaps and words while shown, rebuilt only when the rows differ. Assumed decision (on #36): the field changes type rather than a second `key` field (`Modifiers::ALT.into()` keeps old literals one call away); the legend sits at the window's centre.

**Tests** (`tests/badges.rs`, 9 new, 17 in the file; mutation-checked: dropping the no-modifier test and dropping the typing/capturing gate each fail the one test that holds them): K after the delay and gone the frame it goes up; Ctrl+K in both orders, K then Shift, typing, a waiting row; focus moving while held; `Never` and a runtime switch; `BadgesShown` read with nothing to badge and written only on a change; the legend's rows, centre, columns, opacity, `Pickable::IGNORE`, redraw on a changed list, no writes while settled, inside the window at every `TEXT_SIZE_STEPS` step in all four looks, ink 4.5:1 on its fill. Existing struct literals in the file take `Reveal::Modifier(..)` (`assumed owner exception: the field's type`, no assertion changed).

**Demo.** `docs/media/shortcut-legend.gif` (5.5 s, 688×432, 84 frames, 134 KB) in `docs/guide/shortcuts.md` and `docs/media/README.md`; `examples/badges` `GOOEY_LEGEND=1` with steps `k`/`unk`; README's shortcuts line names `Reveal::Key`, `BadgesShown` and `ShortcutLegend` (still 250 lines); CHANGELOG, `host-api.md`, AGENTS.md.

**Critique and verdict** (full frames 1280×720 dark and parchment, 2560×1440 at 200% parchment, and the GIF's frames): flaws: the legend's keycaps (the prompts' outlined cap) are heavier than the badge chips, two vocabularies for a chord in one frame; the card is centred and not placeable, so at 200% text on a 720p-sized UI it covers the example's panel (it is above the badges, so nothing speckles through, but it is the host's job to leave the middle empty); a legend with a very long word column stops at 90% of the window and clips by `NoWrap`. Verdict **GREAT** for the plain-key reveal and the readable flag (tested, exact); **NOT YET** for the legend's placement (a host-chosen anchor is the follow-up).

**Checklist.** No new skin colour; the legend's pair is `text` on `solid_surface` (already held by `problems()`); card frame `outline`; badges unchanged (the existing 8 badge tests pass with only the literal's type edited).

**mean_ms** (`examples/badges`, 90 frames, software-rendered Xvfb): legend open 23.96, no legend 23.75 (same run-to-run noise as before); idle code is two resource reads and an empty query, tested to write nothing.

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 806 tests in the `all` executable (797 before) plus 73 lib, 205 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green first run (3.3 minutes). Merged `--no-ff` (e7fdd94).

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks --baseline-rev v0.30.0`: 2 major checks failed (`update_shortcut_badges` takes 11 parameters, the field's type), so a minor: `release: v0.31.0`, published (https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.31.0), tag `v0.31.0` pushed. #36 closed by `Fixes #36`; verification comments on #36 and bevy_grimoire#235.

### Ranked backlog

1. Legend placement: a host-chosen anchor (corner, or a parent entity) and one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend.
2. Slot grid: a "valid slot" hint on every slot that takes the carried item at pick-up, a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.
3. Motion: a scrim and card fade (needs group opacity), a dialog exit, a button press pop, a per-meter opt-out (`MeterSteady`).
4. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement and SetValue acted on.
5. Radial: a pointing arc that follows the stick, an item showing picture and label together.

## Iteration 52 — 2026-11-02 — Drop candidates: the slots that take a lifted item say so at pick-up

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 79): nothing missing. No open issue on `bevy_gooey`, no open `grimoire-ui` request (bevy_grimoire's open `ui` issues name only closed `bevy_gooey#N`), parked work only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, ignored). Corpus not re-scanned; home-still not queried (the sources were fetched).

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **valid-slot hint at pick-up** (backlog #2, deferred in the iteration-50 note's Deviations: a player with an equipment panel hunts for the slot that fits by sweeping the pointer); (2) legend placement (#1, small and host-facing); (3) motion; (4) AccessKit actions; (5) radial arc. (1) beat (2) because every inventory drag shows it and the `takes` test, `DropZone` and the refused look already existed.

**Sources** (`docs/research/drop-candidates.md`, committed alone first): craft, Laubheimer (NN/g) *Drag–and–Drop* ("feedback previewing what will happen before the user 'drops' the object"); shipping game, Path of Exile's forum thread *Inventory Background Colors* (slots turn green or red under the item in the hand, and a player asks for more: the slot should highlight before it is hovered; this **corrects** the iteration-50 note, which said PoE lights matching slots at pick-up); technical, W3C *WAI-ARIA 1.1* `aria-dropeffect` ("authors SHOULD show a visual indication of potential drop targets", the effects being known once the object is grabbed). **Gap:** home-still has no stem on inventory or drop feedback.

**What changed.** `DropCandidate` and `DropRefusing` (public `Reflect` markers) on every enabled, visible `DropZone` but the source while a drag is lifted (pointer, keyboard, pad), by the same `DropZone::takes` the drop uses; `update_drop_candidates` (Style, after `update_drag_visuals`) writes only a difference. A slot draws them in `apply_skin` (`State::{candidate, refusing}`): a taker's frame is `skin.outline`, a refusing filled slot is the panel's well with `text_muted` words and icon; the ring wins. **Measured and rejected:** the first build framed takers in `accent`; in the inventory example every one of ~35 slots took the sword and the screen lit amber (`/tmp/g52/sheet.png`), losing the one slot that mattered. The calm `outline` (already held 3:1 on `surface`) plus dimmed refusers reads by contrast in an equipment panel and stays quiet in an all-take backpack. Assumed decision (design default, no issue): framed not filled, slots only, markers public for a host's own targets.

**Tests** (`tests/drag.rs` 4 new, `tests/slots.rs` 7 new; 817 in the `all` executable, 806 before; mutation-checked: dropping the `InteractionDisabled` filter fails the disabled-target test, dropping the source filter fails six slot tests): exactly the targets that take a payload (Piece 7, Piece 50 refused by "small pieces", a light), refusers, release/Escape/keyboard drop clears, a press that never lifts marks nothing, a disabled target is neither, a filled slot a candidate only when the swap back is taken, a rule changed mid-carry moves the set, two grids, the look in all four presets (taker `outline`, refuser `surface`/quiet frame/`text_muted`, ring wins), thirty carried frames write no component.

**Demo.** `docs/media/slot-rules.gif` (regenerated, 42 frames, 201 KB) and `docs/media/inventory.gif` (regenerated, 92 frames, 162 KB; its old caption was stale since iteration 50: it said Left twice reaches the Cellar key, focus now follows the item so it reaches the Gold) in `docs/guide/inventory.md` and `docs/media/README.md`; README line (still 250 lines), `docs/guide/drag-drop.md`, CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g52/critique.md`; full frames and contact sheets of both GIFs, dark only; the other presets are asserted in tests, not looked at): flaws: in an all-take backpack the frames say little (honest); the one fitting empty equipment slot differs from empty refusers by frame brightness only, not a pop; ghost tooltip clutter (existing). Verdict **GREAT** for a calm, discriminating, correct hint; a stronger pop for a lone fitting slot is the follow-up.

**Checklist.** No new skin colour: `outline on surface` (3:1) and `text_muted on surface` (4.5:1) already in `problems()`; slot size unchanged.

**mean_ms** (`examples/inventory`, 200 frames, software Xvfb, 10 alternating pairs, no input): before avg 23.89 / min 23.05, after 23.84 / 22.96. With two staged drags (8 pairs): before avg 26.6, after 27.4 (±5 ms run noise; the extra work exists only while a drag is lifted).

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 817 tests + lib, 205 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green first run (3.2 minutes). Commits on `main` (research 357319d, code eda6bb6, docs 472c219), branch `loop/iter-52` at the same tip.

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks --baseline-rev v0.31.0` ran 0 breaking checks (new public items only): patch. `release: v0.31.1`, published (https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.31.1), tag `v0.31.1` pushed.

### Ranked backlog

1. Legend placement: a host-chosen anchor and one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend.
2. A stronger cue for a lone fitting slot in a big grid (a lit fill for takers when the takers are the minority), a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.
3. Motion: a scrim and card fade (needs group opacity), a dialog exit, a button press pop, a per-meter opt-out.
4. AccessKit actions: `ActionRequest` Click, Focus, Increment, Decrement, SetValue.
5. Radial: a pointing arc that follows the stick, an item showing picture and label together.

## Iteration 53 — 2026-11-02 — Accessibility actions: a screen reader can press, focus, step and set

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 80): nothing missing. No open issue on `bevy_gooey`, no `grimoire-ui` request, no parked work except the old `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (ignored). Corpus not re-scanned, home-still not queried (sources were fetched from the web and the vendored crates).

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **AccessKit actions** (backlog #4; the tree of iteration 42 was read-only, so a narrator, voice-control or switch-access user could hear a slider and not move it, a WCAG 4.1.2 "set" gap); (2) legend placement (#1, host-facing and small); (3) a stronger cue for a lone fitting slot; (4) motion; (5) radial arc. (1) beat the rest because every widget in the crate is affected and the plumbing (`bevy::a11y::ActionRequest`, `Entity::to_bits` node ids) was already delivered by Bevy and unused.

**Sources** (`docs/research/access-actions.md`, committed alone first): craft, W3C *Understanding SC 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value* ("states, properties, and values that can be set by the user can be programmatically set") and XAG 112; shipping game, Naughty Dog *TLOU2 Accessibility Features Detailed* (TTS over every menu) and Xbox narration; technical, `accesskit` 0.24 `Action`/`ActionRequest`/`ActionData` and `bevy_winit::accessibility`, `bevy_ui_widgets`. **Gap:** home-still has no stem on screen-reader invocation.

**What changed.** `src/access_actions.rs`: `actions_of(&Handles)` is the single table of which actions a widget takes; `update_accessibility` declares them with `Node::add_action` (only on a difference, none while disabled) and `act_on_requests` (Navigate, after `gamepad_select`) turns each request into the widget's own event: Click is `Activate` (`ToggleChecked` for a checkbox, a `ValueChange<Option<Entity>>` on the strip for a tab), Focus is `InputFocus` plus the ring, Increment/Decrement are `SetSliderValue::RelativeStep`, `MoveChoice::Step`, `NumberStepBy`, `AreaStepBy`, SetValue is `SetSliderValue::Absolute` or a final `ValueChange<String>` on the text row (a number field parses and holds it to its range through `number_on_text`), Expand/Collapse are `Activate` when the state differs. A request for an undeclared action, a disabled widget, a gone entity or the window is ignored. The plugin registers the `ActionRequest` message (a `MessageReader` of an unregistered one panics headless).

**Tests** (`tests/access.rs`, 10 new, 28 in the file; mutation-checked: dropping the declared-action guard fails the disabled-button and stale-node tests; the tab re-click guard in `select_tab` is redundant with `tabs_on_select`'s own, which the mutation showed): the declared set per widget (and none for a heading, a disabled button), toggle Click once and a disabled one stays, button Activate once, slider SetValue/Increment/Decrement/held to range/words ignored, choice steps and stops at the end, text field SetValue (one final change, shown), number field SetValue 150 on 0..100 reports 100 and Decrement 99, tab Click once and none for the shown tab, section Expand/Collapse idempotent, Focus lands with the ring, stale/window/heading/root requests do nothing. The existing `a_settled_interface_marks_no_node_changed` still passes with actions declared.

**Demo.** None: nothing visible changes (a screen reader is audible, not drawable); a guide section with a table and a test snippet in `docs/guide/accessibility.md`, README line extended in place (still 250 lines), CHANGELOG, AGENTS. Not run against a real platform adapter: this machine has no screen reader; the proof is the same `ActionRequest` message `bevy_winit` writes, delivered in a headless window.

**Critique and verdict.** No pixels. Flaws: Collapse/Expand on a tree row and a menu *item* that is not a submenu are not offered (a tree row is not a `Button`); `ReplaceSelectedText` and `SetTextSelection` not offered; a radial's Increment is not offered. Verdict **GREAT** for the contract (one table for declaring and handling, one event per action); the three gaps above are the follow-up.

**mean_ms.** Not measured: no drawing path changed. With no screen reader connected `update_accessibility` returns at once (unchanged) and `act_on_requests` reads an empty message queue.

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` (one `too_many_arguments` fixed with a `SystemParam`), 827 tests + 73 lib, 205 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green. Merge `6dfdf9d`.

**Release.** `cargo semver-checks --baseline-rev v0.31.1`: no change, patch. `release: v0.31.2`, published (https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.31.2), tag `v0.31.2` pushed.

**Cleanup.** No baseline worktree or /tmp scratch made this pass (`/tmp/aa.rs`, the mutation copy, removed). Leftovers from earlier passes, listed not removed: worktrees `~/g17_base`, `~/g17_parked`, `~/g17s_base`.

### Ranked backlog

1. Legend placement: a host-chosen anchor and one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend.
2. Accessibility: a tree row's `level` and `expanded`, and its Expand/Collapse; a radial's Increment/Decrement; `ReplaceSelectedText`.
3. A stronger cue for a lone fitting slot in a big grid, a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.
4. Motion: a scrim and card fade, a dialog exit, a button press pop, a per-meter opt-out.
5. Radial: a pointing arc that follows the stick, an item showing picture and label together.

## Iteration 54 — 2026-11-02 — Prompts anchor: the legend in the corner the game chooses

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 81): only `demo?`, met by iteration 53's line (nothing drawable changed). No open issue, no `grimoire-ui` request, only the old `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` branch (ignored). Corpus not re-scanned; `hs distill search` for legend placement returned only an unrelated case series (live, answered in 3 s).

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) **legend placement** (backlog #1, host-facing: a game whose HUD or minimap holds the bottom-left had no way to move the Select/Back legend and keep it constant across screens); (2) tree row `level`/`expanded`/Expand, radial Increment; (3) a stronger cue for a lone fitting slot; (4) motion; (5) radial arc. (1) beat the rest because it is a one-line setting every console-style game needs and the other four are deeper work with fewer users. Narrowed from the backlog's "one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend", which is a separate cosmetic change.

**Sources** (`docs/research/prompts-anchor.md`, committed alone first): craft, Microsoft *XAG 112* ("these elements still appear in the same location and relative order": constancy, not a corner); shipping practice, Epic *Using the Common Bound Action Bar* ("Typically, this widget is placed across the bottom of the screen", host places it); technical, taffy 0.14 `perform_absolute_layout_on_absolute_children` (left+right with auto width stretches, so a centred bar needs `left: 50%` and a translation). **Gap:** home-still has no stem on HUD hint placement.

**What changed.** `PromptsAnchor` (`BottomLeft` default, `BottomCenter`, `BottomRight`, `TopLeft`, `TopCenter`, `TopRight`; a `Component`) and `prompts_at(anchor)`; `prompts()` is `prompts_at(default)`. `apply_skin` reads it through the inner `StateQuery` tuple (`Changed<PromptsAnchor>` restyles) and writes all four insets every time (`next` starts from the node's current value); a centred legend is `left: 50%` plus a constant `UiTransform` -50% from `place_prompts` (Style, `Changed`, writes only a difference). Gallery `GOOEY_KEYS` token `legend` cycles the anchor.

**Tests** (`tests/prompts.rs`, 5 new, 832 in the `all` executable, 827 before; mutation-checked: a `place_prompts` that never centres fails three): every anchor 5% from its edges (measured, width % for left/right, height % for top/bottom) and wholly inside at 720p, 1080p, 1440p, a centred one on the window's middle, the default is bottom-left, a live legend moved through five anchors leaves no old inset or translation, a long legend squeezes inside 90% under all six, a dialog's legend with the same anchor stands where the screen's does; the idle test now includes a legend of every anchor and `Changed<UiTransform>`.

**Demo.** `docs/media/prompts-anchor.gif` (76 frames, 5.1 s, 800×450, 99 KB), in `docs/guide/prompts.md` and `docs/media/README.md`; README caption extended in place (still 250 lines), CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g54/sheet.png`, `gsheet.png`; dark only, 1280x720): the six stills show the same bar mirrored to each corner and edge, 5% in, centred one on the window's middle, no panel moved. Flaws: at 720p the gallery's tall panel nearly touches a top-anchored legend (the panel's own margin, not the legend's); the 2560×1440 and other presets are asserted in tests, not looked at; no per-anchor dialog capture. Verdict **GREAT** for a calm, predictable setting.

**Checklist.** No new skin colour or size. **mean_ms:** not measured; per-frame cost unchanged (`place_prompts` iterates an empty `Changed` filter at rest; the idle test proves zero writes for six legends).

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt (one reflow), clippy `-D warnings` (a doc comment moved off `stage_keys` and lost its `#[allow]`: fixed), 832 tests + 73 lib, 206 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green.

### Ranked backlog

1. Accessibility: a tree row's `level` and `expanded`, and its Expand/Collapse; a radial's Increment/Decrement; `ReplaceSelectedText`.
2. One keycap vocabulary for the shortcut badges and legend.
3. A stronger cue for a lone fitting slot in a big grid, a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.

## Iteration 55 — 2026-11-02 — State roles: hover, pressed, held, card and preview edge on the skin

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 82): nothing missing. One open issue: #37 (G-33, `grimoire-ui`, `consumer:bevy_grimoire`), the only request, so the pass's item; bevy_grimoire#210 (UI-02) waits on it. No parked work for it (only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…`, ignored). Corpus not re-scanned; home-still's MCP bridge answered 401 (refresh token expired), `hs distill search` worked but found nothing on interaction-state colour (Pending index).

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) #37 (consumer, grimoire-ui); (2) tree row `level`/`expanded`/Expand, radial Increment; (3) one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend; (4) a stronger lone-slot cue; (5) motion. (1) outranks by rule.

**Sources** (`docs/research/state-roles.md`, committed alone first): craft, W3C *Understanding SC 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast* ("does not require that changes in color that differentiate between states ... meet the 3:1 contrast ratio when they do not appear next to each other"); shipping, Material 3 *State layers* and VS Code *Themes* (hover, selection and widget background as named keys; no shipped-*game* source in the corpus); technical, Bevy `Mix`, this crate's `cie_lightness`.

**What changed.** `GooeySkin::{hover, pressed, held, held_ground, unchecked, card, preview_edge}` (`Option<Color>`, `None` in every preset), accessors that answer from the nearest field (`pressed()` and `card()` = the panel's solid fill, `unchecked()` = `text_muted`, `preview_edge()` = `outline`, `held_ground()` = the panel faded toward the background, `held()` = `text` faded toward `held_ground()`), `held_fade` 0.6 (one fade for both), `Role::{Hovered, Pressed, Held, HeldGround, Unchecked, Card}` (fills) and `Role::PreviewEdge` (a border). `hover` is read by `hovered()`, so every hovered widget and `problems()` follow it. `problems()`: text on pressed and card, unchecked and preview_edge on the panel, **pressed vs hovered by `STATE_STEP` (ΔL\* 4) as the new `SkinProblem::Indistinguishable`**, `held_fade` outside 0 to 1 as `BadSize`. `with_colours_of` and `SkinPreset::of` carry the colours.

**Assumed decisions (owner may override), also on #37:** (a) option 1 (roles), not reserved tones; (b) a `Hover` role and `hover` field added: the acceptance names "Pressed and Hover", the issue's table has `ROW_BG`; (c) `pressed` defaults to the panel's fill, not `accent` (a host's `text` cannot sit on accent); (d) `VOID` is `Role::Surface(0)`, `PANEL_BG` a ladder step or `surface_raised`, `DIM` a host tone: no new role; (e) `STATE_STEP` 4 is a design default (WCAG sets no number).

**A first build was wrong:** `held()` faded `text` toward the *background*; in parchment (dark text, dark background) it stepped nothing back (the capture showed the same glyph twice). It now fades toward `held_ground()`, which also makes a host-set ground move the glyph.

**Tests** (`tests/state_roles.rs`, 10; 842 in `all`, 832 before; mutation-checked: the step threshold, `hovered()` ignoring `hover`): every preset answers every role with its fields `None`, a node of each role is drawn with the skin's colour (and PreviewEdge is a border and no fill), pressed at hover's lightness is `Indistinguishable` and nothing else is wrong, text on pressed/card and the two marks are checked, `hover` is what a really-hovered button draws (real `PointerInput`: a hand-inserted `Hovered(true)` is reset by picking before `Update`), held and held ground come off one fade (0, 1, monotone, a set ground moves the glyph), a bad fade is `BadSize` and never extrapolates, roles follow a changed skin, a swapped role and a scope, a theme step carries them, a skin file round-trips (serde).

**Demo.** `examples/states.rs` and `docs/media/states.gif` (36 frames, 3.6 s, 800×400; guide `docs/guide/skins.md`, `docs/media/README.md`, README line extended in place, still 250 lines, CHANGELOG, AGENTS).

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g55/dark.png`, `parchment.png`, `high_contrast.png`, `tool.png` at 1280×720, the GIF frames): swatches read as named states in every look; Hovered is a clear lifted fill (amber-brown, rose, blue in Tool), Unchecked a mark colour, Preview edge a 3 px frame, the held glyph dimmer on a darker ground in dark and parchment. Flaws: by default **Pressed and Card are the same colour** (both the panel's fill), so those two swatches look identical and nearly vanish on a black high-contrast panel; that is the honest default (a pressed row sinks into the panel it stands on, a card is the dialog's own fill) and a skin that wants them apart sets them; 2560×1440 and 150% text not captured (no layout change, only fills). Verdict **GREAT** for the contract and the demo of it; the identical defaults are the one thing a designer would set.

**Checklist.** No existing widget's colour changed (`hover` is `None` in every preset: `tests/state_roles.rs` and the whole suite hold the default `hovered()`). **mean_ms:** `gooey: frames 60 mean_ms 22 to 26` on Xvfb software rendering for the new example; no existing drawing path changed (six fill arms and one border arm in `apply_skin`'s match), so no before/after.

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 842 tests + lib, 208 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green.

### Ranked backlog

1. Accessibility: a tree row's `level` and `expanded`, and its Expand/Collapse; a radial's Increment/Decrement; `ReplaceSelectedText`.
2. One keycap vocabulary for the shortcut badges and legend.
3. A stronger cue for a lone fitting slot in a big grid, a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.
4. Motion: a scrim and card fade, a dialog exit, a button press pop, a per-meter opt-out.
5. Radial: a pointing arc that follows the stick, an item showing picture and label together.

**Release (iteration 55).** `cargo semver-checks --baseline-rev v0.31.3`: 3 major checks (new `Role` and `SkinProblem` variants, new `GooeySkin` fields): minor bump pre-1.0. `release: v0.32.0`, published (https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.32.0), tag `v0.32.0` pushed. #37 closed by the merge's `Fixes #37`; comments on #37 (assumed decisions, `assumed-decision` label) and bevy_grimoire#210 (move the pin to 86caae7).

## Iteration 56 — 2026-11-03 — Split drag: refusals observable, image-target drags proven (#38)

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 83): nothing missing. One open issue: #38 (G-34, `consumer:bevy_grimoire`, `grimoire-ui`): a divider does not move under a drag that `bevy_picking` reports as in progress. It is the pass's item (bug-shaped consumer issue). No parked work for it.

**Finding.** Not reproduced. Tests added to `tests/split.rs` for every shape the issue names: the interface drawn into an image (`draw_into_an_image`, the pointer's target is the image), at an image scale factor of 1.5 under a UI scale of 1.2, ten moves one a frame, minimums of 96. All pass: `divider_on_drag_start`, `divider_on_drag` and `ValueChange` move `SplitFraction` by exactly `d` over the room. The code path has silent `return`s only for: a non-primary button, a divider with no `ComputedNode`/`ChildOf`, a split root without `SplitFraction`/`SplitMin`/`ComputedNode`, `InteractionDisabled`, no `SplitGrab`, and a computed fraction equal to the old one (e.g. a pointer whose position never changes between press and move: distance zero, which would also read `pressing 1, dragging 1`). A press in the same frame as the pointer's first appearance is no drag either (picking has no hover yet; a harness shape, not a bug). Another possibility outside this crate: the host rewrites `SplitFraction` every frame.

**What changed.** Each of those silent returns is now a `debug!` (target `bevy_gooey::split`) naming the condition, and every move logs the pointer distance, layout px, room and fraction before and after. Guide `docs/guide/splits.md`, CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md. Asked the host on #38 to run with `RUST_LOG=bevy_gooey::split=debug`; the issue stays open until the host's output names a cause.

**Tests** (`tests/split.rs`, 2 new; 844 in `all`, 842 before): the image-target drag asserts the divider's camera renders to an image; the second is the host's shape. Not mutation-checked: they guard behaviour that already held.

**Demo.** None: no visible change (logging only), per the audit's `demo?` rule.

**Verdict.** n/a (no visual change). **mean_ms:** not measured; the added `debug!` calls compile to a level check per drag event.

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 844 tests + 73 lib, 208 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green.

2. Accessibility: a tree row's `level` and `expanded`, and its Expand/Collapse; a radial's Increment/Decrement; `ReplaceSelectedText`.
3. One keycap vocabulary for the shortcut badges and legend.
4. A stronger cue for a lone fitting slot in a big grid, a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.

## Iteration 57 — 2026-11-03 — Accessibility of virtualized rows: tree level, place and Expand; table counts; radial steps

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 84): `demo?` only, answered by iteration 56's "no visible change" line. One open issue, #38 (`consumer:bevy_grimoire`): the host has not replied with `RUST_LOG=bevy_gooey::split=debug` output, nothing in `git log` fixes it, and no open bevy_grimoire `ui` issue names it, so it is skipped (waiting on the host, not blocked on a decision). Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` (old, unrelated). No bug in the backlog.

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) tree row `level`/`expanded`/Expand, table counts, radial Increment/Decrement (accessibility of the two virtualized widgets: a screen reader walking a 10,000-node outliner heard "tree item, Rope", with no depth, no "closed", no "row 5 of 10,110", and could not open a node it focused); (2) one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend; (3) lone-slot cue; (4) motion (scrim fade, dialog exit); (5) radial pointing arc. (1) wins: a shipped promise (WCAG 4.1.2, APG "required when the set is not fully present") with a concrete gap listed as "Not done" since iteration 42, small and fully testable; the visual items are larger and (4) needs a decision on exit animations of a host-despawned dialog.

**Sources** (`docs/research/access-rows.md`, committed alone first): craft, W3C WAI-ARIA APG *Tree View Pattern* ("If the complete set of available nodes is not present in the DOM due to dynamic loading as the user moves focus in or scrolls the tree, each node has aria-level, aria-setsize, and aria-posinset specified"; end nodes carry no aria-expanded); shipping, Naughty Dog *The Last of Us Part II: Accessibility Features Detailed* (every menu voiced); technical, `accesskit` 0.24.1 and its consumers `accesskit_consumer` 0.38 / `accesskit_windows` 0.34 (read from source: `level` and `position_in_set` are 0-based and the adapter adds 1; **`size_of_set` is read from the nearest ancestor that has one, never from the item**, so the set announced is the tree's rows shown, "row 5 of 10,110", with `level` and `expanded` giving depth).

**What changed.** `src/access.rs`: `Wanted` gains `level`, `position_in_set`, `size_of_set`, `row_index`, `row_count`, `column_count`, `end_node`, `tree_branch`; the `Table` arm says a tree's rows shown or a table's row and column counts, the `TableRow` arm reads the row's place from `TableRow::index` and `Tree::outline_of(key)` (`tree_place`); an end node clears `expanded` (a pooled row rebound from a branch must lose it). `src/access_actions.rs`: Expand/Collapse on a tree row go to `tree::toggle` (before the declared-action gate; only a branch of an enabled tree declares `TREE_BRANCH`), a radial joins the choice's declared set and Increment/Decrement are `radial_step` + `point_at` (the arrow keys). `src/tree.rs`: `Tree::outline_of`, `outline` shares `node_outline`, `toggle` is `pub(crate)`. No public item changed.

**Tests** (`tests/access.rs`, 6 new, 850 in `all`, 844 before; mutation-checked: dropping the end-node clear fails the rebind test, a fixed radial direction fails the radial test): a tree's level, expanded and place (A, B closed, C end, E), a pooled row rebound to an end node, Expand/Collapse on a tree (one `TreeToggled`, none for an open node or an end node, D appears at level 2 and the set grows to 5), a disabled tree declaring and taking nothing, a 40-row table's counts and indexes (the set, not the pool), a radial's Increment/Decrement; and `a_settled_interface_marks_no_node_changed` now holds a tree, a table and a radial.

**Demo.** None: no visible change (the accessibility tree only), per the audit's `demo?` rule. **Verdict.** n/a. **mean_ms:** not measured; `update_accessibility` returns at once without a screen reader, and with one it reads the new fields only for table and tree rows.

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 850 tests + 73 lib, 208 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green.

**Process slip.** I applied the first `access.rs` edit with a one-off script instead of the `edit` tool; its diff is the committed one.

### Ranked backlog

1. Whatever the host's debug output on #38 shows.
2. Accessibility: `ReplaceSelectedText`; a table cell's `column_index`; per-parent sibling counts for a tree (blocked by AccessKit reading `size_of_set` from the container).
3. One keycap vocabulary for the shortcut badges and legend.
4. A stronger cue for a lone fitting slot in a big grid, a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.
5. Motion: a scrim and card fade, a dialog exit, a button press pop, a per-meter opt-out.

## Iteration 58 — 2026-11-03 — `confirm`: a modal question in one line

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 85): `demo?` only, answered by iterations 56 and 57's "no visible change" lines. One open issue, #38 (`consumer:bevy_grimoire`): the host has not replied since the debug-logging release (no new comment, nothing in `git log`), so it is skipped (waiting on the host's `RUST_LOG=bevy_gooey::split=debug` output, not blocked on a decision). No bug in the backlog; parked work only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, unrelated). home-still: `hs status` 401 (token expired), `hs distill search` 500 `gpu busy`; corpus scan skipped, listed under the note's `Pending index`. The "one test binary" job from the command is long done (`tests/all.rs`).

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) `confirm(title, body, choices)`: a game's most common dialog took a 14-line `dialog()` + `panel()` + `menu()` + three observers, and the three things games get wrong by hand (which choice has focus, what Escape does, closing it) are the crate's to get right; (2) accessibility `ReplaceSelectedText` / cell `column_index`; (3) one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend; (4) lone-slot cue; (5) motion (scrim fade, dialog exit). (1) wins: "a widget that needs more than a few lines to use is not done" applies to the dialog the gallery itself uses, and it is small and fully testable.

**Sources** (`docs/research/confirm.md`, committed alone first): craft, Microsoft *Command Buttons in Windows 7* ("Select the safest (to prevent loss of data or system access) and most secure command to be the default … Don't make a destructive action the default command button", fetched this pass); shipping, XAG 112 (Halo Infinite / Forza Horizon 4, focus "should always be predictable"; no corpus source names the confirm dialog itself); technical, the W3C APG modal dialog pattern and this crate's `dialog()`, `Cancel` and `return_focus`.

**What changed.** `src/confirm.rs`: `Confirm { title, body, choices, safe }` (a component that requires the scrim `Node`, `Role::Scrim`, `Dialog`, `GlobalZIndex(100)` and a modal `TabGroup`, so it is a `dialog()` with no wrapper; its `on_add` hook queues one command that spawns the panel, title, a body line only when the body is not empty, and a `button` per choice with `ConfirmButton`, `AutoFocus` on the safe one: the last by default, `.safe(i)`), `confirm()` (the free function the crate's widgets are), `ConfirmChosen { entity, index, cancelled }` (an `EntityEvent` sent right before the despawn, so an observer on the dialog still reads it) and two global observers in `GooeyNavPlugin` (`confirm_on_activate`, `confirm_on_cancel`: a `Cancel` that reaches the dialog answers with the safe choice and stops). The gallery's Quit dialog is now one `confirm` plus its legend as a child.

**Tests** (`tests/confirm.rs`, 9; 859 in `all`, 850 before; mutation-checked: `AutoFocus` on the first button instead of the safe one fails three): opens with focus on the safe choice and the words drawn, Enter on it answers `(1, false)` once with the dialog alive for the observer and gone after, a click answers the clicked index, Escape answers `(safe, true)` once, `.safe(0)` focuses and answers the first, an empty body draws no label and one choice is its own safe choice, no choices answers index 0 on Escape and breaks nothing, focus returns to the opener, arrows and Tab never reach the screen behind.

**Demo.** None new: the dialog is the existing one. Pixel check, gallery `GOOEY_KEYS=down,down,enter` at 1280×720, 2560×1440 and parchment, before (the v0.32.2 binary) against after: `magick compare -metric AE` = 0 at all three; Escape logs `cancel: the Quit dialog is closed` and puts the ring back on Quit; Enter on Stay closes it. `docs/media/dialog.png` stays true.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g58/critique.md`, `before/quit-*.png`, `after/quit-*.png`): the card, its 3:1 frame, the ring on Stay and the legend are unchanged. Open flaws: no dialog legend built in (the game adds one as a child, as the gallery does), no exit animation, nothing marks a destructive choice but its order. Verdict **GREAT** (API; look unchanged).

**Checklist.** Dialog unchanged: measured identical pixels, so every earlier number holds. **mean_ms** (gallery with the dialog open, same machine, load 4 to 8): 1280×720 24.63 → 24.37, 2560×1440 100.57 → 94.43, parchment 27.06 → 22.90 (noise; no drawing path changed).

**Gate.** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 859 tests + 73 lib, 211 doctests, `cargo check --lib`, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green.

### Ranked backlog

1. Whatever the host's debug output on #38 shows.
2. Accessibility: `ReplaceSelectedText`; a table cell's `column_index`.
3. One keycap vocabulary for the shortcut badges and legend.
4. A stronger cue for a lone fitting slot in a big grid, a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper.
5. Motion: a scrim and card fade, a dialog exit (now a natural fit for `Confirm`, which owns its despawn), a button press pop, a per-meter opt-out.

## Iteration 59 — 2026-11-03 — `WorldAnchor`: an interface that follows a thing in the world

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 86): `demo?` only (iterations 56 to 58, "no visible change"). One open issue, #38 (`consumer:bevy_grimoire`): the host has not replied to the debug-logging release (no comment, nothing in `git log`), so skipped, waiting on the host's `RUST_LOG=bevy_gooey::split=debug` output. Every other `G-NN` request is closed; no open bevy_grimoire `ui` issue names an unbuilt gooey issue. Parked work: only `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` (old, unrelated). home-still: `hs distill search` 500 `gpu busy`; corpus scan skipped, listed under the note's `Pending index`.

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) `WorldAnchor`: a health bar over an enemy, a "Press E" over a door and a beacon for an off-screen objective are in nearly every shipped game, and a game hand-writes `world_to_viewport`, the `UiScale` division, the behind-camera case and the cleanup each time (the crate had nothing: `grep world_to_viewport src` empty); (2) accessibility `ReplaceSelectedText` and a table cell's `column_index`; (3) one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend; (4) lone-slot cue; (5) motion (dialog exit, scrim fade). (1) wins: "a widget that needs more than a few lines to use is not done" and a HUD is the crate's stated first use.

**Sources** (`docs/research/world-anchor.md`, committed alone first): craft, Fagerholt and Lorentzon, *Beyond the HUD* (Chalmers / EA DICE, 2009; downloaded and searched: "Geometric elements are UI elements presented in the 3D geometry without being an entity of the fictional game world"); shipping games, the same thesis's game study (Mirror's Edge checkpoint beacons, Left 4 Dead outlines, Heavy Rain's spatial elements); technical, Bevy 0.20's `Camera::world_to_viewport` (fails past the planes) and `bevy_ecs::relationship` (`linked_spawn`).

**What changed.** `src/anchor.rs`: `WorldAnchor { target, offset, pivot, off_screen, camera }` (a Bevy relationship: `WorldAnchors` on the target with `linked_spawn`, so the node is despawned with the thing; requires `Node`, `Pickable::IGNORE`, `Visibility::Hidden`), `OffScreen::{Hide, Pin}`, `AnchorPinned { direction }` and `update_world_anchors`, the crate's one system outside `Update`: `PostUpdate` after `TransformSystems::Propagate` and `CameraUpdateSystems`, before `UiSystems::Prepare` (bevy_ui 0.20 does not order layout against transform propagation any more, so the node is on the point in the frame it is drawn; in `Update` it trails a moving target by a frame, which a test shows). `model::{pin_to_edge, box_outside}`. `examples/world.rs`, `docs/guide/world-anchor.md`, README line, `docs/media/world-anchor.gif`.

**Tests** (`tests/anchor.rs`, 10, plus two in `model`; 871 in `all` after the gate; mutation-checked: the system moved to `Update` fails two, dropping the `/ UiScale` fails the 2x test, mirroring the behind-camera direction fails the perspective test): a meter stands at the exact pixel in the frame it spawns and in the frame it moves, at window scale 2 with a player UI scale 1.25, pivot, hide and return (a half-visible node still draws), pin inside the 5% edge with the direction and the marker gone on return, a point behind a perspective camera (hidden / pinned the way it lies), despawn of the target despawns its nodes at once and not another thing's, `Pickable::IGNORE` required and a game's own kept, a named world camera under a different UI camera, thirty idle frames writing nothing.

**Demo.** `docs/media/world-anchor.gif` (800×450, 120 frames, 911 KB, `examples/world`, the sweep run by frame count so it is reproducible): five nameplates riding wandering enemies; "Camp  >" pinned inside the right edge, then "Camp" over the camp as the camera sweeps, then back to the edge.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g59/after/w120.png`, `w330.png`, a six-frame montage): nameplates sit exactly above their sprite (name, bar), the beacon holds the 5% margin (right edge at 1216 of 1280) and its arrow text turns; the bar fills are proportional (0.85, 0.40, 0.62, 0.20, 1.0). Flaws: a nameplate half past the edge is cut by it until none of it is in view (by design, "a bar whose body is half in view still draws"); the beacon is a plain `panel()` with an ASCII arrow, a game would draw its own art; no fade by distance. Verdict **GREAT** for the API and the follow; the beacon's look is the game's.

**Checklist.** No existing widget changed: nothing to compare. **mean_ms** gallery (1280×720, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, same machine, load 3.7): 22.63, 22.56, 24.47 against iteration 58's 22.9 to 24.4; the new system walks an empty query when no anchor exists. The world example: 22.7 to 23.7 ms with five nameplates.

## Iteration 60 — 2026-11-03 — `Cooldown`: a timer on any widget, drawn as a radial sweep

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 87): nothing missing (`-`). One open issue, #38 (`consumer:bevy_grimoire`): still no reply from the host to the debug-logging release (no comment, nothing in `git log`), skipped, waiting on `RUST_LOG=bevy_gooey::split=debug` output. Parked branches: `wiggum/parked-pass-23-20261030-162214` is old (iteration 17 era), stale, ignored. home-still: the CLI answered `500 gpu busy: yielded to bevy_grimoire@big`, the MCP server was not connected, so no `catalog_recent` re-scan and no `distill_search`; sources read directly and listed under the note's `## Pending index`.

**Pick.** Ranked: (1) `Cooldown`: an ability, a reload or a potion with a delay is on every action game's hotbar, and a game hand-writes the conic sweep, the seconds, the clock and the "ready" event each time (grep for `cooldown` in `src` was empty); the backlog's other items were accessibility corners (`ReplaceSelectedText`, `column_index`), a keycap vocabulary, a lone-slot cue; (2) accessibility `ReplaceSelectedText` / cell `column_index`; (3) one keycap vocabulary for badges and legend; (4) a locked-slot look, a stack-merge helper; (5) motion: a scrim and card fade, a dialog exit.

**Sources** (`docs/research/cooldown.md`, committed alone first): craft, Accessible Game Design, *Status Effects: Cooldowns* ("Any time you have a timer in a game, think about representing it visually, such as a bar or clock … without having to count or read anything"; "Skills generally have three statuses: available, in use, or on cooldown"); shipping games, Xbox Accessibility Guideline 103 (Sea of Thieves and Forza Horizon: "If a change of color (graying) is used to inform the player of the existence and state of a control that isn't available, another method is also used") and XAG 106 (Gears 5 narrates the seconds left), Team Fortress 2's reload as a cooldown with its own sound; technical, Bevy 0.20 `ConicGradient` / `AngularColorStop` (a hard-stop pie, clipped by the node's corners, no material).

**What changed.** `src/cooldown.rs`: `Cooldown { duration, remaining }` (`new`, `start`, `is_ready`, `fraction`; on any positioning-context node; its hooks keep one hidden `Role::Cooldown` cover child exactly while it exists, holding a `Role::CooldownText` chip), `CooldownReady { entity }` and `update_cooldowns` (Style: counts down on game `Time`, writes the cover only on a difference). The sweep is a conic gradient of hard stops from twelve o'clock clockwise, a thin hand at its edge, the skin's `background` at 66% over the rest; the seconds are whole numbers from 3 s, tenths under (`model::{cooldown_text, cooldown_words, cooldown_sweep}`); the widget's accessible name carries them ("Fireball, 6 seconds remaining"). `problems()` gained `"text on cooldown chip"` (the worse of the chip over black and over white, 4.5:1 and 7:1 in high contrast). Two new `Role` variants (a minor release). `examples/hotbar.rs`, `docs/guide/cooldowns.md`, README line, `docs/media/cooldown.gif`, AGENTS entry, CHANGELOG.

**Tests** (`tests/cooldown.rs`, 16; 885 in `all` after; mutation-checked: `skin_of` ignoring the scope, no `CooldownReady`, the sweep reversed, no name suffix, a tick while paused): a ready widget's hidden cover and empty chip; a started one dims fully and reads "8"; the wedge a quarter turn after two seconds and the chip equal to `cooldown_text(remaining)`; hidden and one `CooldownReady` at the end, none for a game's own zero; a paused game holds it; restart; removal in the same flush and one cover back after a re-insert; no layout change (the widget's size is the same running); the cover is the widget's padding box and the chip sits inside it; pointer goes through; the dim follows a `SkinScope` (the first build dimmed in `solid_surface`: 1.1:1 against the clear slot in parchment, found in the captures, moved to `background`: 2.75:1); the accessible name; nothing written while ready and the chip's text not rewritten within a second; `problems()` empty in all four presets and a bad text colour reported; the words.

**Demo.** `docs/media/cooldown.gif` (800×480, 108 frames, 599 KB, 7.2 s): four slots cast 0.4 s apart sweep clear one after another, the seconds go to tenths, the line under the bar says "Dash ready", "Frost nova ready", "Fireball ready", "Heal ready" (checked frame by frame).

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g60/critique.md`, `after/zall.png`, three skins at 3x): flaws: in dark and high contrast the dim/clear step is only 1.36:1 and 1.21:1 (a dark skin cannot dim below its background; the hand, the chip and the shape carry it, and XAG forbids hue alone); in parchment the dim hides a dark icon almost entirely (2.75:1 step); the hand is 1 px at 1x near the middle; no "ready" flash (the cover just goes; the game plays a sound on `CooldownReady`); the icon faintly shows through the chip. Verdict **GREAT** for the API and the three cues (shape, hand, number) in all presets; the dark step is named.

**Checklist.** Nothing existing changed (the cover is a child that exists only on a widget with the component): the gallery draws pixel-identical by construction. Chip contrast (all four presets) ≥ 4.5:1 by `problems()`. Slots 80 px at 1x. **mean_ms** gallery (1280×720, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, six alternating pairs, load 8 to 11, so ±5 ms noise): before (v0.32.4) 23.6 to 30.2 (median 26.5), after 25.2 to 32.1 (median 27.0); the new system walks a query that is empty in the gallery. The hotbar's own `mean_ms` prints 33.333 (the capture's fixed clock).
