## Iteration 62 — 2026-11-04 — `hold_button`: a button that confirms only when held, and the setting that makes it a tap

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 89): `demo?` missing, met by the iteration-61 log line ("Demo. None: the change is not visible by construction"). No open issue in `bevy-coven/bevy_gooey` (#38 is closed: the host's own fixes), no open `grimoire-ui` request, no `bug` issue; parked branches: only the stale `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…`, ignored. home-still was down (`hs status`: refresh token expired), so the three source kinds were read directly (listed in the note's `Pending index`).

**Ranked candidates.** (1) `hold_button`: hold-to-confirm is the standard guard for delete, quit-without-saving and abandon-run, and the crate had only `confirm()` (a dialog, one more screen) and a plain button. (2) A "ready" flash for `Cooldown`. (3) An option row for `HoldSettings`/`MotionSettings`. (4) Accessibility: `ReplaceSelectedText`. (5) One keycap vocabulary for badges and legend. (1) beats (2) because it is a missing widget a shipping game's menu has, and it carries an accessibility obligation (XAG 107) no host would build right by hand.

**What changed.** `hold_button(label, seconds)` (`src/hold.rs`, `src/widgets.rs`): a `Role::Button` root over a column of its label and a meter-shaped bar (`Role::HoldTrack`, `Role::HoldFill`, styled in `apply_skin` as the meter's frame and fill). **Not a `bevy_ui_widgets::Button`**: Bevy's `Button` sends `Activate` on a key's down edge and on a click, so a hold on it would confirm on a tap; the widget sends only `HoldConfirmed` (and one `UiFeedbackKind::Activate`). `hold_by_input` (Navigate, after `gamepad_select`) holds by Enter/Space/South on a focused button (a fresh press: Enter still down from opening a dialog never holds) and by the primary pointer button over it (entity observers; leaving the button ends it). `model::hold_step` rises on `Time<Real>`, unwinds 4x faster, resumes from where it stands, confirms once. `HoldSettings { duration_scale }` (`tap()` at 0: the confirm is on the UP event, XAG 107's "single input press instead of an input hold" and its up-event rule). AccessKit Click confirms at once (a screen reader cannot hold). `model::{hold_seconds, hold_step, HOLD_UNWIND_RATE, HOLD_SCALE_MAX}`.

**Sources.** Craft: Microsoft XAG 107 *Input* (holds, up-event activation, "a single input press instead of an input hold") and Game Accessibility Guidelines *Avoid / provide alternatives to requiring buttons to be held down*. Shipping games: XAG 107's examples The Long Dark ("converts all press and hold actions into press actions") and Gears 5 (press and hold or quick taps), Capcom's Resident Evil 4 manual (a gauge that fills while the button is down). Technical: Bevy 0.20 `bevy_ui_widgets::Button` source (why it cannot be one), `bevy_picking` pointer events. Note `docs/research/hold-button.md`.

**Tests** (`tests/hold.rs`, 20, on a 0.1 s clock; real keyboard, pointer and gamepad messages): key held the whole time confirms once and the bar stays full until let go, early release unwinds and confirms nothing, unwind 4x and resume, a stale Enter never holds, focus leaving ends a key hold, another widget's Enter does not hold it, South holds and sends no `Activate`, a pointer holds while over the button, a click is not a hold, the pointer leaving cancels, key plus pointer survive one letting go, a half scale halves the time, tap mode on the up event of a key, of a click (and not of one the pointer left), a tap cancelled by focus leaving, disabled never starts and one disabled mid-hold ends, AccessKit Click confirms at once and the node is a named button, one Activate feedback at the confirm and none at the press, the bar's width follows the progress and thirty idle frames write nothing, the button is at least 44 px with the track inside it. Mutation-checked: the fresh-press rule (`enter_already_down…` fails), the hover cancel (`the_pointer_leaving…` fails), the `spent` guard (5 fail).

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g62/critique.md`, `/tmp/g62/after/{sheet,skins,large,gifsheet}.png`). Zoomed 2x at the dark skin: label centred over a full-width bar, the amber fill's left end flat against the track's rounded frame (the meter's own look), ring and bar coexist with the label about 8 px clear. Parchment (dark red fill), high contrast and 200% text read; the Tool look's blue fill on its grey track has the least contrast of the four and is the meter's pair. The hold button is 1.36x a plain button's height (the bar's room). Flaws left: no "ready" flash at the confirm; no cursor change. Verdict: GREAT.

**Checklist and mean_ms.** Hold button 44+ px high at 1280x720 (test); bar 10 px (the skin's `meter_height`); no new colour. Gallery `mean_ms`, three alternating runs before/after (the gallery draws no hold button; the new systems are one query each): before 26.03, 26.10, 39.80; after 25.91, 31.60, 35.21 (the host was noisy; the minimum is 26.03 against 25.91). `examples/hold` runs on a 1/30 s clock so its `mean_ms` prints 33.333.

**Demo and docs.** `docs/media/hold.gif` (6.5 s, 780x558, 97 frames, 92 KB) in `docs/guide/hold-buttons.md` and `docs/media/README.md`; README line in the navigation bullet; CHANGELOG, AGENTS.md, research note, rustdoc.

### Ranked backlog

1. Whatever a host reports on `hold_button` (a cursor change while held; a "ready" cue on the confirm).
2. An `OptionRow` for `HoldSettings` ("Hold to confirm: Normal, Short, Tap") next to the text-size and theme rows, so a game wires the setting with one line.
3. A "ready" cue for `Cooldown` (a one-frame flash under `MotionSettings`), and a charges variant.
4. Accessibility: `ReplaceSelectedText`; a table cell's `column_index`.
5. One keycap vocabulary for the shortcut badges and legend.

## Iteration 63 — 2026-11-06 — A keycap's words are centred in it (#44)

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 90): nothing missing (`-`). Two `bug` issues were filed today, both `consumer:bevy_grimoire`: **#44** (12:33Z) a `PromptKey` keycap's letter is not centred in its box, and **#45** (13:06Z) Mac chord glyphs draw as tofu in a host whose face lacks them. A bug outranks every other kind of work, and #44 is the older, so #44 is this pass's item; #45 is recorded as the first item of the next pass's backlog. #40–#43 are open `enhancement` requests and are untouched. Parked branches: only the stale `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…`, ignored (its files conflict with `main` and the item it held shipped in iteration 41). home-still: distill is up, scribe's GPU backend yielded to the local model, so the three source kinds were read directly.

**Ranked candidates.** (1) #44, a filed bug with a measured repro and a host waiting. (2) #45, the other filed bug (tofu chord glyphs). (3) The `HoldSettings` option row from the last backlog. (4) A "ready" flash for `Cooldown`. (5) One keycap vocabulary for badges and legend. (1) beats (2) only on being older: both are real, #44 is the smaller and fully measured.

**The issue's proposed fix is wrong, and the measurement says why.** #44 asks for `justify_content = Center` + `align_items = Center` on the cap, citing the other centred roles in `apply_skin`. I applied that and measured it: **no change at all** — same node size, same glyph x. A keycap is not a container: it *is* a `Text` node, and `JustifyContent` lays out a node's *children*, of which it has none. The issue's own diagnosis (that `Justify::Center` centres within the run rather than the box) is right, but its fix does not reach it.

**Root cause, measured.** Bevy 0.20's `measure_text_system` gives a `LineBreak::NoWrap` text node `NodeMeasure::Fixed(max)`, and `text_system` then lays it out against `TextBounds::UNBOUNDED`. Parley's `align_impl` centres a line by distributing `line_width - advance` — of an unbounded width there is no free space, so `Justify::Center` is **dead code under `NoWrap`**. Measured on an isolated `Text` 100 px wide: `NoWrap` + `Justify::Center` and `NoWrap` + `Justify::Left` put the glyph at the same x (6.0); `WordBoundary` + `Justify::Center` puts it at 50.0. That is the whole bug: the cap's `min_width` is a square, the run is pinned left, and every pixel of slack lands on the right.

**Why `WordBoundary` was rejected.** Switching the three keycap `TextLayout`s to `LineBreak::WordBoundary` centres them exactly, and the centring test passed. It also **wraps** a cap whose name has a space: `Node` 140x38 with `NoWrap` for "Left Click" became 39x62 on two lines, and `D-pad Up` went to three. AGENTS.md records this exact float-hair wrap trap from the large-text work. Two lines in a legend is a worse defect than 3 px off-centre, so `NoWrap` stays.

**What changed.** `centre_keycaps` (`src/prompt.rs`, public, in `PostUpdate` at `UiSystems::PostLayout` after `bevy::ui::widget::text_system`, registered beside `anchor::add_systems`): for every `Role::PromptKey`/`PromptButton`, if the cap's content box is wider than the shaped run, it widens the horizontal padding by half the difference — which makes the content box exactly the run's width, so the run is centred **by arithmetic** rather than by alignment. It writes only a difference (`node.padding.left != next`), skips a cap with no shaped run yet (`text.size.x <= 0.0`, no font loaded), and is a read a frame once settled. It is the same shape as `place_prompts`: a layout-time correction the crate cannot express in `apply_skin`, because the run's width is not known until Bevy has shaped it.

**Tests** (`tests/prompts.rs`, 2 new, both mutation-checked): `a_keycaps_character_is_centred_in_its_box_whichever_cap_it_wears` reads the laid-out glyphs against the node's content box at `text_scale` 1.0, 1.5 and 2.0, for a single key letter and for a chord wide enough to fill its cap, and asserts the run's middle is within 0.5 px of the node's middle. Reverting the system makes it fail with `"X" at 1x is -3 px off the middle of its cap, in a Vec2(38.0, 39.0) box`. `a_keycap_with_two_words_stays_on_one_line_and_keeps_its_square` holds the line that `WordBoundary` broke: "Left Click" and "Num Enter" at 1280x720 and 640x360 stay on one line and keep the cap's height (mutation-checked against the `WordBoundary` build, which fails it). The existing 20 prompts tests pass unchanged.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g63/{before,after}/*.png`, `compare_pad.png`). Measured on the rendered gallery at 1x, not by eye: on the pad legend's "A" the ink's centre sat **3.0 px left** of the cap's middle before and **0.0 px** after; on "B", 4.5 px before and 0.5 px after. The keyboard's "Enter" and "Esc" were already all-but-centred (their runs fill their square), so the fix is invisible there and the pad's circles are the honest test: they were the worst case, and they now read centred at 3x zoom. Nothing else moved: the legend's width, height, word spacing and the gap between caps are unchanged, because the padding is taken out of slack the cap already had. Flaws left: none that this pass introduced. Verdict: GREAT.

**Checklist and mean_ms.** The keycap's ink is centred to 0.5 px at 1x, 1.5x and 2x text (test); the cap is a square or a circle and the words are inside it (unchanged); contrast unchanged (`the_hints_read_in_every_preset`, three presets); hit targets are unchanged and a prompt is still no tab stop and no pointer target; one spacing scale, unchanged. Gallery `mean_ms`, three alternating runs at 120 frames: before 22.76, 22.81, 22.80; after 22.88, 22.56, 22.74. Minimum 22.76 against 22.56: no regression (the new system is one query over the caps that exist).

**Demo and docs.** `docs/media/prompts.gif` **regenerated** (the shipped file was captured before the fix and showed the off-centre letters): 7.7 s, 920x490, 115 frames, same crop and staging as before, verified against its caption (pad's X turns the caps round to "A"/"B", Q turns them back, the Quit dialog's own legend over the scrim). `docs/media/README.md` row updated (captured 2026-11-06, shows 0.36.0). `docs/guide/prompts.md` says the words are centred whichever cap they are on; `docs/research/prompts.md` gains the validation target, the measured before/after and a `Deviations` entry on why the fix is arithmetic on the padding rather than text alignment; CHANGELOG `## [Unreleased]` → `### Fixed`; rustdoc on `centre_keycaps`. README: no line changed, because the sentence already describes the legend and a centring correction is not a feature.

### Ranked backlog

1. **#45**: Mac chord glyphs (⌘ ⌃ ⌥ ↩ ⎋ ⇥) draw as tofu in a host whose face lacks them — a `Words` spelling the host can ask for. Filed bug, host waiting.
2. The `HoldSettings` option row ("Hold to confirm: Normal, Short, Tap") next to the text-size and theme rows.
3. A "ready" cue for `Cooldown` (a one-frame flash under `MotionSettings`), and a charges variant.
4. One keycap vocabulary for the shortcut badges and legend (the legend and a badge draw the same chord two ways today).
5. Accessibility: `ReplaceSelectedText`; a table cell's `column_index`.

**Gate (iteration 63).** `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4`, sccache off (`CARGO_BUILD_RUSTC_WRAPPER=`): fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 984 tests in `all` + lib (982 + 2 new), 222 doctests, check lib, wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`: green. **`main` had a broken `cargo fmt --check` before this pass** — `tests/split.rs` from iteration 62's split work was committed unformatted (`cargo fmt --check` fails on `main` at `1cc4c7d`), so this pass's `cargo fmt` reformatted it; that is the only change to a file this pass did not otherwise touch.

**Release (iteration 63).** `cargo semver-checks --baseline-rev v0.35.0`: 202 checks pass, no semver update required (only a new public fn), so a PATCH bump: `release: v0.35.1` (README compatibility row `0.1 – 0.35.1`), published (https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.35.1), tag `v0.35.1` pushed, `cargo info bevy_gooey@0.35.1` resolves. #44 closed with the merged sha and the version.

## Hand-driven pass — 2026-11-06 — toast inset (#43), primary button (#40), card grid (#41), vertical tabs (#42)

**Hand-driven, owner request 2026-11-06, host big_mac.** Not a loop iteration: no `steps.py`, `loop-stats` or audit bookkeeping. The owner asked each Bevy Coven repo's session to take its top open issues in a given order; this one was #43, #40, #41, #42 (all `consumer:bevy_grimoire`). #45 (Mac chord glyphs) was left to the agent on big that owns it.

- **Toast inset (#43).** `ToastSettings::inset: ToastInset { top, right, bottom, left }` (logical px). The stack was a 100% x 100% node at the window's corner with only the skin's padding, so a `TopCenter` toast drew over an app's menu bar. It is now an absolute node whose four sides are the inset, written by `update_toasts` only on a difference. Tests in `tests/toast.rs` (4, mutation-checked: with the sides forced to zero, 3 fail): a `TopCenter` stack under a 34 px bar puts the toast's top at 34 + the stack padding, the default stays at the edge, 34 logical px are 51 window px at `UiScale` 1.5, a bottom/right inset, a rewritten resource followed, NaN and negative sides keep no room. Assumed decision on the issue: four f32 sides, not `UiRect` (`Val::Auto` has no meaning for an inset).
- **Primary button (#40).** `primary_button(text)` = `button(text)` + `PrimaryButton` marker: accent at rest, `accent_text` ink, `primary_hovered()` / `primary_pressed()` lifts. **Measured:** a fixed one-sixth mix toward `text` gave only 2.8 L\* (rest to hover) and 3.0 L\* (hover to press) in the dark preset, under `STATE_STEP` (4), so the lift is a perceptual step: `model::lift_toward(accent, text, PRIMARY_LIFT_L = 6)` and twice that. `problems()` adds `accent_text on primary hovered/pressed` and two `Indistinguishable` pairs; all four presets pass (and `high_contrast_problems()` for high contrast). `tests/primary_button.rs` (6, mutation-checked: the ink). Assumed decision: a marker on `Role::Button`, not a new role.
- **Card grid (#41).** `card_grid(cards)`, `Card::new(name).image(h).line(t).disabled()`, `CardCursor` (roving tab stop), `CardSelection` (lit with `Selected`), `CardRef` per card (a Bevy `Button`, so `ContextMenu` goes on it). Roles `CardGrid`, `CardCell` (**`Role::Card` already is the state-role fill**), `CardImage`, `CardName`, `CardLine`. A card rests on `solid_surface()` so its `text_muted` lines meet 4.5:1 (the pair `problems()` already holds). `tests/card.rs` (6, mutation-checked: the roving `TabIndex`). Assumed decision: spawn once, respawn to change.
- **Vertical tabs (#42).** `side_tabs(tabs, selected)` + `SideTab::badge` + `side_tab_page(strip, index, widgets)` (`TabPageOf`). Reuses `tabs_on_select`, `update_tabs`, `landing`; `VerticalTab` is out of `KeepsSideways` and `navigate_by_input` zeroes `net_y` for it. `tests/side_tabs.rs` (5, mutation-checked: without the `net_y` guard Down moved two tabs; without `Without<VerticalTab>` Right stayed on the strip). Assumed decision: pages are separate nodes naming the strip.
- **Demo.** `examples/cards.rs` (a map browser: a primary button beside a plain one over a card grid). **No still or GIF:** the capture ran on big_mac's M1 GPU and saved a 2560x1440 PNG that was entirely black: this host has no GUI (Aqua) session, so the window is never composited. A capture on a host with a display is the open item; nothing in `docs/media` was changed, so no caption is stale.
- **Disk.** The first test runs omitted `--lib --tests`, so nextest linked all 29 examples (32 GB) into the shared target and the disk reached 190 MB free; those example binaries (this session's, identified by their `.d` files) were deleted. Always pass `--lib --tests`.
- **Host note.** `prompts::a_keycaps_character_is_centred_in_its_box_whichever_cap_it_wears` fails on this Mac: on `Platform::Mac` a chord reads "⌃⇧Z", glyphs the test font does not hold (it measured -1.5 px off centre). It passes on big (Linux, "Ctrl+Shift+Z"). It is #45's domain (the agent on big is on it); no file of this pass touches prompts or shortcuts.

## Iteration 64 — 2026-11-06 — `ChordGlyphs`: a host spells a mac's chords in words (#45) (hand-driven, owner request 2026-11-06, host big)

**Triage.** #45 is a filed `bug` and `consumer:bevy_grimoire`; the owner asked for it by name. An earlier session left a never-built draft in `~/bevy_gooey_loop` (the research note as `df8e16f` and a 14-file diff); this pass adopted both, built them for the first time and fixed what the build and the tests found.

**Root cause, measured** (research note, `docs/research/chord-glyphs.md`). `model::shortcut_label` spells a mac chord with `⌃⌥⇧⌘` and `↩⌫⌦⎋⇥`. Bevy 0.20's bundled `FiraMono-subset.ttf` maps 95 codepoints and none of the thirteen; the host's `FiraMono-Regular.ttf` (1,350 codepoints) lacks seven. The crate had no way to ask for a spelling the face can draw.

**What changed.** `model::ChordGlyphs { Glyphs (default), Words }`; `model::shortcut_label(code, mods, mac, glyphs)`; `Shortcut::label_with(platform, glyphs)`; `BindingSettings::{chord_glyphs, platform, chord(), input_label()}`; `ChordText(Shortcut)` + `update_chord_texts`. Every chord the crate draws now reads `BindingSettings::chord`: a tooltip's keycap, a menu row, a prompt, a shortcut badge, the legend and the palette's hint. `Shortcut::label()` and `label_on` keep Apple's symbols.

**Fixed in review of the draft.** (1) The menu row and the tooltip keycap were spawned with `shortcut.label()` and only corrected a frame later by `update_chord_texts`; they now spawn in the host's spelling. (2) The badge carried a `ChordText` it re-inserted every frame; the badge system owns its text, so the component is gone from badges. (3) Four draft tests used `Shortcut::primary`, which is Ctrl on the Linux host and failed there; they use `Modifiers::META`. (4) A draft assertion that `Enter` is the same in both spellings on a mac was false (`↩` against `Enter`) and is replaced by a plain-letter one. (5) The draft's rustfmt drift in `src/lib.rs` and `tests/shortcut.rs`. (6) The draft's `AGENTS.md` and `host-api.md` were not updated; the CHANGELOG now says the `shortcut_label` signature change is breaking.

**Tests.** `shortcut::a_macs_chord_in_words_is_ascii_for_every_key_and_modifier` (42 keys × 16 modifier sets), `the_symbols_are_still_apples_and_the_words_are_ascii` (`⌃⇧P` against `Ctrl+Shift+P`, `Ctrl+Opt+Shift+Cmd+X`, `Opt+Enter`, `Ctrl+Esc`), `the_spelling_only_changes_a_macs_chord`, `the_settings_spell_a_chord_and_an_input_in_the_hosts_way`, `a_tooltips_keycap_changes_with_the_spelling_without_being_respawned`, `a_menu_rows_chord_reads_the_hosts_spelling`; `badges::the_overlay_spells_its_chords_the_hosts_way_and_follows_a_change`; `palette::a_rows_chord_hint_follows_the_hosts_spelling_while_the_palette_is_open`. **Failing first:** with `shortcut_label` forced to ignore `Words` (a temporary edit in `src/model.rs`, restored), `shortcut::` showed 5 of 25 failing (the ASCII test, the symbols-and-words test, the settings test, the menu row and the tooltip keycap) and 20 passing; the badge and palette tests were added afterwards and pass.

**Failing first, re-run on the whole set (restart of this pass, 2026-11-06 14:55).** The same mutation (both `match glyphs` in `shortcut_label` replaced by `match ChordGlyphs::Glyphs`), run on `test(shortcut::) | test(badges::) | test(palette::)`: 63 run, 7 failed (`a_macs_chord_in_words_is_ascii_for_every_key_and_modifier`, `the_symbols_are_still_apples_and_the_words_are_ascii`, `the_settings_spell_a_chord_and_an_input_in_the_hosts_way`, `a_menu_rows_chord_reads_the_hosts_spelling`, `a_tooltips_keycap_changes_with_the_spelling_without_being_respawned`, `badges::the_overlay_spells_its_chords_the_hosts_way_and_follows_a_change`, `palette::a_rows_chord_hint_follows_the_hosts_spelling_while_the_palette_is_open`); `the_spelling_only_changes_a_macs_chord` passes, as a control on the Linux spelling must. Mutation reverted (`src/` diff empty). The previous run of this pass was OOM-killed by systemd-oomd in its gate (big at 30 of 33 GB, swap full); this one builds with `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4` and `--test-threads 4`.

**Gate (iteration 64).** Run once, on the tree merged with big_mac's v0.36.0 (#43, #40, #41, #42): see the release line.

**Demo.** `examples/shortcuts` gained `GOOEY_PLATFORM=mac` and `GOOEY_GLYPHS=words` for a capture; see the release line.

1. The `HoldSettings` option row ("Hold to confirm: Normal, Short, Tap") next to the text-size and theme rows.
2. A "ready" cue for `Cooldown` (a one-frame flash under `MotionSettings`), and a charges variant.
3. One keycap vocabulary for the shortcut badges and legend (the legend and a badge draw the same chord two ways today).

The orchestrator finished the gate on the merged tree: fmt, clippy, nextest 1013/1013 (`/tmp/g64gate`), `cargo test --all-features --doc` 233/233 after `00790d4` (the new `BindingSettings::chord` example lacked `bevy::prelude::*` for `KeyCode`), `cargo check --lib` and wasm32, rustdoc `-D warnings`. `cargo semver-checks`: 2 major checks failed (the new `shortcut_label` argument, the new `BindingSettings` fields), so `release: v0.37.0` (`ca2c782`), published (https://crates.io/crates/bevy_gooey/0.37.0), tag `v0.37.0` pushed. #45 closed by `76485a5`.

## Iteration 65 — 2026-11-07 — Springs by duration and bounce (#47), `MotionTrace` (#46), retargetable `MotionTarget` tracks (#48)

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 90): nothing missing. No `bug` issue open (#44, #45 closed). Parked branches: `wiggum/parked-pass-90-…` holds the loop's own unbuilt take on `ChordGlyphs` (#45), which a hand-driven pass finished and released as v0.37.0 while this clone was stopped (local `main` was 18 commits behind `origin/main`; merged first, and the clone's own `Research: chord glyphs` commit was already on `origin`), so it is stale and ignored; `wiggum/parked-pass-23-…` stale as before. All 28 open issues are `grimoire-ui` `G-NN` requests. Ready ones (Depends on none or already met): #46, #47, #54, #55, #59, #62, #63, each named once on a bevy_grimoire Needs line, so lowest numbers first; #48 became ready when #46 and #47 closed in this pass and builds on both, so the cluster is **#47, #46, #48**. Skipped (and why): #54, #55 (elevation/seeds: another module), #59 (text grid), #62, #63 (input counting, type-select).

**Ranked candidates.** (1) #47+#46+#48 the motion foundation, which 12 later `G-NN` issues name on their Depends line. (2) #54/#55 skin from seeds and elevation (the Desk preset's inputs). (3) #62 double press counts. (4) #63 type-select. (5) #59 text on the pixel grid.

**Sources.** Craft: WCAG 2.2 SC 2.3.3 and *Good Game Feel* (Hicks et al., doi 10.26503/dl.v2018i1.936). Shipping: Apple WWDC23 *Animate with springs* (10158). Technical: Rune `rune-motion` (`spring.rs`, `layer.rs`, `props.rs`, `tokens.rs`) and `motion_gate`. Note `docs/research/motion-tracks.md`. home-still's MCP bridge answered 401; the CLI search worked.

**What changed.** (#47) `model::{Spring, SpringState, spring_step, spring_at_rest, spring_settled, spring_settle_seconds}`: SwiftUI's duration/bounce mapping in closed form for all three damping regimes; `GooeySkin::motion: MotionTokens` (snappy 0.25, default 0.3, calm 0.4, lead 0.2 s, fade 0.15 s) and `SkinProblem::BadMotion`. (#46) `testing::MotionTrace` (`watch`, `step`, `max_step_px`, `max_speed_change_px_per_s`, `settled_after_seconds`, `writes_at_rest`, `still_on_first_frame`, `TraceEnded`), write detection by change ticks. (#48) `MotionTarget { translation, scale, rotation, spring, enter_from }` and `InstantMotion`: a spring track on `UiTransform` that retargets from the drawn value *and velocity*. Clean cutover: `SlideIn`, `model::ease_out`, `DIALOG_SLIDE_SECONDS`, `POPOVER_SLIDE_SECONDS` and `METER_GAIN_TAU_SECONDS` are deleted; the dialog card (`enter_from` 12 px), `PopoverSlide` (6 px, a spring state instead of an age) and the meter's gain run on `motion.snappy`.

**Assumed decisions** (owner may override; recorded on the issues): (1) tracks, the popover slide and the meter rest by `spring_settled` (speed limit `tolerance × ω`), not Rune's absolute limit (`spring_at_rest`, kept as the issue names it): with the absolute rule a spring stretched ×2 measured 0.63 s against 1.17 s, three frames short of #48's "doubles ±1 frame". (2) #46 asks the Quit card to be "still on its first frame": an entrance starts on its first frame by design (it is a first target that does not land), so `still_on_first_frame()` means frame 0 == frame 1, and the card test asserts its start pose and settle time instead. (3) `MeterShown` gains `speed` and `aim`; `aim` is what tells a bouncy spring's overshoot (back to the same target) from a loss (a lower one).

**Tests** (1,041 in `all` + lib, 222 doctests; mutation-checked where stated). `model`: spring never passes its target and rests by `spring_settle_seconds`, bounce 0.3 overshoots once and −0.5 never does and trails bounce 0, a retarget keeps value and velocity, 60 and 120 Hz agree to 1e-4, a stretched spring settles in proportion, refusals (NaN, zero duration, bounce ±1), the meter's gain carries its speed, a loss during it drops at once, a bouncy gain overshoot is no loss. `tests/motion_trace.rs` (17): the card rises with no step larger than its slide and settles within its spring's time, writes nothing for 30 frames; a 100 px move reports a 100 px step (the check can fail); a spring has no speed snap and a stop-dead has one; despawn mid-trace ends the trace; same-value writes count as writes; reopening a card 0.05 s into a close has no step larger than an open's; **a track equals the model spring carried through four retargets to 0.05 px (mutation: zeroing velocity at a retarget fails it, and failed none of the weaker step tests first)**; a game's own transform write drops the track (mutation-checked: removing the check fails it); `InstantMotion`; scale and rotation lanes; `duration_scale` 2 doubles the settle within a frame; a NaN target is refused. `tests/motion.rs` and the meter, serde and skin tests were moved to what the player sees (spring settle times instead of 0.18 s).

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g65/before|after/motion/f_*.png`, `cmp_all.png`). The dialog card's top edge (ring row) per rendered frame at `GOOEY_MOTION=3`: before 206, 204, 202, 200, 199, 198, 198, 197; after 208, 207, 205, 204, 202, 201, 200, 200, 199, 198, 198, 198, 198, 197: the spring starts gently (no jump on the first frame) and has a longer, quieter tail; the card and its ring are drawn whole in both, nothing clips. At scale 1 the card is within one pixel at 0.25 s against 0.15 s before, and settles by about 0.45 s against 0.18 s. Flaws left: the entrance is slower to *finish* than before (the issue's palette asks for it); the meter's heal GIF reads the same as before. Verdict: GREAT (the visible change is small and clean; nothing at rest moved: the settled gallery is pixel-identical, 0 px differ in both skins).

**Checklist and mean_ms.** Gallery at 1280×720, 120 frames, `GOOEY_HEALTH=60`, before (v0.37.0, `6f6d14b`) 22.14, 22.82 (dark), 22.77, 23.04 (parchment); after 21.91, 21.84 (dark), 21.45, 21.64 (parchment): no regression (the settled-track system is a query that `continue`s before reading). Contrast, targets, spacing, skins' `problems()` (now also `BadMotion`-free in every preset) unchanged.

**Demo and docs.** `docs/media/motion.gif` and `meter.gif` regenerated (the shipped ones showed the old ease-out/exponential): 2.7 s 600×300 30 frames, and 6.95 s 466×64 139 frames, rows in `docs/media/README.md`; README caption, `docs/guide/motion.md` (the palette table, `MotionTarget`, testing), guide index, CHANGELOG `[Unreleased]`, AGENTS.md motion entry, research note with measured numbers.

## Iteration 66 — 2026-11-07 — Presence: a widget stays drawn while it goes (#49)

**Triage.** Last audit row (pass 91): nothing missing. No `bug` issue open. Parked branches:
`wiggum/parked-pass-90-…` is the stale ChordGlyphs draft (released as v0.37.0 by a hand-driven pass), ignored as before. 28 open
issues, all `grimoire-ui` `G-NN`; each is named on exactly one bevy_grimoire `Needs` line, so the tie broke on
lowest number. **Ready: #49 only.** Every other ready-looking request waits on it: #50 (reduced motion keeps fades),
#60 (hover curves), #64 (tooltip timing), #69 (split panes), #71 (popovers open from their anchor), #72 (toasts
enter from their edge) all list `#49` on their Depends line, and each of those is named on a grimoire slice that is
otherwise ready. So #49 unblocks 6 of the 22 open `G-NN` requests at once — more than #55 or #65, the two the
backlog ranks above it, each of which unblocks one.

**Ranked candidates.** (1) #49 presence — unblocks 6. (2) #65 gliding scrolls — unblocks 1, and `bevy_gooey#59`
(pixel-grid text) is the same weight. (3) #55 elevation as data — no dependants yet. (4) #61 press scale.

**Sources** (`docs/research/presence.md`): craft — Iacovides et al., *Removing the HUD*
(doi:10.1145/2793107.2793120: "The HUD may initially provide important information to players, but can later act
as a barrier to deeper levels of involvement") and WCAG 2.2 SC 2.3.3; shipping/technical — Rune `f73f72c`
(`rune-motion/src/scene/presence.rs`: "The app removed a keyed node. It stays drawn, plays its removal effect on the
exit spring, and is reported in `exited` only once its presence has settled at 0 (opacity 0)"; `transition.rs`
`Transition::identity()` "presence still holds removal until its fade settles"; `tokens.rs` exits 10–25 % shorter
than entrances; `README.md` "Under reduced motion … opacity, colour and presence take a 0.18 s ease-in-out fade");
Hicks et al., *Good Game Feel* (doi:10.26503/dl.v2018i1.936). home-still's MCP bridge answered 401; the CLI search
worked.

**What changed.** `Opacity(f32)` (public, 0..=1, held to the range, NaN reads as 1) multiplies every colour
`apply_skin` writes on a `Role` node at or below it — fills, borders (`faded_edge`), text, an icon's
`ImageNode::color`, a text caret's colour, selection and selected ink, and a floating box's `BoxShadow` — by the
product of the `Opacity` at and above each node; a node with no `Role` is the host's and untouched. `Leave` starts an
exit: the subtree takes `Pickable::IGNORE`, loses its `TabIndex`, a `Dialog` and its modal `TabGroup` come off (so
`return_focus` hands focus back in that frame, as the despawn it replaces did), anything else focused inside lets
focus go, and the node fades on `Spring::new(motion.fade_seconds, 0.0)` scaled by `MotionSettings::duration_scale`,
despawning at `PRESENCE_GONE` (1/255). `Stay` reverses from the drawn value with its velocity, restores all of it
and takes itself off with the exit; the `Leave` is spent when the exit starts, so a second one changes nothing.
`Leaving` exposes `opacity()` and `returning()`. Cutover: `confirm`, `popover` (`close`), the palette (three close
paths), and with them the menu, select list, context menu and colour picker (all popovers).

**Assumed decision** (owner may override; on #49 with the label): `Leave` and `Stay` are components a game inserts,
not `EntityCommand`s a game queues — this crate's state is components (`Opacity`, `MotionTarget`, `InstantMotion`), a
component is what a test can assert on, and `insert(Leave)` needs no deferred indirection.

**Tests** (1,073 in `all` + lib, 241 doctests). `tests/presence.rs`, 9, mutation-checked (an `instant = true` in
`begin_leaves` fails 5 of the 9): a dialog's card is drawn on every frame of its exit with its opacity strictly
partway and falling, and is gone within `motion.default` plus a frame; a press during the exit hits the screen's
button under the veil, never the exit; `Stay` three frames in comes all the way back with no step larger than an
uninterrupted exit's largest; `Opacity(0.5)` halves a panel's own fill (skin alpha × 0.5), its button's fill and its
button's words, and leaves a host's own `Text` at Bevy's default ink; motion off despawns a `Leave` in its own
frame; a second `Leave` is one exit and a plain `despawn()` is still immediate; 30 idle frames write nothing; a
despawned parent takes a leaving child at once; a focus inside an exit is let go.

**Fixed in review of my own work.** (1) `spread_opacity` first wrote a `PresenceAlpha` on every node unconditionally,
which is a write per frame for the whole tree; it now writes only when the factor differs, and a whole subtree at 1
carries no component at all. (2) A returned exit kept the `Opacity` the fade had written, so the node came back at
0.995 rather than whole; the fade now removes it when the node had none. (3) Four test suites (`confirm`, `popover`,
`palette`, `context_menu`) read "gone" one frame after the close and needed the exit's frames: each got a real
`TimeUpdateStrategy` clock and a longer `settle`, which is what a headless window without one lacked. (4) `select`
and `color` needed the same. (5) clippy: `type_complexity` on two queries and `too_many_arguments` on two systems,
fixed with a `Touched`/`Already` alias and a `World`/`Tree` argument bundle.

**Disk incident (recorded, not a code fault).** A `cargo nextest run` without `--lib --tests` linked all 29
examples again — the trap `AGENTS.md` names — and filled the host to 926 MB free; 4,736 example binaries from that
run (19 GB), then the regenerable `debug/incremental` (5.5 GB) and this crate's stale duplicate `all-*` fragments
(3.8 GB minus the two newest) were removed. That is why two full-suite runs reported dozens of 0.005 s failures:
they were the linker failing, not the tests. The pass's own scratch was 162 MB under `/tmp/g92`.

**Critique and verdict** (`/tmp/g92/before|after`, `/tmp/g92/gif`, `/tmp/g92/{egrid,vgrid,zoomcmp}.png`). The exit is
the point: at `GOOEY_MOTION=3` the card's fill mean goes 34 → 44 → 56 over six frames while the veil goes 19 → 24 →
34, both on one curve and both arriving where the screen already stands, and the four frame strips show the card
and the veil thinning together with the settings panel's own colour returning underneath and nothing of the dialog
left — which is what Rune's `presence` aims at and what the issue's first acceptance asks for. Flaws found and
answered: my first GIF cut at frames 40–96 caught only the *entrance* and the whole open dialog (the Escape lands
after), so the capture was re-cut to frames 29–68, which is entrance → open → exit; the first three encodes showed
green frames because `paletteuse` was run without `stats_mode=diff` on a *cropped* set, so the final one is encoded
from the cropped frames directly (720×416, 40 frames, 2.67 s). The still captures outside an exit differ by 505
pixels of 921,600, all inside `392x12+207+375` — the Health meter's drain, on the game clock. Verdict: **GREAT**
for the exit; the entrance GIF (`motion.gif`) is unchanged and still true.

**Checklist** (`/tmp/g92/findings.md`): contrast 8.9:1 body on the panel, ring 8.0:1 off the button and 3 px, targets
44 px, gap 10 / padding 14, `problems()` empty in every preset, the scrim 2.7:1 in parchment, the meter at 70 % for
70 — all unchanged, because nothing in `apply_skin`'s styling arms moved except the colour it writes being
multiplied by the node's opacity. The new lines: the exit is 6 frames on a 60 Hz clock (0.1 s) and every frame of it
is partway; a settled interface writes 0 in 30 frames open or after a return; the screen under an exiting dialog
takes the pointer in the frame the exit starts.

**mean_ms** (gallery, 1280×720, 120 frames): before 16.635, 16.665; after 16.667, 16.673 — no regression (the fade
system returns on `exiting.is_empty()`, and a fade writes one `Opacity` and one `PresenceAlpha` per frame).

**Demo and docs.** `docs/media/presence.gif` (720×416, 40 frames, 2.67 s, 1.5 MB), row in `docs/media/README.md`;
guide `docs/guide/presence.md` and its index line; README's feature-list line; CHANGELOG `[0.39.0]`; AGENTS.md module entry.

## Iteration 67 — 2026-11-07 — #75: the keycap centring test measured the wrong middle (test-only)

`tests/prompts.rs::cap_off_centre` took the midpoint of the first and last glyph positions as the run's middle. A `PositionedGlyph::position` is its quad's centre, so that midpoint is `content.min + size/2 + (w_first - w_last)/2`, which no layout can change: the chord `⌃⇧Z` (6 px `⌃`, 10 px `Z`) read 1.5 px off while dead-centre. It now compares the run's shaped width (`TextLayoutInfo::size.x`) centred in the content box against the node's border-box centre, so a left-pinned run still fails. No `src/` change: the keycap was centred. Sources: Bevy 0.20 `TextLayoutInfo`/`PositionedGlyph` (technical); craft and shipping-game sources not applicable to a test-measurement fix.

Gate on the merged tree: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, `cargo nextest run --all-features --lib --tests` 1133/1133 (was 1 failing), 250 doctests, `cargo check --lib` + wasm32, `cargo doc -D warnings`. No package file changed by this pass, so no release of its own; `v0.40.0` (d6fa6dd, released by the grimoire loop) already covers the G-43..G-54 commits. `mean_ms`: not measured, no visual change.

Disk: `/` was 100% full (504 MB free) and linking examples failed; nextest's bare `--all-features` builds every example, so use `--lib --tests` (as AGENTS.md says). Removed the ten >50 MB example binaries this session wrote into the shared target's `debug/examples` (today's mtime only): 504 MB -> 3.5 GB free. The shared target (`~/.cargo-target-shared/global`, tens of GB) and the stale `wiggum/parked-pass-91-by-hand`, `wiggum/pass-91-research-note`, `loop/iter-66` branches are left, listed here.
